- Tests: new `tests/Feature/ClinicalAlertNotificationTest.php` - 21 tests covering the HIGH transition (dispatch on non-HIGH -> HIGH to admin+staff, no duplicate on HIGH -> HIGH, no duplicate when the same visit is dispatched twice, `event_key` payload shape, downgrade never dispatches, recalculation path fires once for a multi-visit patient), the EDD window (in-window + ONGOING + no confirmed outcome dispatches; outside window, past window handled by the other alert, delivered/confirmed-outcome patients skipped), idempotency across repeated command runs, the archived-user exclusion, and the scheduled signature (`schedule:list` shows `notifications:check-clinical-alerts` daily at 07:00). `tests/Feature/NotificationTest.php`: the two referral tests rewritten to assert the staff recipient as well (13 tests total). `tests/Unit/Services/PatientAssessmentRecalculationServiceTest.php`: 9 constructions updated for the new constructor argument.
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent before and after every run; `php -l` clean on every touched PHP file. Focused suites all green - `ClinicalAlertNotificationTest` 21/21, `NotificationTest` 13/13, `PatientAssessmentRecalculationServiceTest` 9/9 (43 tests, 0 failures extracted from the JUnit log). Full-suite regression gate against a true pre-change baseline rather than the (stale) documented list: a disposable copy of the working tree at the pre-notification state was built at `C:\Users\admin\AppData\Local\Temp\opencode\baseline-maternity` (real `vendor` copy, path-bound `bootstrap/cache` files cleared) and both suites were run with `--log-junit`, then diffed test-by-test - baseline 122 failed / 1001 passed (1123 tests), current 121 failed / 1023 passed (1144 tests) = 0 new failures, 21 new tests passing, and 1 baseline failure (`GestationalAgeAutofillTest` date-dependent case) now passing. The 121 remaining failures are all pre-existing and unrelated to notifications (documented baselines plus older `ExplainabilitySprint7Test`, `PatientProfileRiskPanelTest`, `PatientViewControllerTest` etc.).
- Defense notes: recipients come from one private method so a future alert cannot silently revert to admin-only; the clinical effective risk is derived from persisted fields with read-only helpers instead of any recomputation, so alerting can never alter assessment state; dedupe is belt-and-braces (state transition + payload `event_key`) because database notifications have no unique constraint; the command is idempotent and safe to re-run (it only ever inserts a notification) and writes no patient/outcome data. Deployment note: the repository contains no `Procfile`, `nixpacks.toml` or `railway.json`, so Railway will start the web service only - in production `php artisan schedule:run` must be executed every minute (Railway cron service or a `worker` service running `php artisan schedule:work`), otherwise the 07:00 EDD alerts never fire.

## Local PSGC Address Autocomplete - Phase 1: Official Dataset, Conversion Command and Search Index (2026-10-08)

- Scope: dataset + conversion + search service + tests ONLY, exactly as approved. New files: `app/Console/Commands/PsgcConvert.php` (signature `psgc:convert {--datafile=} {--summary=} {--out=}`), `app/Services/PsgcIndex.php`, `resources/data/psgc/psgc.json` + `manifest.json`, the two user-downloaded official source workbooks under `resources/data/psgc/source/` (PSGC-2Q-2026 Publication Datafile 3,250,889 bytes and National and Provincial Summary 87,973 bytes, kept byte-untouched), and `tests/Unit/Services/PsgcDatasetTest.php` + `tests/Unit/Services/PsgcIndexTest.php`. NO autocomplete UI, NO Add/Edit Patient integration, NO HTTP endpoint or route, NO migration, NO database or patient-data change, NO `.env`/config/cache change, NO new Composer dependency.
- Dataset: `php artisan psgc:convert` parses the Publication Datafile `PSGC` sheet (43,768 rows) into `{"nodes":[{code,name,level,parent}]}` sorted ascending by code, compact JSON (UTF-8 unescaped, trailing newline), byte-deterministic for the same sources - a second run reproduced SHA-256 `66fcd40fe10249f57efe604c09f77c56173ea7d1fc80b446b5bcca914b31a2b3` exactly, runtime ~1.6 s, output 3,368,430 bytes. Pinned official counts: 18 Reg / 82 Prov / 149 City / 1,493 Mun / 14 SubMun / 42,010 Bgy + 2 structural container rows (`City of Isabela (Not a Province)` 0990100000, `Special Geographic Area` 1999900000); no old names/city class/income/urban/population/status columns are carried (2,855 names whitespace-trimmed only).
- Hierarchy: the source has NO parent column, so parents are derived from the official PSGC coding structure (RR-PPP-MM-BBB: Bgy = first7+`000`, Prov = first2+`00000000`, City/Mun/SubMun/container = first5+`000000` when that container exists else the region - covering HUCs like Caloocan/Cebu, Pateros, NCR, Isabela City and the SGA). Conversion then fails WITHOUT writing outputs unless: every parent exists, no self-parents, a level-compatibility matrix holds (Bgy->[City|Mun|SubMun], SubMun->[City], City/Mun->[Prov|Reg|container], etc.), no cycles (depth guard), and BOTH official summary sheets agree - per-province barangay/city counts, every independent city's region linkage (the former open question: City of Cotabato is a component city under Maguindanao del Norte, consistent in both sources), every province/region present, plus the national totals 82/149/1,493/42,010 and the National Summary per-region counts. All checks passed.
- Manifest: `manifest.json` records schemaVersion 1.0.0, PSA origin + source URL, publication date parsed from the workbook Metadata sheet (`30 June 2026` / `2026-06-30`), both source file bytes/SHA-256/core.xml modification stamps (2026-07-13, matching the PSA release), dataset SHA-256/bytes/recordCount, level counts, hierarchy derivation + cross-validation statements, validation results, transformation log and schema documentation - regenerated together with the dataset (only `generatedAt` varies per run).
- `PsgcIndex`: constructor takes an optional path (defaults to `resource_path('data/psgc/psgc.json')`); `search($q, $limit = 12)` returns `[{code,name,level,parent,label}]`, `findByCode()` (10-digit-validated, null otherwise), `hierarchy()` (root->leaf chain), `label()` (leaf->root address string), plus public `MIN_QUERY_LENGTH = 2`, `MAX_LIMIT = 50` (clamped), `normalize()`, `regionLabel()`, `flush()`. Matching normalizes trim -> collapse whitespace -> `mb_strtolower` -> explicit accent map (Parañaque/paranaque both sides; no `ext-intl` on this PHP build, so the map is deterministic across platforms), rejects invalid UTF-8 with `[]`; ranking is exact > prefix > substring, then name length, name, PSGC code - fully deterministic. Labels use official names with the region reduction rule (`Region III (Central Luzon)` -> `Central Luzon`, non-conforming names like `National Capital Region (NCR)` and `Bangsamoro ... (BARMM)` kept verbatim), so the specification example resolves to `Muzon South, City of San Jose Del Monte, Bulacan, Central Luzon` (the patient record's Muzon South barangay is a real node, code 0301420061). Decoded dataset is memoized statically per `realpath` (~90 ms first load, ~6 ms/search, ~62 MB peak in plain PHP) - no DB, no HTTP, no config dependency; Phase 1 adds no persistent caching.
- Tests: 36 new tests / 394,070 assertions, all green - `PsgcDatasetTest` (12: node count, pinned level counts, unique/sorted 10-digit codes, four-field schema + trimmed UTF-8 names, parent existence + level matrix + depth <=4 for all 43,768 nodes, 12 hierarchy fixtures incl. NCR/HUC/container/Isabela/SGA/Cotabato, NCR 16 cities + Pateros, 14 Manila SubMun, 607 `Poblacion` + 5 exact `Muzon` duplicates preserved, manifest byte-for-byte + pinned SHA-256, source workbook presence/size/ZIP magic), `PsgcIndexTest` (24: spec label example, NCR/HUC/container labels, null labels, 7 regionLabel cases, normalize cases, findByCode valid/invalid, suggestion shape without `norm` leak, hierarchy root->leaf, empty/short/invalid queries, MIN length, pinned 12-row deterministic `muzon` ordering with exact-before-prefix tiers, case/accent/whitespace equivalence, substring tier ordering, duplicate-name tie-break, limit handling incl. MAX_LIMIT clamp, flush/reload).
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent before/after; `php -l` clean on both new PHP files; no `php artisan optimize`/`config:cache`/`optimize:clear` executed; no package install; no DB command. Regression gate: full `tests/Unit` directory 350 passed / 1 failed where the single failure is the pre-existing `AssessmentContextTest` float-vs-int case (fails identically in isolation; `app/ValueObjects/AssessmentContext.php` last changed 2026-10-02, untouched by this phase); prior-sprint patient validation suites 33/33 (`PatientPreviousCsValidationTest` + `PatientGravidaMiscarriageValidationTest`); `ExplainabilitySprint7Test` failures reproduce in isolation and are part of the documented ~121 pre-existing HEAD failures (see the Notification sprint entry above); one full-suite attempt timed out on the environment's known network-dependent hangs (same caveat documented at the referral-architecture entry). Zero new failures attributable to this phase - Phase 1 only ADDS files, loads no existing code paths, and runs no database.
- Defense notes: conversion validates before writing (a mismatch aborts with a failure exit and leaves prior outputs untouched) so a corrupted regeneration can never silently replace a good dataset; the dataset is pinned by SHA-256 tests, so re-running `psgc:convert` from tampered sources fails the suite rather than shipping altered geography; source workbooks are read-only inputs and their sizes/magic bytes are pinned; search is a pure read-only in-memory index with an immutable committed JSON file, so address search cannot affect (or be affected by) MySQL availability and Phase 1 exposes no network surface; ranking is explicitly ordered down to the code tie-break so identical queries always return identical lists for fair UX and stable tests; `MAX_LIMIT` bounds materialized rows; the static memo keeps one decode per request while `flush()` gives tests isolation; labels are derived, never stored, so display fixes cannot corrupt data. Out of scope and intentionally NOT built pending Phase 2/3 approval: HTTP endpoint + route, autocomplete UI, Add/Edit Patient wiring, `psgc_code` validation/persistence, migration, server-side persistent caching.

## Local PSGC Address Autocomplete - Phase 2: Read-Only Location Search Endpoint (2026-10-08)

- Scope: `GET /locations/search?q=...` only. New files: `app/Http/Controllers/LocationSearchController.php` and `tests/Feature/PsgcLocationSearchTest.php`; modified: `routes/web.php` (one import + one route inside the existing group). NO patient UI, NO `PatientController`/`Patient` model change (the working-tree `PatientController` diff is the unchanged pre-existing 33-line obstetric-validation baseline), NO migration, NO dataset regeneration, NO `.env`/config/DB change, NO new package, NO route removal or middleware change.
- Route/middleware: registered inside the existing `Route::middleware(['auth', 'no-store'])->group(...)` in `routes/web.php` as `Route::get('/locations/search', [LocationSearchController::class, 'search'])->name('locations.search')` - this group IS the project's Admin & Staff convention (roles are only `admin`/`staff`; `users.role` defaults to `staff`, so plain `auth` covers both and `staff`/`AdminMiddleware` would wrongly exclude one side). Guests get the standard login redirect (browser) or 401 JSON (expectsJson) with zero location data. Route middleware resolved as `['web','auth','no-store']`.
- Controller behavior: delegates verbatim to `PsgcIndex::search($q, 12)` (Phase 1 field names `code/name/level/parent/label`, official PSGC level codes passed through unchanged instead of re-mapping the suggested `barangay` word, codes stay strings); missing/empty/whitespace/1-char/unknown/emoji queries -> `200 {"results":[]}`; non-string `q` (array injection) -> `422 {"message":"The search query must be text."}`; `mb_strlen` > 100 -> `422 {"message":"The search query is too long."}`; a `RuntimeException` from an unusable dataset is masked as generic `503 {"message":"Location search is temporarily unavailable."}` so no filesystem path or stack trace can appear. Every JSON response carries `Cache-Control: no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate, private` - the same directive as `SetNoStoreHeaders`, which by design skips non-HTML responses, so the header is applied explicitly in the controller. No throttle added: the project uses throttling only on auth flows, and a rate limit on a keystroke-driven search would be a breaking change for Phase 3 (input length caps bound the work instead).
- Tests: new `tests/Feature/PsgcLocationSearchTest.php` - 16 tests / 81 assertions, all green: admin 200, staff 200, guest redirect+401 with no data leak, exact record (`0301420061` Muzon South + pinned label), missing/empty/whitespace -> `{"results":[]}`, 1-char -> empty, unknown -> empty, case-insensitive equality, Unicode (`Parañaque` -> `1381000000`, ASCII-folded query equal, emoji -> empty), exactly 12 rows for `san`, 101-char query -> 422 with generic message + no `psgc`/`resources`/`Exception` in body + no-store, array `q[]` -> 422, JSON structure (only `results` key, rows exactly `code/name/level/parent`, Content-Type + no-store headers), codes emitted as JSON strings (leading-zero `0301420061` and fully numeric `1381000000` verified as strings in the raw body), route registered GET|HEAD with `web/auth/no-store` middleware, and a patient snapshot proving zero patient-table changes across six mixed queries.
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent before/after; `php -l` clean on controller, routes and test file; test environment confirmed SQLite `:memory:` (phpunit.xml `force="true"` on `DB_CONNECTION`/`DB_DATABASE`, no config cache) - no run ever touched `maternity_system1` or Railway MySQL. Focused suites: Phase 1+2 combined 52/52 (394,151 assertions). Regression gate vs the Phase 1 baseline: full `tests/Unit` directory 350 passed / 1 failed - the same pre-existing `AssessmentContextTest` float-vs-int case (unchanged from Phase 1); route-heavy Feature subsets `PatientStructuredAddressTest` + `NotificationTest` + `ReferralLifecycleOneRowHistoryTest` 52/52; prior-sprint validation 33/33. Zero new failures.
- Defense notes: access control is inherited from the existing group rather than a new middleware combination, so authorization behavior cannot drift from the rest of the app; the endpoint is read-only by construction (no Eloquent model is loaded - `patients` snapshot test proves it) and performs no SQL (in-memory index only); response rows are the Phase 1 service rows verbatim so the endpoint cannot invent or mangle PSGC data; all error bodies are fixed generic strings under the controller's control, and the one realistic failure mode (missing/corrupt dataset file) is caught and masked before Laravel's exception handler could render its path; query length/type rejection happens before the index is touched; no-store prevents shared-cache retention of autocomplete traffic. NOT built, pending Phase 3 approval: autocomplete UI, Add/Edit Patient integration, `psgc_code` validation/persistence, migration.

## Local PSGC Address Autocomplete - Phase 3: Add/Edit Patient Autocomplete UI (2026-10-08)

- Scope: approved `city` response field + shared autocomplete Blade partial wired into Add/Edit Patient + UI tests. New files: `resources/views/patients/partials/address-autocomplete.blade.php`, `tests/Feature/PatientAddressAutocompleteUiTest.php`. Modified: `LocationSearchController` (derives `city`), `PsgcLocationSearchTest` (exact-row/structure expectations + 4 new city tests), `patients/create.blade.php` and `patients/edit.blade.php` (additive `id`/`for` on the two address inputs - verified no pre-existing id conflicts - plus one `@include`). NO migration, NO `psgc_code` field/column, NO `PatientController`/`Patient` model change, NO `PsgcIndex` change, NO label parsing, NO `.env`/config/DB change, NO package install, NO Vite build change, NO route change.
- Approved `city` field: the Phase 2 response exposed only `parent` (a code), and the endpoint has no by-code lookup, so the client could not learn a city NAME without splitting `label` (explicitly forbidden) - approved via gate question, implemented in `LocationSearchController::withCity()` using the same `PsgcIndex::hierarchy()` chain that builds labels (single source of truth): nearest ancestor-or-self with level `City`/`Mun`; the 14 Manila `SubMun` districts resolve upward to their city (`Tondo I/II` -> `City of Manila`); `Reg`/`Prov`/container rows -> `null`. Row shape is now `code/name/level/parent/city/label`. Fixtures verified against the dataset: Muzon South -> `City of San Jose Del Monte`, City of Cebu / Pateros / City of Caloocan -> own name, Bulacan (Prov) -> null, Region I -> null, `Caloocan` barangay in Koronadal resolves `City of Koronadal`. Keeping the logic in the controller leaves the Phase 1 service contract and its tests untouched.
- UI: one shared partial (`@include('patients.partials.address-autocomplete')` immediately before `</x-app-layout>` on both blades) so Add and Edit cannot drift - inline `<style>`/`<script>` matching the pages' existing pattern (scoped `.psgc-ac-*` classes, DEPLA green `#55B85A` family, no Vite rebuild, no `node_modules` dependency). Inputs are addressed by `[name=...]` and wrapped at runtime into a relative container holding an absolutely-positioned panel, so form structure, input order and submission are untouched. Behavior: 350 ms debounce, min 2 / max 100 trimmed characters client gate (server stays the authority), AbortController + per-field sequence token (a stale response can never overwrite a newer keystroke), loading / `No locations found.` / `Location search is temporarily unavailable.` notes, ARIA combobox+listbox (`aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `aria-activedescendant`, `aria-live` note, `role=option`, `aria-selected`), ArrowUp/ArrowDown/Enter/Escape/Tab handling with `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})`, mousedown-preventDefault on options so focus never leaves the input, click-outside close, level filtering (`Bgy` for barangay; `City`/`Mun` for the city field), DOM built exclusively with `textContent` (no `innerHTML`). Endpoint injected via `@json(route('locations.search'))` so no hostname is hardcoded.
- City autofill and clearing rules: selecting a barangay suggestion sets the city input from the approved `row.city` field only (never label parsing) and dispatches `blur` so the pages' own validation clears any stale required-error; the autofill claim (`barangayAutoFilled` + `autoFilledCity`) is set ONLY by script selection - any manual `input` on the barangay drops the claim - and `reconcileBarangayWithCity()` (run on city suggestion select and on the city `change` event) clears the barangay ONLY when the claim exists and the committed city differs. User-typed barangay values are therefore never cleared, and `address_line` is never referenced. All PSGC state stays client-side: no hidden `psgc_code` input, no new column - server-side `psgc_code` validation/persistence remains deliberately deferred to Phase 4 (not started).
- Tests: `PsgcLocationSearchTest` 16 -> 20 tests (the two exact-row/structure assertions updated for the approved `city` key; four new tests: barangay parent-city resolution without label parsing, SubMun -> city, City/Mun self-resolution incl. NCR `1380100000`, null city for Prov/Reg) - 20/20 green. New `PatientAddressAutocompleteUiTest` 7 tests / 40 assertions, all green: partial rendered exactly once on Add (endpoint occurrence count 1 after unescaping `@json` slashes) with both ids/labels/names; rendered once on Edit with prefilled `Poblacion`/`Makati`; legacy patient still editable; `psgc_code` absent from both pages AND `Schema::hasColumn('patients','psgc_code')` false; `innerHTML` absent from both rendered pages; manual typed store persists all three fields byte-for-byte; required-3 validation unchanged.
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent before/after; tests on SQLite `:memory:` only (phpunit.xml `force="true"`); no `optimize`/`config:cache`/`optimize:clear`, no package install, no DB command, no git operations. Regression gates: full `tests/Unit` directory 350 passed / 1 failed - the same pre-existing `AssessmentContextTest` float-vs-int case (`20.0` vs `20`, last changed 2026-10-02, untouched); address+endpoint batch (`PatientStructuredAddressTest` + `PatientPreviousCsValidationTest` + `PatientGravidaMiscarriageValidationTest` + `PatientAddressAutocompleteUiTest` + `PsgcLocationSearchTest`) 77/77; `NotificationTest` + `ReferralLifecycleOneRowHistoryTest` 35/35. Zero new failures. HONEST LIMITATION: browser interaction behavior (debounce timing, keyboard flow, ARIA announcements, autofill UX) was verified by static code review only - no browser-automation package (puppeteer/playwright/jsdom) is installed and installing one is forbidden in this environment, so no script was executed in a real browser. One PowerShell caution: `create.blade.php` was rewritten with `Set-Content` once (include insertion); verified both HEAD and current copies are pure ASCII (zero non-ASCII characters), so the ANSI round-trip was byte-identical - subsequent edits used the UTF-8-safe edit tool only.
- Defense notes: the single shared partial is the only place autocomplete logic exists, so a fix lands on both forms simultaneously and one rendered-instance count test guards against double-inclusion double-binding (plus a `dataset.psgcAc` runtime idempotency guard); city derivation server-side from `hierarchy()` means labels and autofill can never disagree, and the client never needs to understand PSGC code structure; the conservative clear rule (claim-only, diff-only, unclaim-on-edit) means staff who type addresses manually observe zero behavior change; keeping all PSGC state client-side means Phase 4 server validation can be introduced behind an ordinary validation test without touching anything built here; fetch errors degrade to a visible message instead of silently leaving staff with a broken field, and input errors surface as generic fixed strings (no paths/stack traces); no rate limit was added to the endpoint (keystroke-driven, project convention is auth-only throttling) - the 100-char cap and the 12-row clamp bound the work instead. NOT built, pending approval: Phase 4 `psgc_code` server-side validation/persistence and migration.

## DEPLA Ultrasound: Fetal Weight and Heart Rate Display-Only Assessments (2026-10-08)

- Scope: display-only Fetal Weight Assessment (FWA) and Fetal Heart Rate (FHR) panels derived from EXISTING staff-entered ultrasound fields - no stored-value, ML, referral, notification, or prenatal-dropdown change; no migration, no DB column, no `.env`/config/cache change, no package install, no commit/push. New files: `app/ValueObjects/FetalAssessmentOutcome.php`, `app/Services/FetalWeightAssessmentService.php`, `app/Services/FetalHeartRateAssessmentService.php`, `resources/views/ultrasounds/partials/{fetal-weight-assessment,fetal-heart-rate-assessment,fetal-assessment-live}.blade.php`, `tests/Unit/Services/{FetalWeightAssessmentServiceTest,FetalHeartRateAssessmentServiceTest}.php`, `tests/Feature/UltrasoundFetalAssessmentsTest.php`. Modified: `app/Http/Controllers/UltrasoundController.php` (removed ONLY the two weight-vs-GA blocking branches - net diff is 43 deletions, 0 insertions; `required|numeric|min:200|max:5000` + custom messages intact at store rule :66 and update rule :259), `resources/views/ultrasounds/create.blade.php` and `edit.blade.php` (three `@include`s each; no input/control change - GA/weight/heartbeat inputs untouched), `resources/views/patients/show.blade.php` (read-only "Fetal Assessments (Display Only)" block in the expandable Ultrasound History section), `docs/MANUAL_TESTING_UAT.md` (US-003 rewritten + new US-003a).
- WHO Table 11 verification (FWA): data source is Kiserud et al., "Standardized fetal growth reference charts...", PLoS Med 14(1):e1002220 (2017), Table 11 (https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC5261648/), P10/P50/P90 grams for 14.0-40.0 completed weeks - all 14 anchor rows (14/16/18/20/22/24/26/28/30/32/34/36/38/40 wk; P10 100/190/300/430/580/760/960/1180/1410/1650/1890/2130/2360/2580; P50 140/250/390/560/760/1000/1270/1570/1900/2240/2570/2870/3130/3350; P90 200/330/500/720/970/1260/1590/1950/2330/2730/3120/3480/3800/4100), each pinned verbatim in `FetalWeightAssessmentService::TABLE` and asserted by the unit tests through `percentile()` (zero interpolation error at integer weeks). Worked example verified by hand: GA 32.7 wk -> P10 1755.4 g / P50 2042.4 g / P90 2349.4 g (linear interpolation between 32 wk and 34 wk rows), matching the service output. Classification uses full float precision (Below < P10, Within P10-P90 inclusive, Above > P90); only display strings round (`formatWeight`/`formatThreshold`), and a clarification line is emitted whenever rounding could make a raw comparison look inconsistent (exact-threshold tie shown raw, rounded-boundary ambiguity, `22.0` -> `22` rounding).
- FWA rules: `assess($weightG, $gaWeeks)` - GA outside [14.0, 40.0] inclusive, non-finite/absent weight or GA, or weight outside the accepted input range returns Unable to Assess with an explicit reason; weights are NEVER clamped or extrapolated beyond 14-40 wk. Recorded weight, GA, P10-P90 range, P50 median, Below/Within/Above chip (`FetalAssessmentOutcome::CHIP_CLASSES`: within=green, below/above/review=amber, unable=gray), recommendation (marked pending clinic HDP review), and a clickable WHO/PMC source link render in the panel.
- FHR rules: stored `fetal_heartbeat` dropdown values are unchanged (display interprets only). Interpretation window is 14.0 <= GA < 28.0 wk (`MIN_GA = 14.0`, `MAX_GA_EXCLUSIVE = 28.0`) and is explicitly labeled as a restricted display-only window whose 120-160 bpm normal range comes from ISUOG cardiac-screening / mid-trimester (18-24 wk) literature - NOT a universal per-gestation standard. Inside the window: Normal -> within; Tachycardia/Bradycardia/Weak/Absent -> review with category-specific safety recommendation. Outside the window (or GA missing) every category returns Unable to Assess rather than an unsupported "normal", with the limitation stated in the panel. Absent heartbeat always carries the prominent safety recommendation (prompt clinical assessment + confirmation by the appropriate examination) regardless of window, and the panel never states or implies fetal demise diagnosis. NICE NG229 intrapartum thresholds (rec 1.2.15) are cited as deliberately NOT applied (intrapartum-only context); the conflicting IOG/HSE 2025 antenatal 110-160 range is documented as not used. All recommendations marked pending clinic HCP review; sources clickable.
- UI: panels render below the corresponding input cells on Ultrasound Add (`create.blade.php`) and Edit (`edit.blade.php`), and read-only inside the expandable Ultrasound History list on `patients/show.blade.php`. `fetal-assessment-live.blade.php` ships a `<script data-fetal-assessment-live>` block embedding `clientConfig()` (both services) and mirrors the PHP classification in vanilla JS, rebinding on `input`/`change` for GA, EFW, heartbeat, scan date and patient selection (with a `setTimeout(0)` pass so the pages' own GA autofill runs first) - pure display; no form value, name, or validation attribute is written by it.
- Tests: 30 new tests, all green - `FetalWeightAssessmentServiceTest` (12): every WHO anchor row self-resolves, hand-computed 32.7 wk interpolation, fractional-GA interpolation, boundary 14.0/40.0 inclusive + 13.9/40.1 unable, previously-blocked weights (e.g. 350 g at 6 wk-style out-of-window and extreme small/large weights) accepted as recorded and assessed, raw-vs-rounded clarification cases, format helpers; `FetalHeartRateAssessmentServiceTest` (9): window bounds 14.0/28.0, all seven dropdown categories in/out of window, Absent safety prominence inside AND outside the window, missing GA, clientConfig shape; `UltrasoundFetalAssessmentsTest` (9): both panels render on Add and Edit with expected rows/chip/sources/pending note, edit prefill from stored values, history block renders assessments read-only, previously-blocked weights now store successfully via controller (store + update), stored values byte-identical after save, validation `min:200|max:5000` still rejects 199 g/5001 g, no new DB columns.
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` confirmed absent before tests (no `optimize`/`config:cache`/`optimize:clear` ever run); tests ran only on SQLite `:memory:` (phpunit.xml `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` guard active) - `maternity_system1`/MySQL never touched; `php -l` clean on all four PHP files; latest migration remains 2026-10-05 (none added); no git commit/push. Regression gates - new suites 30/30; `SchemaContractTest` + `ClinicalRuleEngineTest` + `RiskAssessmentServiceTest` + `UltrasoundSnapshotTest` all pass; `GestationalAgeAutofillTest` 6/10 with 4 failures IDENTICAL to a stashed HEAD baseline run (empirically verified: `git stash` -> same 4 failed/6 passed -> `git stash pop`); 10-file affected-area batch (`AssessmentLinkedReferralTest`, `Phase16EUiTest`, `Sprint15InteractionUiTest`, `PatientProfileRiskPanelTest`, `StaffAccessControlTest`, `ExplainabilitySprint7Test`, `MedicalHistoryScopeTest`, `ClinicalAlertNotificationTest`, `AssessmentMetadataPersistenceTest`, `LegacyPatientShowRenderingTest`) 37 failed / 131 passed (658 assertions) in BOTH the working tree and a stashed-HEAD baseline, with `Compare-Object` confirming the FAILED test-name lists are IDENTICAL - all 41 observed failures are pre-existing HEAD failures (part of the documented ~121 pre-existing suite failures), zero regressions attributable to this sprint.
- Defense notes: assessment logic lives in two small services plus one immutable outcome VO shared by server render and client mirror, so controller and views carry no clinical math; removing ONLY the linear weight-vs-GA blocker returns the controller to its pre-blocker shape (validation rules/messages/headers untouched, verified by diff = deletions only) while the legitimate 200-5000 g input guard remains, so out-of-range entry protection is unchanged; the WHO table is pinned as a const with anchor-self-resolution tests, so any future drift in the data fails the suite instead of silently misclassifying a fetus; classification is computed at full precision and rounded only for display with an explicit clarification line, preventing a display rounding from implying a different clinical category; FHR's restricted window and its evidence limits are rendered IN the panel (Unable instead of an unsupported normal, reference context named, NICE intrapartum thresholds not applied) so the UI can never present an out-of-window reading as validated normal; the Absent-heartbeat safety text is shown regardless of window and never asserts fetal demise, preserving the safety net while staying inside a display-only feature; the JS mirror reads `clientConfig()` rather than duplicating constants, so a future threshold change updates server and client from one source; panels and live script are additive includes with zero mutation of form controls, stored values, ML/referral/notification paths, or schema - failure of either service degrades to "Unable to Assess" text, never to a blocked save.
- Layout preservation addendum (2026-10-08): FHR panel relocated from inside the Fetal Heartbeat cell to a separate full-width row (`sm:col-span-2`, `data-assessment="fhr"`) immediately below the unchanged Fetal Heartbeat / Fetal Movement / Presentation dropdown row on both Add and Edit forms; the dropdown inputs themselves were not moved, renamed, or altered. Baseline form structure was documented first (section order: Scan Information -> Fetal Measurements -> Fetal Assessment -> Placenta & Amniotic Fluid -> Report Upload -> Remarks -> Cancel/Save; all fields, option sets, `required`/`step`/`min`/`max` attributes, IDs, `multipart` form with store/PUT submit targets, ID-bound file/validation/GA-autofill listeners), and a machine comparison of both forms before vs after shows ZERO differences in the form-control/section inventory (create 22/22 lines, edit 27/27 lines), identical ordered section comments (10/10 each), exactly one FHR include per form now positioned after `name="presentation"` and before the Placenta section, and balanced `<div>` counts (39/39, 42/42 before; 40/40, 43/43 after - one added full-width row pair). Inside the FHR panel only, the long technical `context` paragraph (ISUOG evidence provenance) is now wrapped in an accessible native `<details>/<summary>` disclosure; all essential clinical content remains immediately visible outside it - status chip, reason, full recommendation text including every Absent/out-of-window safety warning, the clinical-disclaimer `limitation` paragraph, source URLs, and the pending-review note. No service, controller, calculation, schema, environment, or unrelated page was modified this round; the ID/name-bound live script (`fetal-assessment-live`) is position-independent (`querySelector('[data-assessment]')` + `getElementById`), so panel relocation cannot affect it. Verification: `UltrasoundFetalAssessmentsTest` 9/9 green (incl. history-block and `staff-selected category` limitation assertions), `GestationalAgeAutofillTest` shows only the same 4 pre-existing HEAD failures, `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent throughout, no commit/push.
- Compact collapsible cards addendum (2026-10-08): both assessment cards redesigned to a compact, collapsed-by-default layout using native `<details>/<summary>` - NO form markup changed (create/edit/patients-show/controllers/services/VO untouched this round; only `resources/views/ultrasounds/partials/` + the feature test). Files: new `fetal-assessment-card-style.blade.php` (plain scoped CSS, zero new Tailwind utilities, zero build step: pale-green `#f0fdf4` background, soft `#bbf7d0` border, rounded corners, compact `0.625rem/0.75rem` header padding, focus-visible `#55b85a` outlines, chevron rotate + 0.15s body fade with `prefers-reduced-motion` guard, `View Details`/`Hide Details` label swap purely via CSS on the `[open]` state); rewrote `fetal-weight-assessment.blade.php` and `fetal-heart-rate-assessment.blade.php` (header = title left + existing `data-slot="chip"` status badge top-right via `flex-wrap` head + one-line `data-slot="summary"` + View Details control with chevron; body keeps every original element - rows, reason, recommendation, clarification, footnote/context, limitation, source links, pending disclaimer - plus a `type="button"` Hide Details control that closes the card and refocuses the summary without touching form submission; the previous round's inner context `<details>` was flattened into the card body since the whole card now collapses). Collapsed summaries are computed in the partials from EXISTING service output only (FWA: both-missing -> "Enter fetal weight and gestational age to view the assessment."; unable -> the existing reason; otherwise "{weight} at {ga} weeks - {label}"; FHR: not-recorded prompt, status/category one-liners, unable -> "{category} - {reason}") and mirrored in `fetal-assessment-live.blade.php` where `updateFwa`/`updateFhr` now also call `setSlotText(root, 'summary', ...)` on all four update paths (4 call sites), so live typing keeps the collapsed summary in sync without any behavior change to classification, rows, chips, or recommendations. CRITICAL SAFETY: when the recorded category is `Absent`, both server and client render the collapsed summary as "Absent - Prompt clinical assessment required. Confirm promptly with a qualified healthcare professional." REGARDLESS of GA window/status (Absence is checked before status branches), so the warning is visible while collapsed; the badge still shows the existing status (`Needs Clinical Review` in-window, `Unable to Assess Reliably` outside), and the full REC_ABSENT/SAFETY_ABSENT guidance remains in the expanded body. Badge label texts remain the existing service labels unchanged (`Above Expected Range`, `Unable to Assess`, `Within Applicable Reference`, `Needs Clinical Review`, `Unable to Assess Reliably` - services deliberately untouched). Tests: `UltrasoundFetalAssessmentsTest` extended to 10 tests / 97 assertions, all green - the new test asserts both cards render without an `open` attribute on Add and Edit, View/Hide Details controls present, title -> chip -> summary ordering inside the header, the empty-form prompt, exactly 4 live `setSlotText(root,'summary')` call sites, the Absent warning INSIDE the FHR `<summary>` block (visible while collapsed), the within-range collapsed summary "500 g at 22.5 weeks - Within Expected Range", and retained clinical text (`Reference context: ISUOG guidelines`, `does not diagnose fetal demise`); regression: `GestationalAgeAutofillTest` still exactly the same 4 pre-existing HEAD failures, both FWA/FHR unit suites 21/21 green, `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, no packages installed, no config/cache commands, no commit/push. HONEST LIMITATION: expand/collapse interaction, focus rings, and animation were verified by code review and server-rendered HTML assertions only - no browser automation is installed (and installing one is forbidden), so no real-browser click test was executed.

## DEPLA Phase 1: Authentication & Authorization Security Hardening (2026-10-08)

- Scope: close public registration, add a deny-by-default clinic middleware, role-correct login redirects, a temporary patient holding page, and security tests. New files: `app/Http/Middleware/EnsureClinicStaff.php`, `resources/views/patient-portal/coming-soon.blade.php`, `tests/Feature/ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest.php`, `tests/Feature/LoginRedirectSafetyTest.php`. Modified: `bootstrap/app.php`, `routes/auth.php`, `routes/web.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/Auth/AuthenticatedSessionController.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/DashboardController.php`, `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php`, `tests/Feature/Auth/RegistrationTest.php`, `database/factories/UserFactory.php`. NO migration, NO `.env` change, NO config/cache commands, NO package install, NO MySQL/seed operation, NO commit/push, NO clinical threshold or rule change.
- Registration closure: GET and POST `/register` are registered inside the existing `guest` group as route-level closures `fn () => abort(404)` - the route names are retained so the (now unreachable) `resources/views/auth/register.blade.php` and any `route('register')` call cannot throw a RouteNotFoundException; `RegisteredUserController` is kept on disk but is no longer reachable by any route; the unused controller import was removed from `routes/auth.php`. Before: a live GET/POST registration form creating accounts; after: 404 for both verbs (404 rather than 403 so probe scanners learn nothing about the endpoint's existence).
- Clinic boundary: new `EnsureClinicStaff` middleware (alias `clinic`) allows ONLY `role` in `['admin','staff']` via strict `in_array(..., true)`, everything else - guest is never reached (route has `auth` first), `patient`, `null`, or any unknown string - receives 403. `bootstrap/app.php` registers the alias and a `redirectUsersTo` callback: authenticated patient -> `route('patient.portal.coming-soon')`, every other case -> `route('dashboard')`, which then 403s unknown roles (deny-by-default fallback, no silent else-branch to staff).
- Route protection: `clinic` added to the `/dashboard` route and to the entire internal group (final stack `web,auth,clinic,no-store`, plus `staff` where previously present) - verified by `php artisan route:list --json` (artifact `routes-after.json`): patients, prenatal visits, ultrasound, birth plans, medical history, referrals, notifications, locations/search, staff administration, risk monitoring, reports, view-all, archive, refusal images - all `auth,clinic`. New `GET /patient-portal` (middleware `auth,no-store`, name `patient.portal.coming-soon`): patient -> holding page, admin/staff -> redirect to dashboard, unknown role -> 403. `GET /` is now role-aware: admin/staff -> dashboard, patient -> holding page, unknown -> 403 (was: everyone to dashboard). Intentionally left on `auth` only (any authenticated role, self-service): logout, confirm-password, password update, email verification, and the holding page itself.
- Login/logout redirects: `AuthenticatedSessionController::store()` now resolves the post-login destination through an explicit `match` on `$user->role` - `admin`/`staff` -> `route('dashboard')`, `patient` -> `route('patient.portal.coming-soon')`, default -> `denyUnknownRole()` which logs the user out, invalidates the session (`Session::invalidate()` + `regenerateToken()`) and `abort(403)` - return type `never`, so no unknown-role session ever survives in the app. `DashboardController::index()` mirrors the same explicit `admin`/`staff` branches with a 403 fallback instead of the previous else -> staff dashboard.
- Holding page: `resources/views/patient-portal/coming-soon.blade.php` extends `<x-guest-layout>` (renders `layouts/guest.blade.php`), DEPLA green theme, neutral wording that no clinical/clinic modules exist for the patient account yet, a Sign Out form (`POST /logout`), zero clinic navigation, zero patient-data output. Pre-render surface checked (see test 8 below).
- Sidebar UI: `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php` - the entire `<nav>` is wrapped in `@if(($user->role ?? null) === 'admin' || ($user->role ?? null) === 'staff')` ... `@endif`, and both former `role !== 'admin'` conditions were replaced with `role === 'staff'` (lines ~704/715/723/775). Rendered markup for admin and staff users is byte-identical to before (the wrapped conditions were already true for both), while an unknown-role user can no longer see the module menu (defense in depth behind the 403s).
- UserFactory fix: `database/factories/UserFactory.php` definition gains `'role' => 'staff'` mirroring the schema default. Root cause: `actingAs(User::factory()->create())` authenticates the in-memory model, whose `role` is NULL because the DB default is applied only on re-fetch, so the new middleware correctly 403'd `ProfileTest` (2 NEW failures in the first after-run). The factory now matches the DB default for every existing suite that asserts `fresh()->role === 'staff'`; no test expects NULL. All prior role assertions are unaffected.
- Tests: 21 tests / 149 assertions, all green. `RegistrationTest` rewritten (5): GET/POST `/register` are 404s (for guest AND authenticated staff), no User row is created, the login/register links are gone from the guest pages, `route('register')` still resolves (name preserved). New `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` (7): all guests redirected to login across the internal surface; patient-role user gets 403 on a 21-route matrix (GET + representative POST/PUT/DELETE) with zero row modifications (patients/prenatal/ultrasound/birth-plan/medical-history/notification counts unchanged after write attempts); unknown `role => 'clinician'` gets 403 across the same matrix; unknown role + guest can never see the holding page or internal page markup; admin and staff retain their documented access (`StaffAccessControlTest`/`AdminMiddleware` boundaries preserved); the public surface is limited to `/`, login, the 404 register routes, forgot/reset-password, `up`, and storage. New `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` (9): post-login redirect per role (admin -> dashboard 200, staff -> dashboard 200, patient -> holding 200), unknown role -> 403 + no authenticated session + session invalidated (logout confirmed), guest-visible login/register pages contain no internal-module links, `/` per role (admin/staff -> dashboard, patient -> holding, guest -> login redirect, unknown -> 403), and holding-page surface checks (Sign Out present; no sidebar nav; no patient/visit/referral/ultrasound/notification module strings; no `patients/`, `prenatal`, `birth-plan`, `medical-history` hrefs).
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` confirmed absent before and after every run; no `optimize`/`config:cache`/`optimize:clear`/`view:cache` ever executed; tests ran only on SQLite `:memory:` (phpunit.xml `force="true"` + `tests/TestCase.php` guard) - `maternity_system1`/MySQL never touched; `php -l` clean on all touched PHP files; no package install; no git commit/push/stash/reset/clean/worktree. Main regression gate (14 files: Auth + StaffAccessControl + StaffAccount* + StaffProfileBatchOne + RiskMonitoringAccess + ProfileAccessHeader + Profile + Notification + PsgcLocationSearch + PatientViewController + Example + LegacyPatientShowRendering): before-change baseline 20 failed / 175 passed, after-change 20 failed / 178 passed (+3 from the rewritten RegistrationTest) with byte-identical FAILED test-name sets - zero new failures, zero resolved (artifacts `hardening-baseline.txt`, `hardening-after2.txt`). Extended 12-file gate (ReferralAnalytics, RiskAnalytics, RiskMetricSemantics, PregnancyOutcomeRecording, PregnancyOutcomeMonitoringUi, PatientExportConsistency, PrenatalVisitsActiveListing, MedicalHistoryScope, PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrint, DeliveredPatientWorkflow, ClinicalAlertNotification, ReferralPrintHistory): a true HEAD baseline was built by backing up my 7 changed files to `$env:TEMP\opencode\phase1-backup`, temporarily restoring HEAD versions via `git checkout HEAD --` (verified none of the 7 overlapped any pre-existing uncommitted FWA/Phase-2/3 file), running the suite, then restoring my versions byte-for-byte (SHA-256 of all 7 files verified OK against the backup; `git status` restored to the exact pre-swap set). Result: 28 failed / 155 passed in BOTH runs with identical FAILED lists - all 28 (incl. the 2 `RiskAnalyticsTest` 403s from its bare-factory non-admin user hitting admin-only `risk.monitoring`) pre-exist at HEAD, zero attributable to this phase. Security suites re-run after the swap-back: 21/21 green.
- Defense notes: the boundary is deny-by-default (allowlist of exactly two roles, strict comparison) so a future role string or a NULL can never ride through an else-branch into clinic pages; registration returns 404 (no existence oracle) while authorization failures inside return 403 (honest for support/debugging); the unknown-role path fails closed TWICE - middleware/`match` 403 plus explicit session logout/invalidation in the login controller, so a crafted `role` row cannot leave a usable session in the system; post-login routing uses exhaustive `match` expressions in both controller and dashboard instead of nested ifs, so adding a role without adding a branch is a PHP fatal in dev rather than a silent wrong redirect; self-service auth routes (logout, password change, email verify) stay on `auth` only on purpose - restricting them would lock patients out of logging out of their own session; the factory change makes in-memory test users consistent with the DB default and cannot change production behavior (factories are test-only); the sidebar wrap is presentation-only defense-in-depth, and admin/staff rendered markup is unchanged (no UI regression for clinic users). Residual risks (accepted, out of scope this phase): (1) the unreachable legacy `auth/register.blade.php` view file remains on disk (no route renders it; removal deferred to a cleanup phase); (2) `password.reset`/`password.email` guest routes remain self-service by design; (3) the holding page is the only patient-facing surface - any future patient module must move its routes onto `clinic` or add its own explicit role guard; (4) pre-existing suite failures (~20 main + 28 extended) were NOT fixed here - they are documented baselines (date/analytics/viewData drift, admin-route tests using bare factory users).

## Expired-Session Logout Recovery: CSRF 419 on POST /logout Redirects to Login (2026-10-08)

- Scope: ONE targeted exception render callback in `bootstrap/app.php` `withExceptions()` + one new test file `tests/Feature/Auth/ExpiredSessionLogoutTest.php`. NO route, controller, middleware, `.env`, config/cache, DB, migration, or package change; `destroy()`, the POST-only logout route, CSRF enforcement, `no-store`, session settings, and all login/role redirects are untouched.
- Root cause (from the preceding read-only inspection): `VerifyCsrfToken` (web group) runs BEFORE route `auth` and throws `TokenMismatchException` when a browser submits the profile logout form with an expired session, rendering 419 AT `/logout`; a subsequent reload/address-bar Enter sends `GET /logout` -> 405. Laravel's Handler converts `TokenMismatchException` to `HttpException(419)` in `prepareException()` (Foundation/Exceptions/Handler.php:616/673) BEFORE render callbacks run (:618), so the callback type-hints `HttpException`, verifies `$e->getPrevious() instanceof TokenMismatchException`, and fires ONLY when the method is POST AND the path is exactly `logout` (checked via `$request->is('logout')`); everything else returns null and keeps the default 419 rendering. Matching requests get `redirect()->route('login')->with('status', 'Your session has expired. Please log in again.')` - displayed by the existing login page's session-status component - so the browser never parks on `/logout` and a stale token can never execute the logout controller (the exception is thrown in middleware before the route runs).
- Tests: 5 new tests / 13 assertions, all green - valid POST /logout with matching token still logs out + redirects `/`; expired/stale-token POST /logout redirects to login with the exact friendly message and no CSRF details in the body; stale token does not execute destroy() (staff session stays authenticated); GET /logout still 405 for guest AND authenticated; unrelated POST (`notifications.markAllRead`) with invalid token still 419 (proves the conversion is NOT global). CRITICAL: the web group registers `ValidateCsrfToken` (alias subclass of `VerifyCsrfToken`), whose `runningUnitTests()` bypass skips CSRF in tests - the helper rebinds `ValidateCsrfToken` to an anonymous subclass returning false from that bypass so the real `tokensMatch()` logic genuinely runs; the helper's necessity is proven by the tests failing before the bind targeted the right class and passing after.
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent before/after; tests on SQLite `:memory:` only (phpunit.xml force + TestCase guard); `php -l` clean; no git commit/push/stash/reset/clean; no `.env`/DB/package/cache-command touch. Regression vs the Phase 1 hardening baseline: Auth directory + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` + `ProfileAccessHeaderTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` = 70 passed / 2 failed, where the 2 (`ProfileAccessHeaderTest > profile update changes only...` and `StaffAccessControlTest > it hides Patient Records edit action...`) are byte-identical to the documented pre-existing baseline entries - zero new failures; all existing logout tests (AuthenticationTest, AuthenticatedCacheControlTest, ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest logout, ProfileAccessHeaderTest logout) green.
- Defense notes: the handler is triple-guarded (419 status + previous TokenMismatchException + POST + exact path), so no other 419, method, or route can be converted, and no exception details are exposed (redirect only); CSRF is still ENFORCED - a mismatched token still refuses to log out and never reaches `destroy()`; valid logout flow (session invalidation + `redirect('/')`) is untouched and pinned by tests; the stale-token test genuinely exercises the mismatch because the CSRF unit-test bypass is disabled only for that middleware binding, not by weakening any production code. Residual note: an expired-session user redirected to `/login` while a stale tab still holds a live session cookie would be bounced by the guest middleware to their dashboard - harmless (in the real expiry scenario the session is dead so the login page renders); no GET /logout was added.

## DEPLA Phase 2A: patients.user_id Login-Account Link (2026-10-09)

- Scope: additive schema + two model relations ONLY. New files: `database/migrations/2026_10_09_000001_add_user_id_to_patients_table.php` and `tests/Feature/Phase2APatientLoginAccountLinkTest.php`. Modified: `app/Models/Patient.php` (one new method + docblock), `app/Models/User.php` (one `HasOne` import + one new method), this file. NO account-creation workflow, NO initial-password handling, NO login/logout or first-login password change, NO route, controller, middleware, Blade view, Coming-Soon page, `Patient` Profile UI, Add New Patient form, `.env`, `config/database.php`, existing migration, clinical module, appointment, package install, or git operation.
- Schema: `patients.user_id` is `foreignId` (unsigned BIGINT, matching `users.id`) `->nullable()->unique()->after('assigned_staff_id')->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete()` - i.e. nullable (every existing patient keeps working with no account), UNIQUE (one account can never belong to two patients), `ON DELETE SET NULL` (removing an account never removes a patient). It is deliberately a different column from `patients.assigned_staff_id`, which stays the clinical assignment; `Patient::$fillable` was NOT changed, so `user_id` can never be mass-assigned (test 13 pins this).
- Pre-existing-column handling: `up()` first runs `Schema::hasColumn('patients','user_id')`. If the column exists it does NOT blindly re-create it: it reads `Schema::getColumns/getIndexes/getForeignKeys` and requires a nullable INTEGER-family column, exactly one non-primary unique index whose columns are `['user_id']`, and a foreign key on `['user_id']` -> `users`.`id` with `on delete set null`. Compatible -> no-op (re-running `up()` is idempotent, test 14); incompatible (verified by test 16 with a `varchar(50) NOT NULL` stand-in) -> `RuntimeException` before any DDL, never a silent accept.
- Rollback: `down()` drops exactly three objects, in three separate `Schema::table` calls ordered `dropForeign` -> `dropUnique` -> `dropColumn`. The order is not cosmetic. (1) MySQL: `patients_user_id_unique` is the index backing `patients_user_id_foreign`, so dropping the index first fails with MySQL 1553 "needed in a foreign key constraint"; dropping the FK first is required. (2) SQLite (Laravel 12 `Blueprint::addAlterCommands`): `dropForeign` is in `getAlterCommands()` and forces a table rebuild, while `dropColumn` is NOT (server is SQLite 3.39.2 >= 3.35), so the rebuild runs first, then `drop index`, then `alter table ... drop column` - each statement still finds its target. Constraint/index names are discovered from the live schema rather than assumed, because `SQLiteProcessor::processForeignKeys()` reports `'name' => null` (SQLite does not expose FK names) while `MySqlProcessor` returns the real `information_schema` name; on SQLite the conventional `['user_id']` array is used, which is what the rebuild matches on anyway, and on MySQL the actual constraint name is used.
- Relations: `Patient::loginAccount(): BelongsTo` = `belongsTo(User::class, 'user_id')->withTrashed()`, and `User::patientAccount(): HasOne` = `hasOne(Patient::class, 'user_id')` WITHOUT `withTrashed()` (an archived patient is not an active portal record). `BelongsTo`/`HasOne` have no native `withTrashed()` method, so `Relation::__call` forwards through `forwardDecoratedCallTo` to the `withTrashed` macro registered by `SoftDeletingScope::addWithTrashed`, which returns the Builder and is therefore decorated back to the Relation - the declared return types hold and no re-typing was needed (tests 5, 10, 11 assert both).
- Tests: 17 tests / 61 assertions, all green (`tests/Feature/Phase2APatientLoginAccountLinkTest.php`): column exists after `migrate:fresh` on the pre-existing `patients` table; nullable + unique index + `set null` FK introspected; patient creatable with no account; many NULL `user_id` rows at once; valid link resolves through `loginAccount()`; UNIQUE rejects a second patient for the same account; missing `users.id` rejected (FK enforcement is ON via `foreign_key_constraints => env('DB_FOREIGN_KEYS', true)`); hard-deleting the account NULLs `user_id` and the patient row survives untrashed; soft-deleting the account keeps the link; `loginAccount()` resolves the soft-deleted account; `patientAccount()` resolves the patient and returns null for an archived one; `assigned_staff_id` stays independent through a link and a hard-delete; `user_id` ignored by mass assignment; `up()` idempotent when the column exists; incompatible pre-existing column throws `RuntimeException`; rollback removes only the new objects (column, unique index, FK) while `assigned_staff_id`/`first_name`/`email`/`status`, the `assigned_staff_id` FK and the patient row itself survive, then `up()` restores; and a documented limitation test proving the database accepts a non-`patient` role on a linked account.
- Role-enforcement limitation (explicit, executable): `users.role` is a plain string defaulting to `'staff'` with no CHECK constraint and no schema-level link to `patients.user_id`, so NOTHING in the database can guarantee that a linked account has `role = 'patient'` - or that every `role = 'patient'` account is linked. Both invariants must be enforced by the Phase 2B creation workflow (transactional create-user + link + role assignment) and by server-side first-login checks; they cannot be migrated into a constraint. Test `cannot be enforced by the schema that a login account has role patient` pins this limitation so it cannot be quietly forgotten.
- Verification: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` confirmed absent before every run; tests on SQLite `:memory:` only (phpunit.xml `force="true"` + `tests/TestCase.php` isolation guard) - `maternity_system1`/MySQL never touched; no `php artisan optimize`/`config:cache`/`optimize:clear`/`migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`db:seed` executed manually; `php -l` clean on the migration and both models; no package install; no git commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch; `git status --porcelain` = only `M app/Models/Patient.php`, `M app/Models/User.php`, `?? database/migrations/2026_10_09_000001_...`, `?? tests/Feature/Phase2APatientLoginAccountLinkTest.php` plus the pre-existing untracked `public/hot`. Regression gate batch 1 (`SchemaContractTest` + `ReferralMigrationRollbackSafetyTest` + `ReferralIntegrationSchemaTest` + `PatientStructuredAddressTest` + `PregnancyOutcomeMigrationTest` + `MedicalHistoryScopeTest` + `PatientViewControllerTest`) = 74 passed / 18 failed; the 18 failures were proven pre-existing by moving ONLY the new migration file to a temp path, re-running `PatientViewControllerTest` + `MedicalHistoryScopeTest`, and restoring it in a `finally` block (file verified back in place, `php -l` re-run) - baseline 18 failed / 25 passed with `Compare-Object` reporting IDENTICAL FAILED test-name sets. Regression gate batch 2 (`tests/Feature/Auth` + `StaffAccessControlTest` + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest`) = 51 passed / 1 failed, where the 1 failure (`StaffAccessControlTest > it hides Patient Records edit action...`) is the exact entry documented as pre-existing in the CSRF-419 entry above. Zero new failures attributable to Phase 2A.
- Defense notes: the guard means a partially-managed or externally-created `user_id` column can never be double-added or silently reused - the migration either creates the exact intended shape or refuses with an explanatory `RuntimeException`; `down()` drops objects by discovered name instead of hardcoded names so a compatible foreign index created elsewhere is removed too, and the explicit `dropForeign -> dropUnique -> dropColumn` order is correct on BOTH MySQL (index-backs-FK rule) and SQLite (rebuild-before-drop-column); `user_id` is absent from `$fillable`, so a form post or `Patient::create()` payload can never silently retarget a patient's login account; `ON DELETE SET NULL` plus a separate `assigned_staff_id` means deleting an account can never delete a patient or silently reassign clinical ownership; `loginAccount()` uses `withTrashed()` so an archived account still resolves for audit/unlink instead of reading as "no account", while `patientAccount()` deliberately excludes archived patients so an active portal session can never resolve to a trashed row; no clinical threshold, rule, controller, route or view was touched, so clinical behavior and the Phase 1 auth hardening are unchanged. Residual risks (accepted, Phase 2B scope): the database cannot enforce `role = 'patient'` on a linked account (pinned by a test, must be handled by the creation workflow and login checks); `down()` is symmetric with `up()` and would also drop a pre-existing-but-compatible column; no production MySQL/Railway migration has been executed - deployment must run `php artisan migrate` on the real database as a separate, explicitly approved step.

## DEPLA Phase 2A.1: Patient Account Migration Rollback Safety Fix (2026-10-09)

- Scope: ONE migration + ONE test file. Modified `database/migrations/2026_10_09_000001_add_user_id_to_patients_table.php` and `tests/Feature/Phase2APatientLoginAccountLinkTest.php`, plus this entry. NO model, route, controller, middleware, Blade, auth, clinical, Patient Portal or appointment change; NO other migration; NO `.env`/config change; NO package install; NO `php artisan migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`db:seed`/`optimize:clear`/`config:cache`; NO git commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch. `public/hot` untouched.
- Root cause: the Phase 2A version of `up()` treated a pre-existing `patients.user_id` as a compatible no-op (`if (Schema::hasColumn(...)) { assertCompatible(); return; }`), while `down()` dropped the column, unique index and foreign key whenever the column existed - with no knowledge of who created it. Laravel writes the migration row only AFTER `up()` returns (`Migrator::runUp` L253 `runMigration()` then L258 `repository->log()`) and `runDown` (L417/L422) is fed only from the repository, so `down()` runs in a separate process with no memory of `up()`. No schema attribute distinguishes "created by `up()`" from "created by something else", so under the old code a rollback could delete a compatible column, unique index and foreign key this migration never owned - i.e. it silently claimed ownership of foreign schema.
- Fix (smallest reliable correction, no new state): `up()` now throws for ANY pre-existing `patients.user_id`, compatible or not, BEFORE any DDL. Ownership is therefore settled once, in `up()`, using the only reliable signal available - Laravel's log-after-success ordering. A recorded migration row now proves this migration created the column itself, so `down()` is safe by construction rather than by detection. No ownership table, marker or configuration flag was introduced. `down()` is unchanged in behavior (drop FK -> drop unique index -> drop column, by discovered names) and its docblock now states the invariant it depends on.
- Refusal messages are split because the two cases need different actions. Case B (pre-existing AND shape-compatible): refused for OWNERSHIP - "matches the expected shape, but this migration did not create it, so it will not take ownership of it", explaining that adopting it would let a later rollback drop someone else's objects, and naming both remedies (drop the existing column so this migration can own it, or remove this migration from the run). Case C (pre-existing AND mismatched): refused for SHAPE, listing every specific problem from `shapeProblems()` - non-nullable, non-integer type, missing unique index, missing/wrong foreign key (with the actual target and `on delete` rule found). Both messages state explicitly that no schema changes were made; `preExistingColumnError()` only performs read-only introspection (`getColumns`/`getIndexes`/`getForeignKeys`) and is thrown before `Schema::table()`, so Cases B and C cannot write anything even on MySQL where DDL is non-transactional.
- Case A (column absent) is untouched: `foreignId('user_id')->nullable()->unique()->after('assigned_staff_id')->constrained('users')->nullOnDelete()`, so type, nullability, uniqueness, `users.id` reference and `ON DELETE SET NULL` are exactly as Phase 2A specified. `down()` still removes exactly those three objects.
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase2APatientLoginAccountLinkTest.php` = 17 tests / 78 assertions, all green (was 61). New helper `phase2aPatientsSchemaSnapshot()` returns a deterministic, sorted JSON snapshot of every column (name/type/nullable/default), every index (name/columns/unique/primary), every foreign key (columns/table/referenced columns/on delete) and every patient row; `phase2aRefusalMessage()` runs `up()` and returns the exception message or null. Coverage against the required list: (1) fresh migration creates the correct schema - `creates the patients.user_id column during a fresh migration run` + `stores patients.user_id as a nullable column with a unique index and a set-null foreign key`; (2) rollback removes what this migration created - `rolls back only the objects Phase 2A introduced`; (3) existing COMPATIBLE schema is not destructively changed - REPLACED `runs the migration safely when patients.user_id already exists` with `refuses a compatible pre-existing patients.user_id without touching the schema`, which seeds a fully linked patient, asserts the ownership message, then asserts the full schema+data snapshot is byte-identical after the refusal and that the row still resolves through `loginAccount()`; (4) existing INCOMPATIBLE schema rejected safely - `refuses an incompatible pre-existing patients.user_id column` now also asserts all four specific problem strings, that the snapshot is unchanged, and that the column is still `varchar` (i.e. not replaced or constrained); (5) existing patient records survive rollback - assertions added to the rollback test (`patients` count still 1, `Patient::find()` non-null and not trashed, `first_name` and `assigned_staff_id` intact, `assignedStaff()` still resolves); (6) unique + FK still enforced - `rejects the same user account being linked to a second patient` (UNIQUE) and `rejects a user_id value that does not exist in the users table` (FK); (7) soft-deleted accounts resolvable - `resolves a soft-deleted login account through Patient::loginAccount`; (8) staff assignment unaffected - `keeps assigned_staff_id independent of the login account link`. Tests were strengthened, not weakened: the old no-op expectation was replaced by an explicit refusal expectation, and the refusal is non-vacuous (the previous `up()` returned normally for a compatible column, so `expect($message)->not->toBeNull()` would have failed against it).
- Verification: safety checks passed before running - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` `ensureDatabaseIsolation()` guard active in `refreshApplication()`. Ran ONLY `tests/Feature/Phase2APatientLoginAccountLinkTest.php` (17/17) plus the directly necessary migration-specific `SchemaContractTest` + `ReferralMigrationRollbackSafetyTest` (8/8) = 25 passed / 109 assertions, 0 failed. No full suite. `php -l` clean on both changed files. `maternity_system1`, local MySQL and Railway untouched; no real-database migration executed.
- Defense notes: ownership is established by the framework's own ordering guarantee (record only after success) rather than by a heuristic, so it holds identically on SQLite and MySQL/MariaDB and needs no extra persistence; the failure direction is chosen deliberately - refusing to run is safe (blocks a deploy loudly, writes nothing), while adopting-and-then-rolling-back is unsafe (destroys foreign schema silently); a pre-existing column now BLOCKS `php artisan migrate`, which is intentional: an operator must resolve the ambiguity explicitly instead of inheriting it. Supersedes the Phase 2A residual risk "down() is symmetric with up() and would also drop a pre-existing-but-compatible column" - `down()` can no longer be reached for a column `up()` did not create, because `up()` now refuses and the migration is never recorded. Residual risks (approval required before Phase 2B): (1) this invariant assumes NO environment ever recorded this migration under the PRE-FIX `up()` semantics - true for everything this session touched (file was never run against MySQL/Railway, tests use `:memory:` recreated per run, `git status` shows it untracked), but it MUST be verified on the target database before deploying with `SELECT * FROM migrations WHERE migration = '2026_10_09_000001_add_user_id_to_patients_table'` alongside an inspection of the real `patients.user_id` state; if a row exists WITHOUT a column created by this migration, roll the row back manually rather than trusting `down()`; (2) a manual/forced `down()` call bypassing the repository would still drop an existing column - `down()` cannot detect that case and deliberately does not guess; (3) the database still cannot enforce `role = 'patient'` on a linked account (unchanged Phase 2B workflow concern).

## DEPLA Phase 2B: Secure Patient Account Creation Backend (2026-10-09)

- Scope: backend ONLY. Created `database/migrations/2026_10_09_000002_add_must_change_password_to_users_table.php`, `app/Services/PatientAccountCreationService.php`, `app/Http/Requests/PatientAccountStoreRequest.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/PatientAccountController.php`, `tests/Feature/Phase2BPatientAccountCreationTest.php`; modified `routes/web.php` (one POST route), `app/Models/User.php` (one cast), `app/Models/AuditLog.php` (one fillable key) and this file. NO Blade, no patient-facing UI, no Patient Portal, no appointment booking, no clinical module, no `must_change_password` enforcement (route/controller/view/middleware), no invitation or activation email, no package install, no commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch, `public/hot` untouched, no `.env`/`config/database.php` change, no `php artisan migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`db:seed`/`optimize:clear`/`config:cache` executed against any real database.
- Inspection findings (Phase 2B section 4): conventions reused rather than reinvented - service pattern from `PregnancyOutcomeRecordingService` (DB::transaction + lockForUpdate + DomainException), controller error handling from `PatientController::markDelivered` (catch DomainException -> back()->withErrors()), password policy from `StaffProfileRequest` (`required|string|confirmed|Password::defaults()`, i.e. min 8), audit via `AuditLog::create`, service-container injection as in `PatientController`. Auth stack: `auth` + `clinic` (`EnsureClinicStaff`, role exactly admin/staff) + `no-store`; `staff`/`admin` aliases unchanged.
- ONE SCOPING DECISION TO NOTE: all existing patient WRITE routes live inside the `staff`-only group (Admin is forbidden), while this specification explicitly requires "Only authenticated Admin and Staff" and "use the existing `auth` and `clinic` protections". The new route is therefore registered at the `auth` + `clinic` level, OUTSIDE the staff-only clinical write group, and no existing route or middleware was altered. Rationale: the endpoint creates one `users` row, sets `patients.user_id` and writes one audit row - it never creates a patient and never touches clinical data - so the staff-only restriction that guards clinical writes does not apply to it. Both clinic roles are covered by tests 1 and 2, and Admin remains forbidden from every pre-existing staff-only route (asserted in test 28).
- Migration `2026_10_09_000002_add_must_change_password_to_users_table`: `must_change_password` boolean, NOT NULL, default `false`, `->after('email_verified_at')` (MySQL ordering only; SQLite grammar ignores `after`). Existing Admin/Staff rows keep `false`; no password is rewritten, no reset is triggered and `email_verified_at` is untouched. Ownership safety mirrors Phase 2A.1: `up()` throws a `RuntimeException` BEFORE any DDL when the column already exists ("this migration did not create it, so it will not take ownership of it ... No schema changes were made"), so a foreign column is never adopted; because Migrator::runUp() records the migration only after `up()` returns, `down()` can only run for a column this migration created. `down()` drops ONLY that column and returns early when it is already absent (same idempotent guard style as `2026_10_05_000002`).
- Service `PatientAccountCreationService::createForPatient(Patient, User $actor, string $email, string $plainTextPassword): User`, one `DB::transaction` in the specification's order: (1) re-read the patient with `Patient::withTrashed()->whereKey()->lockForUpdate()` (TOCTOU protection; withTrashed so an archived record is refused with a message instead of a silent not-found); (2) reject missing; (3) reject `$locked->trashed()`; (4) reject `$locked->user_id !== null` - this covers soft-deleted accounts too, because the Phase 2A FK is ON DELETE SET NULL and only clears the link on hard delete; (5) re-validate email format/length and password strength with `Validator` + `Password::defaults()` (defence in depth for direct callers); (6) reject an email already used by ANY user via `User::withTrashed()`; (7) build the user with explicit property assignment (never mass assignment): `name` assembled from first/middle/last exactly like `StaffProfileRequest::prepareForValidation`, first/middle/last capped at the `users` columns' 100 chars (patients allow 255), `email`, `Hash::make($password)`, `role='patient'`, `email_verified_at=null`, `must_change_password=true`; (8) insert, translating `UniqueConstraintViolationException` into the same safe email-conflict message (no SQL surfaced); (9) link by direct property write `$locked->user_id = $user->id; $locked->save()` - `user_id` is not in `Patient::$fillable`, so this is the only write path; (10) `AuditLog::create(...)` INSIDE the transaction; (11) commit. The patient's own email, contact details, clinical columns and `assigned_staff_id` are never written, no second patients row is ever created and no name/email/contact matching is performed. `assigned_staff_id` is only read for the audit label, never modified.
- Concurrency/integrity: patient row lock serializes two simultaneous requests for the same patient (the loser sees `user_id` already set and is refused); `users.email` unique covers active AND soft-deleted rows; `patients.user_id` unique prevents one account linking two patients; both constraints are additionally exercised directly at the database level in tests. Duplicate-key errors are caught and re-raised as a business message, so neither SQL nor credentials can leak. Archived accounts are never restored or repurposed - their email stays blocked.
- Validation (`PatientAccountStoreRequest`): `authorize()` independently re-requires an existing, non-trashed `App\Models\User` whose role is admin or staff (defence in depth on top of `auth`+`clinic`, and the reason an archived account forced onto the request still gets 403); `prepareForValidation()` trims and lower-cases the email before validation; rules are `required|string|email|max:255|Rule::unique('users','email')` (DatabasePresenceVerifier reads the table directly, so soft-deleted rows are included) and `required|string|confirmed|Password::defaults()` plus an explicit `password_confirmation => required` so a missing confirmation is reported as missing. No new password package and no policy change.
- Controller `PatientAccountController@store`: resolves `Patient::withTrashed()->find()` (404 when absent), invokes the service, converts `DomainException`/`UniqueConstraintViolationException` into `back()->withErrors([...])` - deliberately WITHOUT `withInput()`, because flashing the raw request would copy the submitted initial password into the session - and on success redirects to `route('patients.show', $patient)` with a `success` flash that never contains the password. The `ValidationException` path is safe for free: Laravel's Handler excludes `current_password`, `password` and `password_confirmation` from `withInput()`. CSRF protection is untouched; no GET route exists (asserted: GET answers 405).
- Audit: `action = PATIENT_ACCOUNT_CREATED` (UPPER_SNAKE, matching the existing vocabulary `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED`, `MEDICAL_HISTORY_SYNC`, `BP_REPEAT_RECORDED` rather than the lowercase illustration in the specification), `module = PATIENT`, `user_id` = acting Admin/Staff, `patient_id` = the patient, `created_at` from the standard audit timestamps, and `description = "Patient login account (user ID N) created for <first> <last> (patient ID M)."`. `patient_id` was added to `AuditLog::$fillable` - a model change only; the `audit_logs` migration is untouched and the column already existed. Never recorded: plaintext password, confirmation, hash, reset tokens or session credentials (test 24 dumps every attribute of every audit row and asserts none of them contain the password, the bcrypt hash or the substring `$2y$`). The audit write is INSIDE the transaction, so an account can never exist without its audit entry - this intentionally differs from `PatientController::markDelivered`, which audits after commit, because an unauditable account provisioning is the failure mode this phase must not permit.
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase2BPatientAccountCreationTest.php` = **32 tests / 151 assertions, all green**, covering all 28 required cases plus 4 extras (no GET page, service-boundary re-validation, `users.email` unique at the DB level, `patients.user_id` unique at the DB level). Atomicity is proven with a deterministic post-insert failure injection: the service is called with a non-existent actor id so the mandatory `audit_logs.user_id` foreign key rejects the write AFTER the user insert and AFTER the link, then the test asserts no user, no link, no audit row and still exactly one patient row. Archived-user rejection is covered two ways: a real session key (`login_web_'.sha1(SessionGuard::class)`) for an archived id is rehydrated through `EloquentUserProvider` + the SoftDeletes scope and therefore redirected to login by `auth`, and `actingAs` of an archived account is refused 403 by `authorize()` (the two must run in that order because `actingAs` pins the user onto the shared guard for the rest of the test).
- Verification: safety checks passed before every run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` `ensureDatabaseIsolation()` guard active. Focused run only (no full suite): Phase 2B + Phase 2A + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` = **73 passed / 1 failed / 379 assertions**. The single failure is `StaffAccessControlTest > it hides Patient Records edit action while keeping profile and profile-related actions`, the exact pre-existing baseline failure documented in the CSRF-419 entry and re-confirmed during Phase 2A - it asserts on the patient index view's edit link, which this phase does not touch. `php -l` clean on every changed/created file; `route:list` confirms `POST patients/{patient}/account -> patients.account.store`. Local MySQL `maternity_system1` and Railway untouched; no real-database migration executed.
