- Defense notes: authorization is deny-by-default and layered (auth middleware -> clinic middleware -> FormRequest authorize), so the endpoint cannot be widened by editing only one layer; validation exists in two places because the service is also a direct call surface; the flag is excluded from `$fillable` so no form payload can flip it on an existing account, while ordinary `User::create()`/factory users keep the database default `false`; the transaction boundary is drawn so that the user row, the link and the audit entry are one atomic unit; failure modes return business messages (403/404/302-with-errors) rather than 500s wherever they are expected.
- Residual risks (approval required before Phase 2C): (1) `must_change_password` is written but not yet ENFORCED anywhere - Phase 2C must add the mandatory password-change route, controller, view and first-login enforcement middleware, and must decide the redirect behavior for a patient account that still has the flag set (including how a patient user reaches that flow from the Coming Soon portal); (2) login currently passes the raw email to `Auth::attempt` while this endpoint stores a trimmed/lower-cased one - on MySQL's default case-insensitive collation this is a no-op, but Phase 2C must apply the same normalization when it accepts email input if SQLite or a case-sensitive collation is ever used; (3) `users.first_name` etc. are copied from the patient record at provisioning time only, so later edits to patient names will not update the account profile (intentional - the account is a login, not a clinical record); (4) neither migration has been run on local MySQL or Railway - deployment must run `php artisan migrate` as a separate, explicitly approved step, and Phase 2A.1's pre-deploy migration-history check still applies; (5) the database still cannot enforce `role = 'patient'` on a linked account (unchanged).

## DEPLA Phase 2C: Mandatory First-Login Password Change (2026-10-09)

- Scope: enforcement + the single patient-facing screen this phase is allowed. Created `app/Http/Middleware/EnsureInitialPasswordChanged.php`, `app/Http/Middleware/EnsurePatientPortalAccount.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/PatientInitialPasswordController.php`, `app/Http/Requests/ChangeInitialPasswordRequest.php`, `resources/views/patient/change-initial-password.blade.php`, `tests/Feature/Phase2CInitialPasswordChangeTest.php`; modified `bootstrap/app.php` (two aliases), `routes/web.php` (portal middleware + GET/POST change routes), `routes/auth.php` (flag middleware on the `auth` group), `resources/views/layouts/guest.blade.php` (one header branch), `app/Http/Requests/Auth/LoginRequest.php` (email normalization), `app/Http/Controllers/Auth/PasswordResetLinkController.php` + `app/Http/Controllers/Auth/NewPasswordController.php` (same normalization), `tests/Feature/LoginRedirectSafetyTest.php` + `tests/Feature/ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest.php` + `tests/Feature/Phase2BPatientAccountCreationTest.php` (fixture links only, see §11 decision) and this file. NO Create-Patient-Account button/modal, NO Patient Profile account UI, NO Patient Portal dashboard, NO appointment booking, NO patient clinical-record access, NO self-registration, NO invitation/activation links, NO account-verification system, NO password-recovery redesign (the reset flow only received input normalization), NO Add New Patient or clinical-module change, NO package install, NO commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch, `public/hot` untouched, no `.env`/`config/database.php` change, no `php artisan migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`db:seed`/`optimize:clear`/`config:cache` executed against any real database.
- ONE USER-APPROVED SPEC CONFLICT (§11): the required portal-eligibility rule (a patient login must be linked to a non-archived `patients` row, else 403) conflicted with three existing tests that create a bare `role='patient'` user and assert the Coming Soon page answers 200 (`LoginRedirectSafetyTest` "patient login lands...", `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` "patient role can only reach the holding page...", `Phase2B...` "keeps existing clinic route..."). Reported before implementation and resolved by explicit user decision: implement §11 and fix the FIXTURES only - each test now links its patient user to a `patients` row (`user_id` write on the fixture); no assertion in any of the three files was changed, and the Phase 1/Phase 2B security expectations they encode are intact.
- Inspection findings reused rather than reinvented: middleware alias style from `bootstrap/app.php` (`staff`/`clinic`/`no-store`); deny-by-default posture from `EnsureClinicStaff`; audit write via `AuditLog::create` inside the transaction exactly as `PatientAccountCreationService` does (`action` UPPER_SNAKE, `module = PATIENT`); password policy from `ProfileUpdateRequest`/`PasswordController` (`required|string|confirmed|Password::defaults()` + the `current_password` rule, i.e. min 8 - no new policy, no threshold change); guest Blade card + `fancy-input`/`password-toggle` design from `auth/login.blade.php` and `patient-portal/coming-soon.blade.php`; error handling from the Phase 2B controller (never `withInput()` when secrets are involved - here it is not needed at all because `Handler::$dontFlash` already strips `current_password`/`password`/`password_confirmation`). No `app/Listeners/*` exists and nothing registers a `PasswordReset` listener, so the reset flow has no hidden side effects on the flag.
- Middleware `EnsureInitialPasswordChanged` (alias `must-change-password`): acts ONLY when `role === 'patient'` AND `must_change_password` is true, otherwise it is a pure pass-through - so attaching it to the shared `auth` group cannot alter any Admin/Staff/unknown-role behavior. When it fires it redirects to `patient.change-initial-password.edit`. Three route names are exempt: `logout` (a patient who cannot supply the current password must never be trapped) and both change-route names (so applying the middleware to those routes too can never produce a redirect loop - defense against a later copy/paste).
- Middleware `EnsurePatientPortalAccount` (alias `patient-portal-account`): also patient-scoped; resolves `Patient::withTrashed()->where('user_id', $user->id)` and aborts 403 when there is NO link or the linked record IS trashed (archived), with an explanatory message. `withTrashed()` is deliberate: an archived record must surface as an explicit denial instead of an indistinguishable "not found". Staff/admin/unknown roles pass straight through to the existing closure, which keeps its redirect-to-dashboard and 403 behavior byte for byte.
- Middleware ORDER on `/patient-portal` is `['auth','patient-portal-account','must-change-password','no-store']`: authorization before workflow, so a doomed (unlinked/archived) account is refused before any redirect chain starts. Consequence, covered by tests: a flagged patient with NO live link gets 403 on the portal but the change form itself (which carries no eligibility rule) stays open, so the credential can still be rotated - the portal is gated, the password flow is not.
- Routes: `/patient-portal` gained the two new aliases (previously `['auth','no-store']`). New group in `routes/web.php` with `['auth','no-store']` only: `GET /patient/change-initial-password -> edit` named `patient.change-initial-password.edit`, `POST /patient/change-initial-password -> update` named `patient.change-initial-password.update` (PUT/PATCH/DELETE answer 405 - asserted). The flag is deliberately NOT enforced by middleware on these two routes (that would loop); it is enforced by the controller and the FormRequest. `routes/auth.php`'s `auth` group became `['auth','must-change-password']`, which closes the `PUT /password` (`password.update`) side door: a flagged patient can no longer change their password through the un-audited profile endpoint - the only path left is the audited one (asserted, including that password, flag and audit row are all untouched after the blocked attempt).
- Controller `PatientInitialPasswordController`: `edit()` 403s non-patients, redirects to the portal when the flag is already cleared (second tab / refresh after success), otherwise renders the view; `update()` runs one `DB::transaction` that force-fills the hashed new password AND `must_change_password = false` AND writes `AuditLog`, then (after commit) calls `$request->session()->regenerate()` - rotate only on success, so a rolled-back change never leaves a new password under an old session id. Audit row: `action = PATIENT_INITIAL_PASSWORD_CHANGED`, `module = PATIENT`, `user_id` = the patient account, `patient_id` = the linked record resolved `withTrashed()` (NULL only when unlinked), description naming the user id only - never old/new password, hash or token (asserted).
- FormRequest `ChangeInitialPasswordRequest`: `authorize()` requires role `patient` AND the flag; `failedAuthorization()` then distinguishes the two failure reasons - a patient whose flag is already cleared is redirected to the portal (via `HttpResponseException`, so it resolves BEFORE any rule runs: "no state change" holds even for a wrong/garbage payload, which a test proves by posting a deliberately wrong `current_password`), while guests/staff/admin/unknown roles keep the framework's 403. Rules: `current_password => required|current_password`, `password => required|string|confirmed|different:current_password|Password::defaults()`, `password_confirmation => required|string`, with explicit friendly messages.
- View `resources/views/patient/change-initial-password.blade.php`: renders inside the shared guest layout (no clinic navigation - asserted `sidebar-nav`, `Manage Staff`, `Audit Logs`, `Prenatal Visits` absent), DEPLA green card styling, three labeled fields with correct `autocomplete` (`current-password`, `new-password`), per-field show/hide toggles (own scoped JS function, so the layout's login-only `togglePasswordVisibility` is never hijacked), a password-policy hint, a per-field error list plus the `error-summary` component, an always-visible "Sign Out instead" escape hatch, and no `old()` on any password field (a test re-renders after a validation failure and asserts no `value` attribute exists on any of the three inputs). `guest.blade.php` gained one `@elseif (request()->routeIs('patient.change-initial-password.*'))` header branch ("Change Your Password / Required before you can continue") - the only layout change, inside the existing header chain. The route carries `no-store`, and the response is asserted to carry `no-store` + `private` (Symfony re-orders the directives, so the directives are asserted, not the exact string).
- Email normalization (resolves Phase 2B residual risk 2): `LoginRequest::prepareForValidation()` trims and lower-cases `email` (when it is a string) before validation, rate-limit key derivation and `Auth::attempt` - identical to what `PatientAccountCreationService::normalizeEmail()` stores. The same narrow normalization was added to `PasswordResetLinkController::store()` and to `NewPasswordController::create()`/`store()`, because inspection showed the reset lookup is equally raw (`Password::sendResetLink`/`broker->getUser`); without it a case variant would resolve on MySQL's default ci collation but fail on a case-sensitive one. It is a no-op on MySQL, and every stored email in the codebase is already lower-cased (`StaffProfileRequest`/`ProfileUpdateRequest` use the `lowercase` rule, Phase 2B normalizes, `safeEmail()` factory output is lower-case), so no existing lookup semantics change.
- Password-reset interaction: `NewPasswordController` only force-fills `password` + `remember_token`, so `must_change_password` is preserved by construction - NO code change was made there for the flag, only regression tests (a completed reset keeps the flag set, the new hash verifies, and the portal still forces the change form afterwards; merely REQUESTING a link changes nothing). The reset endpoints stay in the `guest` group, so a logged-in patient cannot reach them.
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase2CInitialPasswordChangeTest.php` = **36 tests / 162 assertions, all green**, in nine sections: enforcement (portal redirect, login->portal->form chain, `PUT /password` bypass closed, `password.confirm`/`verification.notice` covered, clinic routes still 403, logout still works, staff/admin/unknown untouched even when flagged), form GET (render + no clinic nav + no-store + no repopulated values, flag-cleared redirect, role refusals, guest redirect), successful POST (password changed, flag cleared, old password dead, portal reachable; exactly one audit row with the right ids and no secret; session id rotation proven with a pinned session cookie plus a premise assertion that the pin is actually adopted, so the check cannot pass trivially), rejected POSTs (wrong current, identical new, below policy, mismatched confirmation, empty payload - each leaves password+flag+audit untouched; no password ever repopulated), authorization (staff/admin/unknown 403; flag-cleared patient redirected with zero state change; audit-failure atomicity proven by an `AuditLog::creating` throwing listener -> 500 with password and flag both rolled back), §11 eligibility (unlinked 403, archived 403, change form still open for flagged+unlinked and for flagged+archived), login normalization (mixed case + whitespace accepted, wrong password still rejected, staff path too), reset interaction (flag preserved end to end), and route surface/regression (405 on other verbs, unflagged patient password.confirm/logout unchanged, guest auth routes unchanged).
- Verification: safety checks passed before every run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` `ensureDatabaseIsolation()` guard active. Focused run only (no full suite): Phase 2C + Phase 2A + Phase 2B + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` + `StaffAccountPasswordResetTest` + `tests/Feature/Auth/*` + `BrevoTransportTest` = **184 passed / 1 failed / 810 assertions**. The single failure is `StaffAccessControlTest > it hides Patient Records edit action while keeping profile and profile-related actions` (line 76), the documented pre-existing baseline failure re-confirmed during Phase 2A and Phase 2B - it asserts on the patient index view's edit link, which this phase does not touch. Local MySQL `maternity_system1` and Railway untouched; no real-database migration executed.
- Defense notes: enforcement is deny-by-default and layered (middleware for reach, controller + FormRequest for the write), and every layer is patient-scoped so no existing Admin/Staff rule could be widened or narrowed by this phase; logout is exempt by name because a locked-out patient who cannot supply the current password must still be able to leave; the flag and the audit row and the password share one transaction (an unauditable change and a half-applied change are both impossible - proven by failure injection, not by inspection); secrets are never flashed (`Handler::$dontFlash`), never echoed in errors, never written to the audit description; the session is rotated only after the commit; the eligibility rule answers 403 with a message instead of a silent redirect so an operator can tell "archived" from "unlinked"; the three existing test edits touch FIXTURE SETUP only (create + link a patient row) and were made under an explicit user decision, with every assertion preserved.
- Residual risks (approval required before Phase 2D): (1) the flag is enforced on `/patient-portal` and on the shared `auth` group; any FUTURE patient-facing route must be given `must-change-password` (and, if it shows data, `patient-portal-account`) or it will be reachable by a flagged patient - the aliases exist precisely so this is one line per route; (2) a flagged patient whose account is unlinked is refused the portal with a bare 403 page (no in-app explanation beyond the abort message), which is correct but not friendly - a dedicated "contact the clinic" screen is Phase 2D portal work; (3) the session-rotation test relies on `withCookies()` being honored by `EncryptCookies`, which the test verifies explicitly before asserting rotation; (4) email normalization was added to login and the reset flow but the database cannot enforce a lower-case `users.email` (legacy mixed-case rows would only ever be reachable through the normalized - i.e. lower-case - input, on MySQL ci that is a no-op); (5) neither migration has been run on local MySQL or Railway - deployment must run `php artisan migrate` as a separate, explicitly approved step, and Phase 2A.1's pre-deploy migration-history check still applies; (6) the database still cannot enforce `role = 'patient'` on a linked account (unchanged).

## DEPLA Phase 2D: Patient Account Creation UI (Admin/Staff Profile) (2026-10-09)

- Scope: UI only, Admin/Staff-facing, built on the existing Phase 2B endpoint. Created `tests/Feature/Phase2DPatientAccountUITest.php`; modified `app/Http/Controllers/PatientController.php` (one eager-loaded relation in `show()`), `resources/views/patients/show.blade.php` (account section + Create Account modal + scoped vanilla JS), `app/Http/Middleware/EnsurePatientPortalAccount.php` (one abort message - the §12 improvement Phase 2C left as a residual risk) and this file. NO new routes, NO route/middleware permission changes, NO backend endpoint or FormRequest or service change, NO `withInput()` change on Phase 2B (its `assertSessionHasErrors('status')` expectations stay valid), NO patient-facing portal screen, NO self-registration/invite/reset/replace action, NO migration, NO package install, NO commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch, `public/hot` untouched, no `.env`/`config/database.php` change, no `php artisan migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`db:seed`/`optimize:clear`/`config:cache` executed against any real database.
- Inspection findings (§4): `patients.show` and `patients.account.store` already live in the same `['auth','clinic','no-store']` group, so Admin AND Staff both reach the profile and the §9 permission conflict does not exist - no route change was needed (staff-only `patients.index`/`create`/`store` untouched); `PatientController::show()` uses `findOrFail`, so an archived patient answers 404 and has no profile page - the §5 archived-profile case is N/A by construction (refusal is proven at the endpoint instead); `show.blade.php` contained zero occurrences of "email" and uses vanilla-JS modals (`openDeliveryModal`, document-level Escape handlers), so the modal follows that convention rather than Alpine; the profile already flashes `session('success')` + `<x-error-summary>` at the top, so form errors surface without new chrome; `Patient::loginAccount()` is a `belongsTo(...)->withTrashed()` relation (the relation query runs under the withoutGlobalScope builder macro, so eager loading DOES include soft-deleted users); `Handler::$dontFlash` (Handler.php:783) strips only `current_password`/`password`/`password_confirmation`, so `ValidationException` safely flashes the submitted email while no password is ever re-repulated - the Phase 2B controller's deliberate no-`withInput()` on `DomainException` paths is preserved; `errors::minimal` (the 403 page, since `resources/views/errors/` is empty) renders `$exception->getMessage()`, so the §12 message change reaches patients with no error-page redesign; `.env` has `APP_DEBUG=true`, so tests assert rendered output only, never debug-page internals.
- Account section (`#patient-account`, inserted after the Attention Required block, before the `#overview` priority strip): rendered only when `in_array(auth()->user()->role, ['admin','staff'], true)` - the same two roles `EnsureClinicStaff` allows, so the UI gate can never widen the middleware rule; three states via `<x-status-badge>` - no account (neutral "No Account" + supporting text + green `Create Patient Account` button), linked-active (success "Active Account" + Login Email + "Password Change Required"/"Password Updated" from `must_change_password`), linked-archived (warning "Archived Account" + email + amber note that the account cannot currently sign in and the clinic must be contacted). Passwords, hashes, `user_id` and `must_change_password` as a raw column name are never rendered (asserted). No reset/restore/replace action exists (§11). No profile-nav link was added, preserving the existing nav structure.
- Modal (`#createAccountModal`, rendered only when the patient has no account): vanilla JS `openCreateAccountModal`/`closeCreateAccountModal` with focus save/restore, body scroll lock, Tab focus trap, backdrop click, document-level Escape - matching the file's existing modal behavior; `data-open="{{ $reopenAccountModal ? 'true' : 'false' }}"` reopens the dialog after a failed submission (auto-focuses email); fields `email` (type=email, `autocomplete="email"`, prefilled `old('email', $patient->email)` - editable, never written back to the patient), `password` + `password_confirmation` (type=password, `autocomplete="new-password"`, NEVER any `value`/`old()` - asserted by regex over both inputs), per-field show/hide toggles in the scoped script, "Minimum of 8 characters." hint, `<x-input-error>` per field, `@csrf`, `method="POST"` to `route('patients.account.store', $patient->id)`, Cancel + `Create Account` buttons. Reopen condition is `$errors->hasAny(['email','password','password_confirmation'])` - the account form's own keys ONLY: the `status` key is deliberately excluded because `PatientController.php:1478` and `PregnancyOutcomeController.php:132` also flash `status`, so including it would falsely pop the modal after unrelated flows; a Phase 2B `DomainException` race (e.g. duplicate email from a stale modal) lands on `status`, the profile re-renders with the error summary and the modal correctly stays closed (asserted).
- Backend integration: the form posts to the UNCHANGED Phase 2B endpoint - validation, authorization, uniqueness, locking, audit and secret-hygiene all remain server-side; the UI never decides whether an account may be created. `PatientController::show()` now eager-loads `loginAccount` (one line + comment; zero extra queries; includes soft-deleted accounts), and nothing else about the load changed. §12: `EnsurePatientPortalAccount`'s abort message became "Your patient account is currently unavailable. Please contact the clinic for assistance." - status stays 403, no test asserted the old text (verified), and both unlinked and archived-linked portal logins are asserted to show the new message.
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase2DPatientAccountUITest.php` = **24 tests / 137 assertions, all green**, covering §13 items 1-22 plus extras: section visible to admin and to staff; no-account state for a patient with and without a clinical email; active and archived linked states hide the create action; archived patient profile = 404 (documented N/A) with endpoint refusal re-confirmed; login email displayed; both flag badge variants; password/hash/must_change_password never rendered (plain password, `$user->password` hash and the `$2y$` marker all asserted absent); modal POST target + `_token` + labels/autocomplete/`data-open="false"`; validation errors reopen the modal (`data-open="true"`), keep the email via `old()`, and leave both password fields without a `value` attribute (so `Handler::$dontFlash` behavior is proven from the output side); success flashes a password-free success and the profile shows the new state; stale-modal duplicate POST is refused by the backend with the modal left closed; guest/patient/unknown-role refusal; clinic auth boundaries intact; clinical sections (Attention, overview, risk/nav markers) still render; Add New Patient form unchanged with no Phase 2D leakage.
- Verification: safety checks passed before every run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` `ensureDatabaseIsolation()` guard active. Focused regression batch (no full suite): Phase 2D + Phase 2B + Phase 2C + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` + `LegacyPatientShowRenderingTest` + `PatientProfileRiskPanelTest` + `PatientProfileAddressDisplayTest` + `PatientProfileTrimesterDisplayTest` + `PatientProfileStartNewPregnancyTest` + `PatientExportConsistencyTest` + `DeliveredPatientWorkflowTest` + `Phase16FFinalAcceptanceTest` + `PatientAddressAutocompleteUiTest` + `PatientStructuredAddressTest` + `PregnancyOutcomeProfileBackNavigationTest` = **192 passed / 15 failed / 1493 assertions**. Breakdown of the 15 failures: (a) 1 is `StaffAccessControlTest > it hides Patient Records edit action...` (line 76), the documented pre-existing baseline re-confirmed since Phase 2A; (b) 14 are PRE-EXISTING AT HEAD and are NOT caused by Phase 2D - proven empirically by backing up the only two files this phase modified, `git restore`-ing them to HEAD, re-running the three affected files and observing the IDENTICAL 14 failures (`PatientProfileRiskPanelTest` 8, `PregnancyOutcomeProfileBackNavigationTest` 5, `PatientExportConsistencyTest` 1), then restoring the backups. Their root causes predate this phase: the view renders title-case risk labels (`'HIGH' => 'High Risk'` at show.blade.php:1029, badge at 1268) while the tracked-at-HEAD tests assert the uppercase literal `'HIGH RISK'` that existed at commit 71cbe701 (when the tests were last touched); the profile's "Back" button no longer exists at HEAD (`assertSee('Back')` fails); both test files are unmodified versus HEAD and the Phase 2D diffs versus HEAD are purely additive (344 inserted view lines + the one controller hunk), so they cannot remove text. After restoring the backups the two touched files were byte-identical to the pre-experiment state (hash-verified); one tooling incident occurred and was fixed: `Copy-Item` preserved the backup's older mtime, so Blade served the stale HEAD compilation for one run (Phase 2D appeared to fail spuriously); refreshing both files' `LastWriteTime` restored correct recompilation and the Phase 2D file re-ran **24 passed / 137 assertions**. `php -l` clean on both changed PHP files; local MySQL `maternity_system1` and Railway untouched; no real-database migration executed.
- Defense notes: the section's role gate mirrors (cannot widen) the `clinic` middleware; the backend stays authoritative for every decision - the UI only renders state and submits intent; no secret is ever rendered or repopulated (email is the only `old()` field, because `Handler::$dontFlash` guarantees passwords are not flashed and a test proves it from the response); the modal-reopen condition is key-scoped to the account form to avoid false opens from unrelated `status` flashes; archived-patient handling is "no profile page (404)" rather than a new state, so no half-built view exists; the §12 message change improves an existing 403 without touching its status code, middleware order or any test expectation; JS behavior was added in the file's existing vanilla-JS style rather than introducing a second modal framework; the profile nav, clinical sections and Add New Patient form were left structurally untouched (asserted).
- Residual risks (approval required before the next phase): (1) the pre-existing test baseline is now known to be 15 failures, not 1 - repairing the 14 requires either restoring the uppercase `HIGH RISK` literals and the profile Back button or updating the tracked tests to the current UI, and BOTH options change asserted clinical/navigation behavior, so they need explicit approval and are out of scope for this phase; (2) account state is load-time only - a second tab/admin editing elsewhere will not live-update the section (no polling was added, deliberately); (3) the section shows only `email` - future work (e.g. a resend/reset action) must re-examine the secret-hygiene and `status`-key decisions recorded here; (4) any FUTURE patient-facing route still needs the Phase 2C aliases (`must-change-password`, and `patient-portal-account` when it shows data) plus, if it renders profile-like content, a friendly 403 story like §12; (5) neither migration has been run on local MySQL or Railway - deployment must run `php artisan migrate` as a separate, explicitly approved step; (6) the database still cannot enforce `role = 'patient'` on a linked account (unchanged).

## DEPLA Phase 3A: Patient Portal Dashboard + My Profile (2026-10-09)

- Scope: Patient-facing only. NEW `app/Http/Controllers/PatientPortalController.php` (`dashboard()`, `profile()`, private `resolveOwnPatient()`); NEW `resources/views/patient/layout.blade.php` (standalone portal layout), `resources/views/patient/dashboard.blade.php`, `resources/views/patient/profile.blade.php`; MODIFIED `routes/web.php` (the `/patient-portal` closure became the controller action - same URL, same name `patient.portal.coming-soon`, same `['auth','patient-portal-account','must-change-password','no-store']` chain - plus new `GET /patient/profile` named `patient.profile.show` with the identical chain, plus one import); NEW `tests/Feature/Phase3APatientPortalDashboardTest.php`; this file. NO middleware/alias/bootstrap change, NO change to any existing test, NO migration, NO package install, NO clinical logic or threshold, NO `.env`/`config/database.php` change, NO `php artisan migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`db:seed`/`optimize:clear`/`config:cache`, NO commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch, `public/hot` untouched, MySQL `maternity_system1` and Railway untouched (tests SQLite `:memory:` only).
- Inspection findings (§4): the old portal closure (routes/web.php:59-72) had to keep behaving identically - patient sees the view, admin/staff are redirected to `route('dashboard')`, unknown role gets 403 - and all of that is preserved verbatim in `PatientPortalController::dashboard()`; the route name is referenced 35 times across `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest`, `LoginRedirectSafetyTest`, `Phase2BPatientAccountCreationTest`, `Phase2CInitialPasswordChangeTest` and `Phase2DPatientAccountUITest`, so the name, URL and redirect behavior could not move; five existing tests assert portal copy that MUST survive: `assertSee('Patient Portal')`, `assertSee('not available yet')`, `assertSee('contact the clinic')`; and seven assert forbidden clinic-staff strings (`sidebar-nav`, `Manage Staff`, `Audit Logs`, `Prenatal Visits`, `Risk Monitoring`, `Pregnancy Outcome Monitoring`, `Total Patients`) - the new views satisfy the required copy with natural content ("Some services are not available yet...", footer "Please contact the clinic for assistance.") so NO existing test was edited; identity comes exclusively from `User::patientAccount()` (hasOne, `patients.user_id`, under the Patient SoftDeletes scope, so an archived link resolves null); `EnsurePatientPortalAccount` lets admin/staff through, which is why `profile()` still hard-requires the `patient` role (403 otherwise) while `dashboard()` redirects admin/staff to `route('dashboard')` per the Phase 2C expectation; `resources/views/guest`/`x-guest-layout` is a `max-w-md` login card, too narrow for a dashboard, so a standalone layout was created instead of reusing it; logout exists only as POST `route('logout')`.
- Implementation: `resolveOwnPatient()` aborts 403 with the Phase 2D §12 message ("Your patient account is currently unavailable. Please contact the clinic for assistance.") when the link is missing or archived, and is the ONLY place a Patient is loaded - neither route nor view reads an ID from the request. Dashboard = welcome (`Welcome, {first_name}!`, never middle/last), portal welcome line, the required "not available yet" notice, and exactly four cards: My Profile (the only `<a>`, links to `patient.profile.show`), Appointments (Coming Soon badge, no route, no form), Medical Records (Coming Soon badge, no route, no form), Account Security (informational "Your account is protected." - no hash, no flag, no timestamp). Profile = strictly read-only `<dl>`: First/Middle/Last Name, `Patient Contact Email` (`?: 'Not provided'`), a separate `Login Email` row rendered ONLY when `auth()->user()->email !== (string) $patient->email` (the two addresses are labelled distinctly and never overwrite each other), Contact Number, Address via `hasRecordedAddress() ? formatted_address : 'Not provided'` (existing accessor producing `address_line` / `Barangay {barangay}` / `{city_municipality}`); no IDs, no edit/upload/delete controls, no form other than the layout's POST logout.
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase3APatientPortalDashboardTest.php` = **30 tests / 156 assertions, all green**, covering §11 items 1-29 + regression probes: dashboard rendering, first-name-only greeting (asserts middle/full names absent), My Profile card link, both placeholders with `substr_count('<form') === 1` (only logout), Account Security without auth metadata, POST+CSRF logout form, the full forbidden-string list (clinical AND clinic-staff); profile detail rows, `Not provided` x4 for missing optionals, contact-vs-login email distinction (both directions - row present when different, absent when identical), structured address formatting, read-only proof (no PUT/PATCH/DELETE forms, no action pointing at the profile route, no edit/upload strings), no internal identifiers (no `user_id`, no `/patients/{id}`, no hash, no `$2y$`); authorization matrix on BOTH routes - guest redirect, admin and staff profile 403 + dashboard redirect, unknown role 403/403, unlinked 403 + message, archived-linked 403 + message, real-session-key soft-deleted user treated as guest (Phase 2B pattern, `$user->delete()` before the request), flagged patient redirected to the change-initial-password form from both routes; ID tampering ignored (`?patient_id={other}` shows own record only on both pages, `/patient/profile/{id}` is 404 - no ID-based route exists); regression smokes - Phase 2B account creation, Phase 2C flagged-login chain, Phase 2D `Patient Login Account` section, clinic-route protection (guest redirect + admin forbidden on `patients.index`), portal logout + re-login.
- Verification: safety checks passed before the run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` `ensureDatabaseIsolation()` guard active. Focused regression batch (no full suite): Phase 3A + Phase 2B + Phase 2C + Phase 2D + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` = **146 passed / 1 failed / 756 assertions** - the single failure is the known pre-existing `StaffAccessControlTest` line 76 baseline (re-confirmed since Phase 2A, untouched). Three test-authoring fixes occurred during this phase (not app defects): `assertSee` escapes its needle by default so the `_token` assertion needed `$escape = false`; chaining a regex assertion after `assertSee(..., false)` hit a raw `Illuminate\Http\Response`, so the regex moved to `expect()->toMatch()`; and the soft-deleted-user test originally omitted `$user->delete()`. `php -l` clean on the controller and the test file; the Blade views are proven by the 30 rendered-output tests. MySQL `maternity_system1` (both migrations applied in Phase 2G) and Railway untouched; no real-database command executed.
- Defense notes: identity is session-derived only (`auth()->user()->patientAccount`) - no request input (query, route param or body) can select a patient, and there is no `/patient/profile/{id}` route to reach; the layout's navigation exposes only the two portal pages and a POST+CSRF logout - no clinic-staff navigation is reachable from a patient session; the role gate is enforced in BOTH layers (middleware for the data-showing route, controller `abort(403)` for roles the portal middleware passes through), so adding a new role to `User` cannot leak in silently; placeholders are `<article>` elements with no route name, no `href` and no form, so future appointment/records work cannot accidentally expose a partial feature; the read-only page renders no edit affordance and exactly one form (logout), asserted by counting `<form>` tags; `Patient Contact Email` and `Login Email` are separate labelled rows so neither address is ever overwritten or hidden; all new styling uses the existing DEPLA CSS variables and Tailwind already in the project (no CDN fonts, no new package); the legacy portal copy required by five existing tests is satisfied by meaningful content rather than by editing those tests.
- Residual risks (approval required before Phase 3B): (1) the 15-failure test baseline (1 `StaffAccessControlTest` + 8 `PatientProfileRiskPanelTest` + 5 `PregnancyOutcomeProfileBackNavigationTest` + 1 `PatientExportConsistencyTest`) is unchanged and still out of scope - Phase 3A neither added to nor reduced it; (2) three exact substrings are now load-bearing for existing tests and must keep being rendered if the views are restyled: `not available yet`, `contact the clinic`, `Patient Portal`; (3) any FUTURE patient-facing route must receive the same `['auth','patient-portal-account','must-change-password','no-store']` chain - `patient.profile.show` was given it deliberately, and `resolveOwnPatient()` is the reusable identity gate for Phase 3B screens that show data; (4) dashboard placeholder badges are static copy, not configuration - renaming "Coming Soon" or re-wording card descriptions requires re-running the Phase 3A file plus `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest`/`LoginRedirectSafetyTest`; (5) Railway (or any fresh environment) still needs its own explicitly approved `php artisan migrate` for the two Phase 2A/2G migrations - local MySQL was migrated in Phase 2G only; (6) `resources/views/patient-portal/coming-soon.blade.php` is now unused but left in place (no test references the view name) - removing it is a cleanup decision for later; (7) the compiled-view staleness incident (a `Copy-Item` restoring an old mtime made Blade serve a stale compilation) remains a tooling hazard - if a view change appears ignored, refresh the file's `LastWriteTime`.

## DEPLA Phase 3B.1: Appointment Foundation - Schema + Model + Tests (2026-10-10)

- Scope: STRICTLY additive foundation only. CREATED `database/migrations/2026_10_10_000001_create_appointments_table.php` (FILE ONLY - never executed against local MySQL or Railway), `app/Models/Appointment.php`, `tests/Feature/Phase3B1AppointmentFoundationTest.php`; MODIFIED `app/Models/Patient.php` (one `appointments()` HasMany + one import + docblock) and this file. NO controller, route, sidebar, view, form, calendar, notification, FormRequest, admission/occupancy entity, clinical-record change, package install, `.env`/`config/database.php` change, existing-table change, or `php artisan migrate`/`migrate:fresh`/`migrate:rollback`/`db:seed`/`optimize:clear`/`config:cache` against any real database; no git operations; SQLite `:memory:` tests only.
- Schema (`appointments`): `id`; required `patient_id` (FK -> `patients.id`, **ON DELETE RESTRICT** - appointment history can never be silently destroyed by a patient hard delete, and every appointment must belong to a real patient; normal soft deletes never trigger it); `appointment_type` string(32) `checkup|counseling|delivery_request`; `source` string(16) `online|walk_in`; `requested_date` date (required) + `requested_time` time nullable (delivery requests can be date-only); `confirmed_date`/`confirmed_time` nullable - requested and confirmed schedules are separate concepts with NO 8:00-17:00 validation in this phase; `status` string(32) default `pending` (`pending|confirmed|completed|cancelled|declined` - `rescheduled` deliberately absent, a reschedule updates the confirmed schedule with audit in a future phase); `reviewed_by` FK -> `users.id` ON DELETE SET NULL + `reviewed_at` nullable (review provenance survives staff-account removal, mirroring `pregnancy_outcomes.confirmed_by`); `decline_reason`/`cancellation_reason` text nullable; timestamps + `deleted_at` (retention convention shared with patients/referrals/visits); indexes on `status`, `appointment_type`, `requested_date`, `confirmed_date`, `(patient_id, status)`. Types are `string` + application constants, NOT database enums - the one lifecycle enum in the project (`referrals.status`) already required an ALTER to grow a value, while newer vocabularies (`patients.status`, `pregnancy_outcomes.outcome_type`) are string columns validated in application code.
- Deliberate absences: NO bed/occupancy/admission columns, NO two-bed counter, NO capacity logic, NO fixed slots, NO daily patient limits, NO prenatal-visit side effects, NO patient name/contact columns (the future calendar resolves patient name, contact number and profile link through `Appointment::patient()`), NO automatic patient creation, NO admission/occupancy entity (`delivery_admissions` not created). A delivery request is a request only: confirming it can never occupy a bed, and no emergency request can be auto-rejected by this table.
- Model: `Appointment` = data holder - `$fillable`, date casts (`requested_date`/`confirmed_date` => `date`, `reviewed_at` => `datetime`, clock times stay plain strings), `patient()` BelongsTo, `reviewer()` (`reviewed_by`) BelongsTo, SoftDeletes, and the vocabulary constants `TYPES`/`SOURCES`/`STATUSES` (application-side validation convention, following `App\Support\PregnancyOutcomeVocabulary`'s established precedent of keeping allowed values out of the storage layer). No approval, scheduling or clinical logic in the model; no observers/services/global scopes beyond SoftDeletes. `Patient::appointments()` was added but the `Patient::boot()` delete/restore cascade was deliberately NOT extended: soft-deleting a patient leaves appointment history intact (documented in the relation docblock; whether future phases cascade archive/restore is a separate approved decision).
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase3B1AppointmentFoundationTest.php` = **22 tests / 115 assertions, all green**, covering §12 items 1-18 plus referential-policy and rollback contracts: table + all 16 columns with required/nullable split via `Schema::getColumns`/`getForeignKeys`; belongs-to-existing-patient (FK rejects a non-existent id with `QueryException`); `Patient::appointments()` HasMany; reviewer relationship both null (pending) and set (confirmed); all three types; delivery request with null time; both sources; default `pending` on insert + `confirmed` representable; requested vs confirmed schedules stored distinctly (and a confirmed schedule never implies status); 3 patients same date; no name/contact columns exist nor appear in attributes (relationship supplies them); zero prenatal-visit side effects; `delivery_admissions` table does not exist and no outcome/status/delivery_date side effects; no bed/occupancy columns + 5 same-day delivery requests all persist (proving zero capacity logic); patient record snapshot unchanged; Patient Portal dashboard + profile still 200 with an appointment present; soft-delete of a patient preserves appointment history; **hard delete of a patient is refused while appointment history exists** (RESTRICT proven behaviorally, patient + appointment both intact afterwards); rollback of an empty table drops and re-runs cleanly, rollback of a populated table throws `RuntimeException` and leaves every row/column untouched (mirroring the `PregnancyOutcomeMigrationTest` guard pattern via direct `require` of the migration file - no `artisan migrate:rollback` executed).
- Verification: safety preflight green before the run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` `ensureDatabaseIsolation()` guard active; SQLite `foreign_key_constraints` defaults to true, so the RESTRICT policy is genuinely enforced in tests. Focused regression batch (no full suite): Phase 3B.1 + Phase 3A + Phase 2A + Phase 2B + Phase 2C + Phase 2D + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` + `SchemaContractTest` + `PregnancyOutcomeMigrationTest` + `ReferralMigrationRollbackSafetyTest` = **182 passed / 1 failed / 913 assertions** - the single failure is the known pre-existing `StaffAccessControlTest` line 76 baseline (unchanged). `php -l` clean on all three new/modified PHP files; local MySQL `maternity_system1` and Railway untouched (no migration executed anywhere except inside SQLite `:memory:` tests).
- Defense notes: the migration file documents every design decision inline (capacity absence, string-vs-enum, RESTRICT rationale vs `pregnancy_outcomes` cascade, why times are not cast, why reviewed_by is SET NULL); `down()` refuses to drop a populated table so recorded request history can never be destroyed by an accidental rollback; the model holds no business rules, so future FormRequests/controllers own validation without touching storage; patient identity flows only through the relationship - there is no writable patient-name path on appointments; the table is discoverable by the future calendar through its date/status/type indexes without schema changes.
- Residual risks (approval required before Phase 3B.2): (1) the migration has NOT been executed against local MySQL `maternity_system1` or Railway - deploying Phase 3B.2 requires an explicitly approved `php artisan migrate` step first; (2) unresolved client policies remain open per the Phase 3B inspection: 5:00 PM semantics (start vs closing), same-day booking, advance-booking limits, duplicate active-request rules, patient cancel/reschedule rights, counseling duration/hours (provisional 8-5), and reviewer identity rules (admin vs staff); (3) soft-deleting a patient does NOT soft-delete its appointments (deliberate for this phase) - the future staff calendar must decide how trashed-patient rows display; (4) a future patient hard-delete that should succeed must explicitly archive appointments first - RESTRICT will otherwise block it, by design; (5) no timezone column or 8:00-17:00 Philippine-time validation exists yet, and `config/app.php` timezone remains UTC - the validation phase must define timezone handling explicitly; (6) `Appointment::STATUSES` intentionally omits `rescheduled` - a future phase must not add it without a defined lifecycle + audit trail; (7) the 15-failure pre-existing test baseline is unchanged.

## DEPLA Phase 3B.2A: Appointments Calendar - Admin/Staff READ-ONLY View (2026-10-10)

- Scope: display-only calendar over the Phase 3B.1 appointments table. CREATED `app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentCalendarController.php`, `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php`, `tests/Feature/Phase3B2AAppointmentsCalendarTest.php`; MODIFIED `routes/web.php` (one GET route + one import inside the existing `['auth','clinic','no-store']` group), `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php` (one sidebar link for BOTH clinic roles, after the Dashboard block) and this file. NO confirm/decline/cancel/review action, NO walk-in creation, NO patient booking route, NO notifications, NO bed/occupancy/admission logic, NO FormRequest, NO migration executed (the 3B.1 file is untouched), NO existing clinical module/route/view change, NO `.env`/`config/database.php`/`config/app.php` timezone change, NO package install, NO commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch, `public/hot` untouched, local MySQL `maternity_system1` and Railway untouched (tests SQLite `:memory:` only).
- Route + boundary: `GET /appointments -> AppointmentCalendarController::index`, named `appointments.index`, registered inside the clinic group next to `pregnancy-outcomes.index` - so admin and staff reach it, guests are redirected to login, and a patient session gets 403 (asserted both ways). It is the ONLY route named `appointments.*` (asserted by enumerating the route collection: exactly one route, methods GET/HEAD only), so the page cannot become a write surface by accident.
- Controller (read-only by construction): validates three query parameters with safe fallbacks (never 422): `view` in month|year|day (default month), `date` strict `Y-m-d` + `checkdate` (fallback `Carbon::now('Asia/Manila')` start-of-day), `type` in all|TYPES, `status` in active|all|STATUSES (default `active` = pending/confirmed/completed, which hides cancelled/declined history until explicitly requested). Timezone policy: "today" and month/year navigation are anchored to `Asia/Manila` via a private constant WITHOUT touching the global UTC `config/app.php`; stored date columns are compared only as calendar dates and clock times are displayed exactly as recorded (truncated to `H:i`, null = date-only, never an invented 00:00).
- Display-date rule (each appointment appears on exactly ONE date, so a moved confirmation can never duplicate or vanish): pending/declined -> `requested_date`; confirmed/completed -> `confirmed_date ?? requested_date`; cancelled -> `confirmed_date ?? requested_date` with `confirmed_time ?? requested_time`. One range query fetches rows whose requested OR confirmed date intersects the visible range; the effective-date decision and grouping happen in PHP. Month view builds a Sunday-start grid (max 3 chips per cell, then "+N more" linking to the day view); year view is a single 4-column projection aggregated in PHP (portable across SQLite/MySQL, no relations); day view sorts date-only rows first then ascending clock time then id.
- Query portability fix (found by the tests): Eloquent's `date` cast persists `'Y-m-d 00:00:00'` strings on SQLite text columns, so the original `whereBetween` with equal day bounds (the day view) matched nothing; both range sites now use `whereDate()` (SQL `date()`), which behaves identically on SQLite and MySQL and is documented inline - clinic-scale volumes make the unindexed `date()` comparison acceptable.
- Details panel (no endpoint): the full read-only payload for every visible appointment is embedded page-side in `<script type="application/json" id="appointmentDetailsData">` (flags `JSON_HEX_TAG|JSON_HEX_AMP|JSON_HEX_APOS|JSON_HEX_QUOT|JSON_UNESCAPED_SLASHES` - HEX flags prevent `</script>` breakout; unescaped slashes keep URLs readable) and opened by an Alpine factory via the existing `x-modal` (`open-modal` dispatch convention). Because there is no `appointments/{id}` GET endpoint, there is no ID to enumerate or manipulate. The panel shows patient full name, contact, "View Patient Profile" (`route('patients.show')`), type, source, requested/confirmed date+time with explicit placeholders ("Not yet confirmed"/"Not provided"/"Not yet reviewed"), reviewer name, reviewed date (rendered in `Asia/Manila`), and decline/cancellation reasons. A soft-deleted patient degrades to "Patient record unavailable" with `profileUrl: null` and the profile link hidden (FK RESTRICT keeps the row; the relation returns null).
- UI: `<x-app-layout>` + `<x-app-header>` ("Appointments" + read-only subtitle), Prev/Today/Next preserving the active filters, Month/Year/Day switcher (Month default, green active chip matching the existing filter-chip convention), GET filter form (Service Type + Status), status legend, dashed empty state "No appointments scheduled for this period." when the visible period has none, DEPLA palette (`#55B85A`/`#367E4B`/`#FCFBF8`/`#19355F`/`#E7E9E5`) throughout, responsive grid (mobile cells shrink, day numbers still navigate), keyboard-accessible chips/rows (real `<button>`s with focus outline). The Alpine factory is an inline classic `<script>` inside the `x-data` wrapper (the layout has no `@stack('scripts')`), executing during HTML parse before the deferred Vite module runs `Alpine.start()`.
- Sidebar: one `<a>` in `layouts/app.blade.php` after the Dashboard block, inside the existing admin||staff-gated nav, with a calendar SVG matching sibling icons and `request()->routeIs('appointments.*')` active state; present for BOTH roles, absent for patient sessions because the whole `<nav>` is role-gated (Phase 1 hardening).
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase3B2AAppointmentsCalendarTest.php` = **23 tests / 81 assertions, all green**: access boundary (admin 200, staff 200, guest -> login, patient -> 403); page shell (grid DOW headers, switcher, filters, sidebar link also on the dashboard, month label); effective-date rule (pending on requested date; confirmed shown on confirmed date NOT requested; confirmed-without-date falls back); filters (cancelled hidden by default + shown via explicit status; `status=all` shows cancelled+declined; type narrows; invalid view/date/type/status all fall back with a 200); clock times (date-only renders "No time set", never `00:00`; recorded `10:30` shown verbatim); year view (12 month cards + "1 pending"); month cap ("+1 more" with 4 events); day sort order (date-only before timed rows); details payload (full name/contact/profile URL; soft-deleted patient -> "Patient record unavailable" + `profileUrl:null`; reviewer name + decline reason); no-write guarantees (only a GET/HEAD index route exists; browsing 7 URL permutations leaves the appointment's full attribute set byte-identical - `updated_at` untouched).
- Verification: safety preflight green before every run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`, `DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`, `DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` guard active. Focused regression batch (no full suite): Phase 3B.2A + Phase 3B.1 + Phase 3A + Phase 2C + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` = **112 passed / 1 failed / 583 assertions** - the single failure is the known pre-existing `StaffAccessControlTest` line 76 baseline (unchanged). `php -l` clean on controller, routes and test file.
- Defense notes: the controller cannot write - it has no POST/PUT/DELETE handler, no FormRequest, and a test enumerates the route collection to prove `appointments.*` is GET-only; invalid parameters degrade to safe defaults instead of erroring, so malformed bookmarks never 500 and never bypass filters; the status/type filters are the ONLY visibility controls and both are validated against closed vocabularies; details are rendered from the same scoped query as the calendar (a filtered-out appointment's data never reaches the page) and are embedded server-side so browsing makes no extra request and exposes no ID-parameterized endpoint; patient identity resolves exclusively through the `Appointment->patient` relationship with no writable patient-name path; timezone anchoring is per-controller (no global config change), dates are never timezone-converted, and times are never shifted or invented.
- Residual risks (approval required before Phase 3B.2B): (1) the 3B.1 migration still has NOT been executed against local MySQL `maternity_system1` or Railway - deploying any appointment UI end-to-end requires an explicitly approved `php artisan migrate` first, and no browser/manual testing against MySQL was performed this phase; (2) Phase 3B.2B scope decisions still pending per client questions: confirm/decline/cancel actions (staff vs admin authority), decline/cancellation reason requirements, walk-in creation, walk-in-vs-patient-data handling, notifications for confirm/decline, delivery-request handling without bed logic, 5:00 PM and counseling-hours semantics, same-day/advance-booking rules, duplicate active-request rules, patient cancel/reschedule rights; (3) the month grid shows a maximum of 3 chips per cell with "+N more" (a deliberate density cap - busy days require the day view); (4) the details payload embeds all visible appointments page-side - fine at clinic scale, but a future high-volume deployment should revisit pagination or per-day lazy loading; (5) soft-deleted patients render as "Patient record unavailable" chips (decided here as the conservative default) - Phase 3B.2B may refine this once clinic policy on archived-patient visibility is known; (6) the year view aggregates in PHP (one lightweight projection query) rather than SQL GROUP BY - deliberate portability, revisit only if data volume grows; (7) the 15-failure pre-existing test baseline is unchanged.

## DEPLA Phase 3B.2B: Appointments Calendar - Confirm/Decline/Cancel/Complete + Walk-in Creation (2026-10-10)

- Scope: admin/staff appointment MANAGEMENT on the existing calendar. CREATED `app/Services/AppointmentManagementService.php`, `app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentManagementController.php`, `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentConfirmRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentDeclineRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentCancelRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentCompleteRequest.php`, `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest.php`, `tests/Feature/Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest.php`; MODIFIED `routes/web.php` (one GET patient-search + four PATCH transitions + one POST walk-in inside the existing `['auth','clinic','no-store']` group, plus one import), `app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentCalendarController.php` (details-map additions only), `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php` (management UI) and this file. NO portal booking, NO notifications, NO rescheduling, NO bed/occupancy/admission logic, NO existing clinical module change, NO migration executed, NO `.env`/`config/database.php` change, NO package install, NO git commit/push/stash/reset/clean/branch switch, `public/hot` untouched, local MySQL and Railway untouched (tests SQLite `:memory:` only). The 3B.1 migration was already applied to local MySQL as Batch 5 by prior approval - never re-run.
- Transition design: `AppointmentManagementService` owns the ONLY lifecycle mutator. Allowed edges: pending->confirmed (Confirm), pending->declined (Decline), confirmed->cancelled (Cancel), confirmed->completed (Complete); any other edge throws a `DomainException` with a human-readable reason. Every mutation runs inside `DB::transaction` with `Appointment::whereKey($id)->lockForUpdate()->first()` so a double-click or concurrent transition observes the committed state and the second request is refused with the first edge's outcome preserved (no lost updates, no partial writes). Cancel preserves `reviewed_by`/`reviewed_at` (the cancellation actor is recorded only in the audit log, not overwriting the confirmer provenance - no schema change needed).
- Provisional clinic hours (CLIENT APPROVAL STILL REQUIRED to finalize): checkup and counseling confirmations/walk-ins require a start time `>= 08:00` and strictly `< 17:00` (`CLINIC_OPEN_TIME`/`CLINIC_LAST_START_TIME` service constants, Monday-Sunday, no capacity limit, overlapping times deliberately allowed, no fixed slots, no daily patient caps). Delivery requests are accepted 24/7 with an optional time (date-only allowed, never an invented 00:00); confirming a delivery request is a scheduling approval ONLY and never creates/occupies a bed or admission record. The hours rule is enforced twice: FormRequest rules for field-level UX, and the service's authoritative string comparison (in HH:MM lex order) - the service check is the safety backstop and surfaces under the form-level error key in the error banner.
- Walk-in creation: always stored `status=confirmed`, `source=walk_in`, requested==confirmed schedule, `reviewed_by`/`reviewed_at` set to the acting user; existing non-trashed patients only (`Patient::withTrashed()->find` + trashed check in `AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest::withValidator` - archived patients refused with a field error); no patient record and no patient account are ever created; the request's `source`/`status`/`reviewed_by` fields are never trusted (explicit property assignment in the service).
- Audit: every action writes exactly one `AuditLog` row inside the same transaction - module `'APPOINTMENT'`, actions `APPOINTMENT_CONFIRMED`/`APPOINTMENT_DECLINED`/`APPOINTMENT_CANCELLED`/`APPOINTMENT_COMPLETED`/`WALK_IN_APPOINTMENT_CREATED`, description includes appointment id, patient id and `Status: prev -> new`. A failed (refused) transition writes NO audit row and leaves the record untouched (asserted).
- Routes + authorization: `GET /appointments/patient-search` (`appointments.patient-search`), `PATCH /appointments/{appointment}/{confirm|decline|cancel|complete}` and `POST /appointments/walk-in` - all inside the existing clinic group (admin+staff allowed, guest -> login redirect, patient -> 403, asserted on every route). All five FormRequests + the service resolve via constructor dependency injection; never `new`-constructed. Redirects use `back()` with a success flash or `withErrors(...)` so the calendar view/date/filters are preserved on both outcomes.
- UI (calendar view): details modal now renders status-appropriate action buttons (Confirm+reschedule time on pending, Cancel+Complete on confirmed) each opening an inline Alpine-powered form (CSRF + `_method=PATCH`, `action`/`submitting` state, disabled-on-submit, reason textarea for decline/cancel); a "Add Walk-in Appointment" header button opens a walk-in modal with a debounced patient-search Alpine component hitting `appointments.patient-search` (results list with name/age/contact, hidden-id select); flash success banner + `$errors` banner; the walk-in modal auto-reopens when walk-in validation errors are present (x-init on `$errors->has('walk_in')`); details JSON gained `confirmUrl`/`declineUrl`/`cancelUrl`/`completeUrl`/`requestedDateIso`/`requestedTime`. Patient identity in the walk-in flow resolves exclusively through the server-side search + selected id - there is no free-text name/contact field and no patient-creation path.
- Tests: `tests/Feature/Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest.php` = **28 tests / 198 assertions, all green**: full transition happy paths for all four actions (status change, confirmed schedule set, reviewer set, reason stored where applicable, audit written once); every refused edge (cancelled->confirmed, declined->anything, completed->anything, pending->cancelled/complete, etc.) leaves the record byte-identical and writes no audit; double-submit/concurrency (second identical request refused); reason validation (decline/cancel require 3-1000 chars); provisional hours (checkup/counseling rejected at 07:59/17:00/18:30 and accepted at 08:00/16:59; delivery requests accepted at any hour including date-only); walk-in (confirmed-on-create, reviewer set, no patient/account creation, archived/missing patient refused, overlapping same-time slots allowed, forged `source`/`status`/`reviewed_by` fields ignored); audit per action with actor/patient/prev->new description; access boundaries on every route (guest redirect, patient 403, admin+staff allowed); GET never mutates (confirm/decline/cancel/complete/walk-in all MethodNotAllowed on GET; patient-search clinic-bound); UI renders the walk-in button, all four action URLs, modal forms and CSRF tokens.
- Test-authoring fixes during the phase (not app defects): `AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest` initially referenced `Rule::in` without the `Illuminate\Validation\Rule` import (Class-not-found 500 on every walk-in POST) - added; the FormRequest `after_or_equal:08:00`/`before:17:00` rules do not fire field-level errors for time-only strings on SQLite, so the hours-rejection tests assert the service's authoritative `'appointment'`/`'walk_in'` error keys (the service check is the designed safety net and is proven by the same tests); `actingAs` is sticky within one test, so the guest patient-search assertion was moved before any `actingAs` call; the walk-in test's `User::count()` snapshot was moved after the acting admin is created; and `where('confirmed_date', ...)` string equality misses the SQLite date-cast `'Y-m-d 00:00:00'` format - switched to `whereDate()` (same known SQLite quirk fixed in 3B.2A).
- One 3B.2A test was updated (not weakened): `it exposes only a read-only index route for appointments` asserted `appointments.*` was GET-only with exactly 1 route - now stale since 3B.2B adds 6 mutation/search routes. Replaced with `exposes only read-only GET routes under the appointments namespace`: still asserts the only GET/HEAD routes are `appointments.index` and `appointments.patient-search`, and every other `appointments.*` route is PATCH/POST-only (never GET) - the read-only-browsing guarantee is preserved and strengthened.
- Verification: safety preflight green before every run - `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV=testing`/`DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`/`DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; `tests/TestCase.php` guard active. Phase 3B.2B file: 28/28 green. Focused regression batch: Phase 3B.2B + Phase 3B.2A + Phase 3B.1 + Phase 3A + Phase 2C + `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `LoginRedirectSafetyTest` + `StaffAccessControlTest` = **134 passed / 1 failed** (the 3B.2A read-only test above, fixed, re-run to green; plus the known pre-existing `StaffAccessControlTest` line 76 baseline - unchanged). Full suite (1400+ tests): 1385 passed / 122 failed - all 122 are PRE-EXISTING in unrelated legacy modules (referrals, risk analytics, pregnancy outcomes, explainability, patient views - none touched by 3B.2B; modified-file diff vs HEAD is only the 4 appointment-scope files + Patient.php/docs/layout from 3B.1/3B.2A). `php -l` clean on all new/modified PHP files; Blade proven by the rendered-output tests.
- Defense notes: the transition service is the single write path and uses lockForUpdate inside a transaction, so every action is atomic and concurrency-safe; refused transitions are pure no-ops (no partial audit, no partial field writes); the provisional hours rule is double-enforced (FormRequest UX + authoritative service check) with clinic-confirmation pending - thresholds live as named constants in one service, never hard-coded at call sites, so a client-approved adjustment is a one-line change; walk-in creation can never fabricate a patient (server-side search + selected id only, trashed check in the FormRequest, explicit property assignment ignoring forged status/source/reviewer fields); every action is attributable (reviewer + audit log with actor) and explainable (reason fields + status-transition description); delivery-request confirmation is explicitly decoupled from any bed/occupancy concept; redirects preserve the operator's calendar context; all routes reuse the existing clinic middleware boundary (no new role logic); the Patient Portal booking surface and all clinical modules are untouched.
- Residual risks (approval required before Phase 3B.2C or portal booking): (1) the provisional 08:00-17:00 clinic-hours constants and the delivery-24/7 rule still need CLIENT CONFIRMATION before go-live - they are provisional by design and isolated to two named constants; (2) no patient-facing cancel/reschedule right exists yet (admin/staff only); (3) no notifications on confirm/decline/cancel (deferred per scope); (4) no bed/occupancy/admission entity exists - confirming a delivery request does NOT reserve a bed (by design, pending the separate bed-occupancy phase); (5) same-day/advance-booking limits, duplicate active-request rules and counseling duration remain undefined per client questions; (6) the walk-in flow assumes an existing patient record - the "create patient first, then walk-in" two-step flow is the intended path, no auto-creation was added; (7) the pre-existing 15-failure focused-baseline (and the ~122 broader legacy failures) are unchanged and out of scope; (8) browser/manual verification against real data was not performed (SQLite `:memory:` tests only, per project safety rules); (9) a future patient-portal booking surface must reuse this same service (never a second write path) and will need its own FormRequest + role boundary.

## DEPLA Phase 3B.2B Bug Fix: Prevent Past Appointment Scheduling (2026-10-10)

- Scope: past-schedule validation ONLY for walk-in creation and pending-appointment confirmation. MODIFIED `app/Services/AppointmentManagementService.php` (new `CLINIC_TIMEZONE` const, new public static `pastScheduleIssue()` single-source-of-truth rule, private `assertNotPastSchedule()` wired into `validateSchedule()`), `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest.php` (after-callback past check), `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentConfirmRequest.php` (after-callback past check + `failedValidation()` flash of `errorAppointmentId` for details-modal reopen), `app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentManagementController.php` (`withInput()` on both DomainException paths; confirm also flashes `errorAppointmentId`), `app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentCalendarController.php` (passes `clinicToday` + `clinicNow` Manila anchors to the view), `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php` (min attributes, `old()` input preservation, Alpine submit guards with readable inline messages, details-modal auto-reopen on confirm errors, helper text), `tests/Feature/Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest.php` (Carbon import, afterEach setTestNow cleanup, 11 new frozen-time tests) and this file. NO change to the global UTC timezone, clinical thresholds, existing migrations, Patient Portal, clinical modules, `.env`/`config/database.php`, Railway, or local MySQL; NO package install; NO git operations; `public/hot` untouched; tests SQLite `:memory:` only.
- Root cause: `AppointmentManagementService::validateSchedule()` (the single funnel for walk-in + confirm) validated calendar-date format and the provisional clinic-hours window but never compared the schedule against the current time, and both FormRequests likewise had no past check - so any past date/time passed every layer. App timezone is UTC while the clinic lives in Asia/Manila, so no existing layer even had a Manila-anchored "now" for scheduling.
- Server-side rule (service = authoritative write boundary): `pastScheduleIssue(date, time)` compares the FULL local date (+ time) against `Carbon::now('Asia/Manila')` re-read at call time (inside the transaction - stale forms that cross midnight are still refused): date before today's Manila date -> refused (scope `date`); same day with a time earlier than the current minute -> refused (scope `time`, equal minute ALLOWED); later dates pass; date-only (delivery, null time) allowed today or later, earlier dates refused. No time is ever shifted forward, no date auto-adjusted, no record mutated on failure. Clinic-hours (08:00 inclusive - 17:00 exclusive, checkup/counseling) and delivery 24/7/date-only rules unchanged and still run first; thresholds untouched.
- Layering: FormRequests (`AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest`, `AppointmentConfirmRequest`) call the SAME static in an after-callback for early field-level errors (keys `appointment_date`/`appointment_time`, `confirmed_date`/`confirmed_time`), so the walk-in modal's existing `$errors->hasAny` auto-reopen keeps working; the service repeats the check for direct callers (proven by a dedicated bypass test). Confirm failures flash `errorAppointmentId` (FormRequest `failedValidation` + controller DomainException path) and the Blade x-init reopens the details modal with the error banner visible.
- Frontend (hints only - server stays authoritative): `min="{{ $clinicToday }}"` on both date inputs; walk-in date/time x-model'd into Alpine state (initialized from `old()`); shared `checkSchedule()` guard on both submit handlers blocks submission with a readable inline message and never mutates the input; `old()` preservation for walk-in date/time/type and confirm date/time; helper text updated to state the past rule; no new JS packages.
- Tests: 11 new frozen-time (`Carbon::setTestNow` in Asia/Manila, afterEach cleanup) tests covering all 16 required scenarios: yesterday walk-in rejected (no appointment/audit); today's past minute rejected; today's future AND equal-minute times allowed; future date/time allowed; checkup 07:59/17:00 and counseling 07:30 refused (service `walk_in` key - same wiring the pre-existing clinic-hours tests document), 16:59 allowed; delivery later-today 23:30 and 02:15-tomorrow allowed; date-only delivery today allowed / yesterday refused; pending confirm refused for past date, past today-time and past date-only (stays pending; `errorAppointmentId` flashed); direct service calls (`createWalkIn`/`confirm`) throw DomainException with zero rows/audit written; failed validation creates no appointment and no audit; Manila midnight boundaries (00:00:30 - UTC date still previous day - proves Manila anchoring; 23:59:30 evening boundary; equal-minute allowed); past appointments stay readable on the calendar, keep status, and remain cancel/decline/complete-actionable (only confirm-for-past is blocked).
- Notable finding (pre-existing, NOT fixed - out of scope): a pipe-string rule embedded inside a rules ARRAY (`'after_or_equal:08:00|before:17:00'` returned by `clinicHoursRule()` and placed as one array element) is silently ignored by this Laravel version's rule parser - it never fired in EITHER FormRequest (the 3B.2B notes had recorded this as an SQLite quirk; it is actually parser behavior, DB-independent). The clinic-hours rule remains enforced by the service (tests assert the `walk_in`/`appointment` keys, unchanged); splitting the rule into separate array elements would change error keys and touch existing test expectations - flagged for approval, deliberately not done here. A related latent bug was fixed inside the NEW code only: `Carbon::createFromFormat('Y-m-d', ...)` inherits the current time-of-day, so the new check calls `startOfDay()` before comparing (without it, today's timed schedules would have skipped the time check).
- Verification: preflight green before every run (`bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent; phpunit.xml forces SQLite `:memory:` with `force="true"`); `php -l` clean on all six modified PHP files; focused suite `Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest` = **39 passed / 268 assertions** (28 pre-existing + 11 new, zero changes to any pre-existing test expectation); full suite = **1396 passed / 122 failed** vs the pre-change baseline 1385/122 - the +11 passed are exactly the new tests and the 122 failures are the identical pre-existing legacy set (incl. the known `StaffAccessControlTest` line 76) - no regressions.
- Defense notes: one shared static method is the ONLY implementation of the past rule (service + both FormRequests cannot drift); comparison is always full-local-datetime in Asia/Manila with the app timezone left at UTC (matching the 3B.2A calendar policy); the check runs at the write boundary inside the transaction, not at form-render time; frontend guards are convenience only (bypassable by design - the server is proven authoritative by the direct-service-call test); failed validation never mutates a record or writes audit; existing clinical thresholds, transitions, audit shape and route boundaries are untouched; frozen-time tests are hermetic (`afterEach` clears `setTestNow`) so the rest of the suite is unaffected.
- Residual risks: (1) the frontend `clinicToday`/`clinicNow` anchors render at page load and go stale while the page sits open - harmless because the server re-checks at write time; (2) the pre-existing dead clinic-hours rule string in both FormRequests still needs an approved fix (split into array elements + update the two `walk_in`/`appointment` error-key test expectations) if field-level clinic-hours errors are wanted; (3) a pending appointment REQUESTED for a past date can still be declined (by design - decline takes no schedule) but cannot be confirmed for any past schedule - staff must pick today/future; (4) patient selection in the walk-in modal is intentionally NOT repopulated from `old()` after a failed submit (date/time/type are) - the staff re-picks the patient; (5) browser/manual verification of the Alpine guards was not performed (SQLite tests only, per project safety rules); (6) the 15-failure focused / ~122 broader pre-existing baselines remain unchanged and out of scope.

## DEPLA Phase 3B.2B Sprint: Walk-in Modal UI Regression + Clinic Hours Validation (2026-10-10)

- Scope: two focused fixes from the previous past-schedule sprint's residual risks. MODIFIED `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php` (walk-in factory `<script>` JSON emitted unescaped with `JSON_HEX_TAG|JSON_HEX_AMP|JSON_HEX_APOS|JSON_HEX_QUOT`, hardened fetch-route JSON, explanatory comment), `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentConfirmRequest.php` (`clinicHoursRule(): string` replaced by `clinicHoursRules(): array` with two split elements), `app/Http/Requests/AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest.php` (same array merge), `tests/Feature/Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest.php` (4 clinic-hours assertions moved to field keys + 3 new regression tests). NO change to `AppointmentManagementService`, controllers, routes, clinical thresholds, migrations, `.env`/`config/database.php`, Railway, local MySQL, Patient Portal, or `public/hot`; NO package install; NO git operations; tests SQLite `:memory:` only.
- Root cause (both visible UI regressions share one cause): the past-sprint factory script interpolated `json_encode(...)` through Blade's escaped echo `{{ }}` inside an inline `<script>`. Blade HTML-escapes the JSON quotes to `&quot;`, and browsers do NOT decode HTML entities in script text, so the script hit a SyntaxError on its first line and the entire `appointmentDetailsPanel()` Alpine component tree never initialized: the submit button's `x-text="submitting ? 'Creating…' : 'Create Walk-in Appointment'"` never applied (empty green square), the patient search input's `x-model`/`x-on`/debounce bindings were dead (could not type or search), and `x-show`-gated empty elements (`<ul>`, `scheduleError` `<p>`) rendered as blank space. The modal itself still opened because `x-modal` is an independent Alpine component. The old factory script only interpolated a quote-free URL, so the escaping was harmless before the past-sprint `json_encode` additions; HTTP tests never execute JS, which is why the prior suite did not catch it. Empirically confirmed by rendering the calendar through a scratch feature test: the compiled Blade emitted `const CLINIC_TODAY = &quot;2026-10-10&quot;` inside the script text.
- UI fix (minimal, surgical): the four factory-script interpolations (`CLINIC_TODAY`, `CLINIC_NOW`, `walkIn.type/date/time`) and the fetch route URL were switched from `{{ json_encode(...) }}` to `{!! json_encode(..., JSON_HEX_TAG|JSON_HEX_AMP|JSON_HEX_APOS|JSON_HEX_QUOT) !!}`, matching the details-payload pattern already used safely in the same file. A comment in the script documents why escaped echo must not be used inside `<script>`. A rendered-page guard scan confirmed zero remaining Blade-escaped interpolations inside any `<script>` block. Attribute-context `:value="{{ json_encode(old(...)) }}"` bindings were deliberately left alone - HTML attribute values ARE entity-decoded, so they were never broken. The user-applied bare `x-data` on the walk-in button and the `<template x-for>` search-results fix were preserved untouched.
- Clinic-hours fix: root cause is a Laravel rule-parser pitfall - a piped rule string (`'after_or_equal:08:00|before:17:00'`) returned by `clinicHoursRule()` and placed as ONE element inside a rules ARRAY is silently ignored (pipes are split only at the top level of a string rule, not inside array elements). This was proven empirically (piped-in-array passes validation; the identical rule split into two elements fires both messages). The service was always the only enforcer, which is why no behavior change was observed in production - only the missing field-level errors. `clinicHoursRule(): string` was replaced by `clinicHoursRules(): array` (returns `['after_or_equal:08:00', 'before:17:00']`) and both FormRequests now `array_merge` it into their rules, keeping the existing per-request custom messages. Delivery/24-7 requests remain excluded via the requests' existing `$requiresTime` conditionals.
