- Files ADDED: `app/Services/PatientSetupTokenService.php` (create/exists/recentlyCreated/isEligible/consume; KEY_PREFIX `setup:`; EXPIRE_SECONDS 86400; THROTTLE_SECONDS 60; purpose-bound hash over `setup:{id}:{plaintext}`; consume() = one DB::transaction with lockForUpdate on the token row then the users row, eligibility recheck, password write, remember-token rotation, flag clear, token delete, PATIENT_SETUP_COMPLETED audit inside the transaction); `app/Http/Controllers/PatientSetupLinkController.php` (guest GET/POST; unified INVALID_MESSAGE for every ineligible link; GET never consumes; failed password validation 422 never reaches consume; Referrer-Policy no-referrer on the form response); `app/Mail/PatientAccountSetupMail.php` + `resources/views/emails/patient_account_setup.blade.php` (24-hour single-use warning, contact-the-clinic note, no PII beyond first name); `resources/views/auth/patient-setup.blade.php` (hidden token+email, New/Confirm Password with visibility toggles). Files MODIFIED: `app/Services/PatientAccountCreationService.php` (createForPatient(Patient, User $actor, string $email) - password parameter removed; assertEmailIsValid replaces assertCredentialsAreValid; Hash::make(Str::random(40)) random internal placeholder; buildUser no longer takes a plaintext password); `app/Http/Controllers/PatientAccountController.php` (store drops password, post-commit sendSetupEmail with try/catch Throwable, warning flash + PATIENT_SETUP_EMAIL_FAILED audit + Log::error (IDs and exception class only) on delivery failure; new resendSetupEmail - 404 missing patient, defense-in-depth 403 non-admin/staff, error if no pending setup, 60-second throttle via recentlyCreated, audits PATIENT_SETUP_EMAIL_RESENT / PATIENT_SETUP_EMAIL_FAILED); `app/Http/Requests/PatientAccountStoreRequest.php` (email-only rules; posted password fields silently ignored); `routes/auth.php` (GET/POST patient-setup/{token} behind guest + no-store); `routes/web.php` (POST /patients/{patient}/account/setup-email - admin+staff); `resources/views/layouts/guest.blade.php` (patient.setup.show header branch); `resources/views/patients/show.blade.php` (session warning flash, account-state copy, Resend Setup Email button while must_change_password, modal password fields removed, mandated 24-hour explanation text, dead toggleAccountPassword JS removed).
- Token storage (no migration): setup tokens live in the existing password_reset_tokens table under namespaced key `setup:{user_id}` in the email column. Hash computed over `setup:{id}:{plaintext}` so even a transplanted row cannot validate cross-purpose. Disjoint keyspaces from ordinary reset tokens (plain email) guarantee no cross-purpose acceptance, invalidation or expiry leakage. Setup = 24 hours; ordinary broker untouched at 60 minutes. Both kinds of rows can coexist for the same account (tested).
- Concurrency: consume() holds lockForUpdate on the token row for the entire hash-check + expiry + eligibility-recheck + password-write + delete sequence; a competing request blocks then fails with no row. Lock order: token row then users row; no other path locks both in reverse. SQLite tests prove sequential double-submit; the MySQL/InnoDB parallel-race guarantee comes from lockForUpdate (documented in the service docblock).
- Email behavior: mail is sent only AFTER the provisioning transaction commits (never inside it). Delivery failure never rolls back the account, pointer, pregnancy links or Task B associations - the account stays pending setup, a warning flash and PATIENT_SETUP_EMAIL_FAILED audit are recorded, and the Resend Setup Email action is the recovery path. Mail wording distinguishes transport acceptance from inbox delivery. No auto-login after setup; redirect to login with status message. Production mailer (Brevo-compatible) must be configured on Railway before go-live - configuration is an operator action, not a code change.
- Tests: new `tests/Feature/Phase15PatientAccountSetupEmailTest.php` - 37 passed / 326 assertions covering the full approved matrix: email-only provisioning authorization/integrity/random password/setup flag; post-commit email delivery and nothing sent on failed provisioning; email-failure preserves account + pregnancy links + audit; resend no-duplicate/throttle/invalidates-old/guards; GET form without consuming + Referrer-Policy + no-store; valid POST sets password, clears flag, consumes, audits PATIENT_SETUP_COMPLETED; used-token GET/POST permanently rejected; 23h59m valid / 24h+1m rejected; forgot-password still 60-min; unified refusal for garbage/missing/mismatched email; weak password and confirmation mismatch do not consume; activated/archived/non-patient refused; setup-to-reset and reset-to-setup cross-purpose rejection + both token rows coexist; audit safety dump (no tokens/URLs/passwords/setup: prefix); login works with no forced change; Task B links survive the flow; sequential double-submit = one success one refusal; audit-write failure rolls the whole consume step back; namespace storage assertion; route surface; unified message rendered on the login page. Existing tests updated to the new contract: Phase2B (email-only payload; random-password test; email-only service-boundary test; password-fields-ignored tests), Phase2D (modal assertions + mandated copy; password inputs never rendered), Phase3A and Phase3HTaskB (payloads), Phase3H2 (createForPatient call sites drop password argument).
- Verification (isolation re-confirmed before every run: bootstrap/cache/config.php absent; phpunit.xml forces APP_ENV/DB_CONNECTION=sqlite/DB_DATABASE=:memory: with force=true; TestCase.php guard active; every run in a fresh process): focused Phase15 file 37/37; regression chunk (1) Phase2B+2D+2C = 116/116 after fixing one apostrophe-escaping assertSee in Phase2D; chunk (2) Phase2A+2C+3A+3H2 = 112/112; chunk (3) TaskB+StaffAccountPasswordReset+3H3+3H4+3H5 = 91/91; chunk (4) Auth PasswordReset+PasswordUpdate+PasswordConfirmation+3E2+3E3+3F = 80/80. php -l clean on every touched PHP file. No lint script in composer.json. No migration executed. No .env/config/package/git change. public/hot untouched. MySQL and Railway untouched.
- Defense notes: token purpose separation without a migration was chosen so the mandate could ship inside the approved scope (no schema change) while remaining cryptographically disjoint from ordinary reset tokens (namespaced key + purpose-bound hash input - two independent barriers). Email-only provisioning removes the single largest staff-side credential-exposure path (a password spoken over the phone or written on paper) without weakening authorization - the modal and route still require admin/staff, the service still re-validates at its boundary, and posted password fields are ignored rather than honored. Strict single-use with lockForUpdate answers the double-submit race the mandate called out; the sequential SQLite proof plus the documented MySQL lock semantics are the honest split of what each environment can demonstrate. Email-failure handling follows the same post-commit recoverable doctrine as the account itself: never un-commit, always audit with IDs only, always give staff a resend path. Task B compatibility was proven end-to-end (provisioning expands history links, setup completes, links remain).
- Known limitations: (1) production Railway MAIL_MAILER must be confirmed/operated before go-live (unknown from this environment); (2) SQLite cannot prove parallel-race behavior - only lockForUpdate on MySQL/InnoDB does, and that is asserted by construction, not by a multi-process test; (3) the setup link carries the login email in the query string (same convention as the ordinary reset links) - the anti-enumeration unified message means an observer still cannot learn whether an address exists; (4) the 60-second resend throttle is per-account via the token row timestamp, not a rate limiter on the HTTP route itself; (5) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - all UI assertions are HTTP/markup level; (6) the pre-existing monolithic full-suite hang documented in Phase 3D.2A was avoided by running focused gates in fresh processes.
## DEPLA Phase 2 Sprint 1: Admin Patient Account Management (2026-10-16)

- Requirement: one centralized Admin-only page listing every Patient Portal account (active, pending setup and deactivated) with validated search, status filters, real summary counts and a secret-free details modal, plus two reuse-first actions - Resend Setup Email (existing Phase 1.5 endpoint) and Send Password Reset Link (ordinary Laravel broker, Admin-only). Status doctrine is fixed: Deactivated = deleted_at present; Pending Setup = live row with must_change_password true; Active = live row with must_change_password false; email_verified_at is never consulted in either direction. Route parameters accept only an account ID - never an email; no password is revealed or settable anywhere on the page.
- Files ADDED: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PatientAccountManagementController.php` (index + sendResetLink); `app/Http/Requests/AdminPatientAccountIndexRequest.php` (admin-only authorize, trimmed search max 100, status in all/active/pending/deactivated defaulting to all, page integer min 1); `resources/views/admin/patient-accounts/index.blade.php` (DEPLA theme, x-app-header, x-flash, three real-count stat cards data-stat=total/active/pending, four status pill tabs with counts preserving the search term, GET search form with hidden status, 10-row table with data-status / data-links / data-details-* attributes, Details modal and one generic action-confirm modal, $accounts->links()); `tests/Feature/Phase2Sprint1PatientAccountManagementTest.php` (16 tests / 253 assertions, pa2s1-prefixed helpers). Files MODIFIED: `routes/web.php` (use statement + AdminMiddleware group with GET patient-accounts.index and POST patient-accounts.send-reset-link ->withTrashed()); `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php` (Patient Accounts nav item inside the existing role-gated Administration block, active state via request()->routeIs('patient-accounts.*')); `app/Models/User.php` (read-only pregnancyLinks(): HasMany used only for withCount).
- Backend doctrine: the listing source is User::withTrashed()->where('role', 'patient') so deactivated rows remain countable and filterable; summary counts (total/active/pending) are live DB counts independent of the search term, the deactivated card count is shown only on its status pill; search matches the account name, the registered email and the linked live patient's name columns through whereHas('patientAccount') so a legacy portal name no longer hides a record; results paginate at 10 per page with withQueryString() so page/ status/ search all survive. sendResetLink re-checks role === 'admin' inside the controller (defense in depth over the middleware), 404s non-patient account IDs, refuses deactivated accounts ("Deactivated accounts cannot receive account-management reset links.") and pending-setup accounts ("This account has not completed setup. Use Resend Setup Email instead."), sends through Password::sendResetLink on the registered email only (a crafted email field in the body is ignored - the target is always the route-bound account), audits PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED (module PATIENT, ID-only description, no token/URL) on success and PASSWORD_RESET_EMAIL_FAILED (exception class only) on transport failure with a safe user-facing error, and redirects to patient-accounts.index carrying the original query string.
- Reuse decision (resend): the pending-setup row's Resend button posts to the EXISTING Phase 1.5 endpoint route('patients.account.resend-setup-email', $patientId), which keeps its 24-hour single-use namespaced setup token, 60-second throttle, PATIENT_SETUP_EMAIL_RESENT audit and patients.show redirect untouched - the sprint adds no second token system (asserted: every password_reset_tokens row still carries the setup: prefix after both actions).
- Security: AdminMiddleware on both routes + FormRequest authorize + in-controller role recheck (three layers); no password field exists in the page, modal or request; no token, reset URL or setup link is rendered or logged; route parameters are numeric account IDs only; deactivated and pending rows expose neither action button; the details modal payload is identity + status + setup state + created date + link count only (no contact number, address, password hash or remember token); Staff keep the Patient Profile create/resend permissions (tested) while both Admin-module routes stay 403 for staff and patients; pregnancy ownership (patients.user_id pointer, user_pregnancy_links rows) and clinical fixtures (prenatal visits, medical histories) are asserted byte-identical after exercising every new surface.
- Tests (SQLite :memory: only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: no bootstrap/cache/config.php, phpunit.xml force=true guards active, TestCase guard active; fresh processes): new Phase2Sprint1PatientAccountManagementTest 16/16 - guest redirects; admin render + sidebar visibility for admin and absence for staff/patient; staff+patient 403 on both routes with zero notifications/audits; non-patient target 404; status doctrine with email_verified_at independence in both directions; search by account name, email and legacy linked-patient name plus max:100 422; all four status filters, 10-per-page pagination with page-2 counts, query-string preservation into the page-2 href (search + status) and invalid-status 422; linked-pregnancy data-links counts and secret/clinical absence; resend reusing the Phase 1.5 endpoint (2 mails, one audit, single setup: token system, no resend row for active accounts); active reset-link success (Notification sent to the account only, attacker never, audit ID-only, broker row present, password/pointer/links unchanged); 60-second reset throttle (one send, one audit, safe error on the second); transport failure (failed audit without the exception message, no success audit, state unchanged); pending and deactivated refusals; Staff permission preservation; ownership + clinical-record invariance across every new surface. Regression chunks, all green: Phase15+2B+2D+StaffAccountPasswordReset+ClinicRouteAuthorization = 110; Phase3H2+3H4Retirement-suite+3F+3A+Phase15 (second gate) = 118; Unit suite = 371 pass / 1 pre-existing AssessmentContextTest float-vs-int failure; StaffAccessControl+StaffAccountAdministrationBoundary+StaffAccountLifecycleBatchTwo+Phase2C+ProfileTest+LoginRedirectSafety = 87 pass with only the 2 documented pre-existing failures (StaffAccessControl edit-visibility, ProfileTest soft-delete).
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this sprint, verified and deliberately left unfixed: PatientViewControllerTest (17), PatientProfileRiskPanelTest (8), MedicalHistoryScopeTest (1), Phase16EUiTest (3: I/D3/I2), AssessmentLinkedReferralTest (2), ReferralFollowThroughTest (4), RiskMonitoringStatusTest (2 AdminMiddleware 403), StaffAccessControlTest (1), ProfileTest (1), ExplainabilitySprint7Test / GestationalAgeAutofillTest / PatientPhilhealthTest / PatientExportConsistencyTest / AssessmentContextTest sets reproduced identically in earlier session tool-outputs predating this sprint; none of those files were touched here (this sprint's only shared-file edits are routes/web.php additions, one sidebar nav item and a read-only User relation). The monolithic full-suite run was attempted once, reproduced the documented pre-existing failure set, then hung on the known network-dependent tests documented in Phase 3D.2A and was stopped - focused gates in fresh processes were used instead.
- Defense notes: the status doctrine is derived exclusively from deleted_at + must_change_password and is enforced at the controller (refusals), the view (data-status attributes and action visibility) and the tests (email_verified_at independence both ways), so no future contributor can quietly reintroduce the email-verified shortcut; summary counts read the database rather than the paginated page so the cards stay truthful under search; the resend action deliberately reuses the Phase 1.5 endpoint instead of a new controller method so the single-use token, throttle and audit contracts cannot fork into two implementations; reset-link assistance is the ordinary Laravel broker rather than a bespoke token flow, inheriting its 60-minute expiry and 60-second throttle, and the failure path follows the established password-reset doctrine (audit with exception class only, never the message); three independent admin checks (middleware, FormRequest, controller) mean removing any single layer cannot open the module; the details modal is a data-minimization surface - it carries exactly the columns an admin needs to decide which action to take and nothing else.
- Known limitations: (1) the reset-link throttle is the broker's per-email 60-second window, not a route rate limiter - a determined admin hammering one account still gets one email per minute; (2) search is un-accented LIKE matching across three columns with no ranking, acceptable at clinic scale but not indexed; (3) the Resend Setup Email button redirects to patients.show (the Phase 1.5 contract) rather than back to the account list - a follow-up could add a return parameter without touching the token system; (4) the pagination links are Tailwind-styled by the paginator default rather than a custom DEPLA component; (5) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - all UI assertions are HTTP/markup level; (6) the monolithic full-suite hang persisted and focused gates remain the verification strategy.

## DEPLA Phase 2 Sprint 2: Deactivate / Reactivate Patient Accounts (2026-10-16)

- Requirement: let an Admin deactivate and reactivate Patient Portal accounts from the Phase 2 Sprint 1 listing, with the SoftDeletes status doctrine untouched (Deactivated = deleted_at present; Pending Setup = live + must_change_password true; Active = live + must_change_password false). Deactivation must deny an already authenticated session on its NEXT request (not just new logins) without any migration, config change, hard delete or auth-layer modification; passwords, remember tokens, must_change_password, the patients pointer, pregnancy links and every clinical record must survive a full cycle byte-for-byte; each action writes exactly one ID-only audit row; repeated submissions get predictable error flashes with no duplicate audit. Also two Sprint 1 cleanups: rename the details-modal label `Verified Linked Pregnancies` to `Linked Pregnancies` (the underlying pregnancyLinks() count has never filtered on verified_at) and update the matching Sprint 1 test assertion.
- Files MODIFIED: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PatientAccountManagementController.php` (new deactivate()/reactivate(): assertAdmin defense-in-depth, abort_unless role === 'patient' 404, predictable repeated-submission error flashes, delete()/restore() + PATIENT_ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED / PATIENT_ACCOUNT_REACTIVATED audit rows (module PATIENT, user_id = actor, patient_id from the pointer, ID-only descriptions with no email/name/clinical value) inside ONE DB::transaction each, redirect to patient-accounts.index carrying the original query string; class docblock updated from "lifecycle not implemented" to the two-write-surface doctrine); `routes/web.php` (two new POST routes in the existing AdminMiddleware group, both ->withTrashed() so a deactivated account resolves for a friendly in-controller error instead of a 404 and a staff/admin id still 404s via the role check; comment block updated); `resources/views/admin/patient-accounts/index.blade.php` (Deactivate button on every live row - Active AND Pending - with data-action-variant="danger", Reactivate button only on deactivated rows with data-action-variant="success", both carrying the current query string; the shared confirm modal recolors its icon and submit button per variant through applyAccountActionVariant() with success as the safe fallback; details-modal label renamed to Linked Pregnancies); `tests/Feature/Phase2Sprint1PatientAccountManagementTest.php` (label assertion updated). Files ADDED: `tests/Feature/Phase2Sprint2AccountLifecycleTest.php` (13 tests / 172 assertions, pa2s2-prefixed helpers mirroring the Sprint 1 conventions).
- Backend doctrine: no migration and no auth-layer code were needed, by design. The Eloquent user provider applies SoftDeletingScope to retrieveById / retrieveByCredentials / retrieveByToken, so the moment deleted_at is stamped: existing sessions resolve no user on their next request (AuthenticationException -> login redirect), new logins fail credential retrieval, the ordinary password broker refuses the account, and PatientSetupTokenService::isEligible/consume refuse it through the trashed() recheck (plus PatientSetupLinkController's user lookup already excludes soft-deleted rows). The Sprint 1 endpoints (send-reset-link, resend setup email) already refused deactivated accounts and keep doing so. must_change_password is never touched, so a deactivated Pending account returns to Pending Setup after reactivation (tested) and an Active account returns to Active. Each lifecycle write is a single DB::transaction holding the delete()/restore() and the audit row together - a failed audit write rolls back the status change, matching the Phase 1.5 consume() doctrine. Repeated submissions are idempotent-safe: a second deactivate or reactivate answers with a specific error flash and writes no second audit row. The redirect always returns to patient-accounts.index with the original query string (status/search/page preserved) so the admin's filter context survives.
- Authorization: three independent layers exactly like Sprint 1 - the auth+clinic route group, AdminMiddleware, and the in-controller assertAdmin() recheck; plus abort_unless(role === 'patient', 404) so a staff/admin account id pasted into the URL is a 404, never a 403 oracle. Guests are redirected to login by the outer group. Staff and patient roles get 403 on both new routes (tested with zero side effects).
- UI: Deactivate is offered for BOTH live statuses (Active and Pending Setup) because the mandate covers every patient portal account; Reactivate appears only on deactivated rows, discoverable through the Deactivated status filter. The shared confirm modal gained a two-tone variant (danger red for Deactivate, the existing green for everything else) so a destructive action is visually distinct without duplicating the modal. Confirm text for Deactivate states that linked pregnancies and clinical records are not deleted - the safety property in the admin's own language.
- Audit trail: PATIENT_ACCOUNT_DEACTIVATED and PATIENT_ACCOUNT_REACTIVATED, module PATIENT, user_id = acting admin, patient_id resolved from the patients pointer, description carries only the numeric user ID (asserted: no '@' and no patient name in the description). Exactly one row per successful transition; zero rows for refused or repeated submissions.
- Tests (SQLite :memory: only; isolation re-confirmed before the run: bootstrap/cache/config.php absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active): guest redirects on both routes; staff+patient 403 with no side effects; non-patient target 404; active-account deactivation (audit row, query-preserving redirect, password/flag/pointer unchanged, ID-only description); Pending round trip (deactivate keeps must_change_password true, reactivate returns the row to the Pending Setup badge); active reactivation (audit row, password preserved, Active badge); repeated-submission handling (error flashes, exactly one audit row per transition); login blocked while deactivated and restored after reactivation (including ordinary forgot-password producing zero notifications and setup-token consumption refusing with the unified message); an already authenticated session denied on its next request after deactivation and honored again after reactivation - proven with a REAL session login, admin deactivation in another "tab", forgetGuards() between requests, no actingAs on the patient request; admin reset-link, resend-setup-email and the emailed setup link itself all refusing a deactivated account; UI affordance matrix (deactivate on active+pending, reactivate only on deactivated, variant wiring present, label rename); summary counts tracking each transition; ownership + clinical-record invariance across a full cycle (users/patients/link rows/visits/histories counts, pointer, password hash, flag, and exactly one password_reset_tokens row remaining - the provisioning setup token). Sprint 1 suite re-run green (16/16, 253 assertions) after the label rename.
- Defense notes: implementing deactivation as SoftDeletes with ZERO new auth code is the core decision - the SoftDeletingScope on the user provider is the single enforcement point, already battle-tested for the Staff archive workflow, so sessions, logins, broker resets and setup tokens all inherit the denial without a parallel "is_active" flag that could drift from the session layer; the pending flag is deliberately preserved so the lifecycle action can never silently promote an un-setup account to Active; the transaction pairing of status change + audit means the trail cannot miss a transition; ID-only audit descriptions keep the trail non-sensitive even if it is later exported; withTrashed() binding on the routes is what converts a double-click into a human-readable error instead of a 404, while the role check still 404s foreign ids; the two-tone modal variant is pure presentation data (dataset attribute -> class swap) with no behavior change to the Sprint 1 actions.
- Known limitations: (1) deactivation does not proactively invalidate the patient's remember-me cookie value - the cookie is re-resolved through the same SoftDeletingScope on every request, so it cannot grant access, but the cookie bytes remain in the browser until expiry (same behavior as the existing Staff archive); (2) setup tokens issued before deactivation remain in the password_reset_tokens table until their 24-hour expiry (they cannot be consumed while deactivated and a resend supersedes them), and no cleanup job exists - acceptable at clinic scale; (3) there is no bulk deactivate - one account per POST, matching the Staff archive contract; (4) the Deactivate button is not offered inside the details modal, only in the row action column; (5) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - all UI assertions are HTTP/markup level; (6) the monolithic full-suite hang persisted - verification stayed on focused gates in fresh processes (Sprint 1 + Sprint 2 only, as instructed; the documented 123-failure / 129-error pre-existing full-suite baseline was NOT re-run). NOTE: limitations (1) and (2) are superseded by the session-revocation work below, which now proactively rotates the remember token and purges reset/setup tokens on every deactivation AND reactivation.

## DEPLA Phase 2: Patient Session + Token Revocation (2026-10-16)

- Requirement: deactivate/reactivate must not merely rely on the SoftDeletingScope to deny access on the next request - it must actively revoke every live credential so an old session, an old remember-me cookie, an old password-reset link and an old first-time-setup link can NEVER resurrect a patient account, even after reactivation; a fresh login is always required after reactivation; Admin and Staff sessions must never be invalidated; no migration and no local MySQL writes; verified with SQLite :memory: tests including the DATABASE session driver, not only the default array driver.
- Backend implementation (all driver-independent, no migration, no config change): (a) `app/Services/PatientSessionRevocationService.php` - per-user cache revocation timestamp (`depl:patient_session_revoked:{id}`, 24 h TTL >= SESSION_LIFETIME) plus the `patient_auth_at` session-marker key; deliberately NOT remember-token-versioned because NewPasswordController:80 and PatientSetupTokenService::consume:172 already rotate remember_token on legitimate password-reset/setup, which would have caused unintended logouts. (b) `app/Http/Middleware/EnsurePatientSessionNotRevoked.php` - appended to the web group; a no-op for guests and every non-patient role; for a patient it compares the session's `patient_auth_at` (sub-second microtime) against the cache revocation timestamp and, when older, logs the guard out, invalidates and regenerates the session, and redirects to login; a missing marker on a live session is treated as 0 (fail-closed for pre-feature sessions). (c) `app/Listeners/SetPatientSessionAuthMarker.php` - a Login-event listener (registered in AppServiceProvider) that stamps `patient_auth_at = microtime(true)` for patient-role users only; the Login event fires for BOTH form login (Auth::attempt) and remember-me re-auth (userFromRecaller -> updateSession + fireLoginEvent), so one listener covers every authentication path without editing the login controller. (d) `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PatientAccountManagementController.php` - a new `revokePatientAccess()` helper called inside BOTH the deactivate() and reactivate() transactions (while the model is live) that rotates the remember token (Str::random(60)), deletes the patient's `sessions` rows (strictly by the indexed user_id), and purges `password_reset_tokens` rows keyed by the plain email AND by `setup:{id}`; the cache revocation marker is written AFTER the transaction commits so a rolled-back transaction never leaves a stale marker. Class/route docblocks updated; the stale "no auth code was changed" claim removed.
- Remember-me / token invalidation: rotating remember_token at deactivation AND reactivation permanently kills every recaller cookie already issued (the provider's hash_equals fails); purging password_reset_tokens (both the ordinary email-keyed row and the namespaced setup row) kills every previously emailed reset/setup link. A fresh login after reactivation works and re-issues a valid recaller only on that fresh login.
- Driver assumptions / limitations: the cache store is whatever the application already uses (database in production, array in tests) - no new table, no schema change; the cache revocation marker has a 24 h TTL, so if it ever expires while an old session is still alive the middleware fails OPEN for that bounded window (accepted trade-off vs. false-positive logouts); the session-marker comparison uses sub-second microtime so a login and a deactivation in the same wall-clock second are still correctly ordered; the database-session row deletion is a defense-in-depth layer on top of the middleware, not the sole enforcement point.
- Tests (SQLite :memory:, isolation re-confirmed: bootstrap/cache/config.php absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards): new `tests/Feature/Phase2SessionRevocationTest.php` (11 tests / 98 assertions, pa2rev-prefixed helpers) covering - database-driver session revocation end-to-end (login writes a sessions row, deactivate deletes it and the old session 302s, reactivate keeps the old session dead, fresh login works); session-row deletion scoped strictly to the target user (patient rows gone, staff/admin rows intact); remember-me recaller replay rejected after rotation; old password-reset link rejected after purge; old setup link rejected after purge; Admin/Staff sessions + remember tokens + requests untouched; repeated lifecycle actions stay audit-free of duplicates; patient/pregnancy/pointer invariance; middleware no-op for guests and non-patients; fail-closed for a pre-revocation session; fresh session honored after revocation. The Sprint 2 lifecycle test's step-4 assertion was flipped from "same session honored again after reactivation" to "same session still refused + fresh login required", and its password_reset_tokens count assertion updated to 0 (the provisioning setup token is now purged by the revocation cycle). All three focused suites re-run green in one process: 40 tests / 529 assertions (Sprint 1 16/253, Sprint 2 13/178, revocation 11/98).

## DEPLA Phase 2: Admin Reset-Link Action Removal (2026-10-16)

- Requirement: remove the redundant Admin "Send Password Reset Link" action from Patient Account Management. The patient-facing Forgot Password flow (`password.request` / `password.email` / `password.reset` / `password.update`, handled by `Auth\PasswordResetLinkController`) is the sole self-service reset surface and must keep working untouched; the separate `StaffController::resetAccount` for staff accounts is likewise out of scope and untouched. After this sprint the Admin account page offers exactly the final action matrix: Active = View Details + Deactivate; Pending Setup = + Resend Setup Email; Deactivated = Reactivate only. No migration, no clinical/auth module changes, no config edits.
- Removal surface (verified exclusive before editing - the only references in app/, tests/, resources/views/ and routes/ were these): (a) `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/PatientAccountManagementController.php` - entire `sendResetLink()` method + docblock deleted; the now-unused `Illuminate\Support\Facades\Log`, `Illuminate\Support\Facades\Password` and `Throwable` imports removed; class docblock rewritten to document a single write surface (deactivate/reactivate lifecycle) and note the former reset-link action was removed as redundant. (b) `routes/web.php` - POST `patient-accounts/{account}/reset-link` (`->name('patient-accounts.send-reset-link')`, `->withTrashed()`) deleted; the route comment block rewritten to record the removal. (c) `resources/views/admin/patient-accounts/index.blade.php` - the `@if ($isActive)` Send Reset Link button block removed; the now-dead `$isActive` variable removed; the details-modal copy updated to "Passwords and clinical records are never displayed here."
- Redundancy rationale (defense notes): with the Sprint 2 SoftDeletes status doctrine plus the session/token revocation work, an Admin-issued reset link added no security the patient-facing broker does not already provide - the ordinary `Password` broker already refuses deactivated accounts (SoftDeletingScope on the user provider), already throttles per-email at 60 seconds, and already expires at 60 minutes. Removing it eliminates a second write path on a patient account without removing any capability a patient cannot already exercise themselves, and shrinks the Admin surface to exactly the two audited lifecycle writes. The Staff module's own reset remains a separate, untouched endpoint (`StaffController::resetAccount`) because Staff accounts are outside the Patient Account Management scope.
- Tests: `Phase2Sprint1PatientAccountManagementTest.php` updated - reset-link import/docblock/guest-redirect line removed; the "denies staff and patient users" test now asserts deactivate forbidden + zero lifecycle audit rows instead of the deleted reset endpoint; the four reset-link-specific tests plus the two non-patient/pending refusals (which existed only to cover the broker refusal paths) replaced by a new section: (1) `it('no longer exposes the admin reset-link route')` - `Route::has('patient-accounts.send-reset-link')` is false and a raw POST to the path 404s; (2) `it('offers exactly the final action matrix per account status')` - one Active/Pending/Deactivated account, counts the `onclick="openAccountDetails(this)"` button usage (not the JS function definition) at 3, asserts the deactivate/reactivate/resend-setup paths per status, and the absence of the reset-link path, 'Send Password Reset Link' and 'Send Reset Link' copy; (3) `it('still lets a patient reset their own password through the forgot-password flow')` - POST `password.email` succeeds with a `status` flash, a `ResetPassword` notification is sent to the account, a `password_reset_tokens` row exists, an attacker body email is ignored, and the full `password.update` consumption changes the password (the Forgot Password contract is proven end-to-end, not merely assumed). The deactivated-account test, the staff-permissions test and the records-invariance test were updated to exercise the remaining lifecycle surface instead of the removed endpoint. `Phase2Sprint2AccountLifecycleTest.php` - the deactivated-account test's reset-link POST replaced with the repeated-deactivate refusal (proving the lifecycle `withTrashed()` route still resolves a trashed account), and the stale `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` count assertion removed (that audit action is no longer produced by this module at all). `Phase2SessionRevocationTest.php` - the old-password-reset-link revocation test now sources its token from the PATIENT-facing `route('password.email')` instead of the deleted admin endpoint, so it simultaneously proves the forgot-password flow still works and that a lifecycle cycle purges its token row (count 1 -> 0 -> consumption refused).
- Audit trail change: `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` and `PASSWORD_RESET_EMAIL_FAILED` are no longer written by Patient Account Management. They remain in use by the separate Staff module (`StaffAccountPasswordResetTest` asserts them) and were left untouched there.
- Tests (SQLite :memory: only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: bootstrap/cache/config.php absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active): all three focused suites re-run green in one process - 38 tests / 509 assertions (Sprint 1 14 tests / 234 assertions, Sprint 2 13 tests / 178 assertions, Phase2SessionRevocationTest 11 tests / 97 assertions). The monolithic full-suite run was NOT attempted (documented hang; focused gates were the instructed verification strategy).
- Known limitations: (1) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - all UI assertions are HTTP/markup level; (2) a patient who has forgotten both their password and their login email still needs a human-assisted recovery path (e.g. in-person identity verification) - the removed Admin action was never a recovery-by-identity path either, since it only ever emailed the already-registered address; (3) `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` is now a Staff-only audit action, so any future consumer filtering on it for patient activity will find nothing - a follow-up could rename it to `STAFF_PASSWORD_RESET_REQUESTED` for clarity, but that is out of scope here.

## DEPLA QA Sprint: Patient-Profile Edit Color, Completion Date Gate, Auto-Applying Appointment Filters (2026-10-18)

- Requirement: three QA-approved, minimal fixes with no clinical-behavior change beyond the mandated rule: (1) the Prenatal Visit Edit icon on the Patient Profile must match the neutral-gray styling of its adjacent Print action instead of the emerald one (header Edit/Print buttons already share `btn-secondary` and were deliberately left alone, as were the Medical History, Ultrasound, Baby and Birth Plan buttons); (2) `Mark Completed` is allowed only when the appointment's effective scheduled date (`confirmed_date ?? requested_date`) is TODAY or EARLIER in `Asia/Manila` - calendar dates only, so a later-today appointment stays completable today, enforced in BOTH the Alpine gate and the server, with no new status and no lifecycle change; (3) the Appointments calendar filters apply automatically on change - the Apply Filters button is removed, `requestSubmit()` preserves normal GET submission, hidden `view`/`date` inputs, combined filters, and Prev/Today/Next/view navigation are untouched, and server-side filtering is unchanged.
- Files MODIFIED: `resources/views/patients/show.blade.php` (one class string: prenatal-visit Edit icon `text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:text-emerald-900` -> `text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 hover:text-gray-900`, matching the neighboring Print link; icon, href, title, aria-label and permission gate byte-identical); `app/Services/AppointmentManagementService.php` (new `private assertCompletionScheduleDue()` called in `complete()` immediately AFTER the existing `assertStatus()` and inside the existing transaction on the locked row; new `public static completionNotDueIssue(?string $date): ?string` - the single source of truth for the date rule, mirroring the `scheduledStartPassedIssue()`/`pastScheduleIssue()` convention); `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php` (Complete button's Alpine gate gains `&& isCompletionDue(selected?.effectiveDateIso)` plus the new `isCompletionDue()` method comparing plain Y-m-d strings against the server-rendered `CLINIC_TODAY`; filter form gains `id="appointmentFilterForm"` + a `data-busy` submit guard, both selects gain `onchange="...requestSubmit()"`, the Apply Filters button is deleted, and an inline `pageshow` listener clears `data-busy` after a bfcache restore); `app/Http/Controllers/AppointmentCalendarController.php` (details payload gains `effectiveDateIso` = `confirmed_date ?? requested_date` as a plain `Y-m-d` string). Tests MODIFIED: `Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest` (4 tests), `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` (2), `Phase3D2CAppointmentChangeRequestReviewTest` (1), `Phase3D3B2StaffRescheduleReviewTest` (1), `Phase3D3B3ReschedulingNotificationsTest` (1) - future-dated completion fixtures/frozen clocks moved so each completion the suites still intend to allow is DUE. Tests ADDED: `tests/Feature/AppointmentCompletionDateRestrictionTest.php` (11 tests / 44 assertions) and `tests/Feature/AppointmentCalendarAutoFiltersTest.php` (3 tests / 20 assertions).
- Backend doctrine (FIX 2): the rule is a date-only comparison `effective date <= Carbon::now('Asia/Manila')` with `startOfDay()` normalization - appointment times are deliberately ignored, a missing schedule date enforces no cutoff (format rules own invalid input, same convention as `scheduledStartPassedIssue()`), and the effective date is resolved exactly like `PatientPortalController`/`AppointmentCalendarController` already resolve it (`confirmed_date ?? requested_date`, so a rescheduled confirmation can never regress to the original request). The check runs inside the existing `DB::transaction` on the row returned by `lockAppointment()`, i.e. re-read under `FOR UPDATE` after `assertStatus()` - the UI gate is therefore never the only enforcement point. `complete()` still returns through the controller's existing `catch (DomainException)` -> `back()->withErrors(['appointment' => ...])`, so a bypassed UI produces the same readable error flash as any other refusal; transactions, authorization, audit logging (`APPOINTMENT_COMPLETED`), auto-decline of pending change requests and every other appointment action are untouched.
- Frontend doctrine (FIX 2): `effectiveDateIso` is emitted as a date STRING and compared with `date <= CLINIC_TODAY` - no `Date()` parsing anywhere, so no UTC conversion can shift the clinic calendar day (the same reason the view already renders `clinicToday`/`clinicNow` Manila anchors server-side). `isCompletionDue(null)` returns true because a missing date is owned by server format rules. Cancel stays available on every confirmed appointment regardless of date, and the confirm modal/action form are unchanged.
- Frontend doctrine (FIX 3): only the Apply button and two `onchange` attributes changed - the form is still a plain GET with the same action, the same `view`/`date` hidden inputs and the same server-side `resolveTypeFilter()`/`resolveStatusFilter()`/`baseQuery()` path, so no new client-side filtering engine and no duplicate submission path exists. `requestSubmit()` (not `form.submit()`) keeps submit-event/validation semantics and matches the existing `prenatal_visits/index.blade.php` house pattern; the `data-busy` guard is cleared on `pageshow` so a back/forward restore can never leave the filters permanently disabled. The Audit Logs Apply Filters button and every other module were left untouched.
- Tests (SQLite `:memory:` only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV`/`DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`/`DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`, `tests/TestCase.php` guard active; no `optimize`/`config:cache`/cache-clear command was run): focused gate of 9 files = 190 passed / 2 failed / 1253 assertions. Coverage added: future confirmed -> direct PATCH refused + no audit row; confirmed today/yesterday -> completed; later-today -> completed (proves date-only); confirmed-date-wins in both directions; Asia/Manila-vs-UTC boundaries in both directions (Manila midnight while UTC still shows the previous day, and vice versa); pending/declined/cancelled/completed keep the existing refusal; `completionNotDueIssue()` unit expectations; the rendered panel carries `effectiveDateIso`, `CLINIC_TODAY` and the gate; filters render with no Apply button + exactly two `requestSubmit()` wirings + `data-busy` guard; combined type+status filtering through the existing query string; selected values and Prev/Today/Next carrying both filters.
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this sprint, verified and deliberately left unfixed: (1) `PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest` "shows only the Print action..." (`:246`) - reproduced identically after temporarily reverting this sprint's one-line class change back to the committed file, i.e. it fails on HEAD too (the test expects the prenatal Edit link to be absent from the Patient Profile while the view renders it for `status === 'ONGOING'`); (2) `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` "does not notify the clinic for walk-in creation" (`:213`) - the untouched `phase3b4WalkIn()` fixture hardcodes `appointment_date = 2026-10-15`, which is in the past relative to the wall clock (2026-10-18), so `assertNotPastSchedule` refuses it; this sprint's only edits in that file are two completion-fixture dates in other tests. Neither file was otherwise modified, and no production code was weakened to make either pass.
- Defense notes: the date rule lives in ONE public static method that both the service and any future caller share, so the view, the FormRequest layer and tests can never drift from the enforced behavior; it is checked under the row lock after the status check, preserving the existing transition-order diagnostics (a stale/future appointment still reports its STATUS problem first when that is the actual problem); the UI gate is presentation-only and its absence changes nothing about what the server accepts; the filter change removed an affordance rather than adding logic, so the server-side query contract (and therefore its tests) are byte-identical; the duplicate-submit guard is attribute-based rather than a JS debounce, which keeps it working with no script dependency and impossible to leave enabled after a page restore.
- Known limitations: (1) the completion rule is date-only by mandate - an appointment can still be completed at 00:00 of its scheduled day, which is the intended clinic-local behavior; (2) a staff member with the panel already open across a date boundary sees the frozen `CLINIC_TODAY` rendered at page load, so the button can appear eligible until the page is refreshed - the server refuses in that case; (3) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - the Alpine gate is asserted at markup/payload level, matching this repository's established HTTP-testing convention; (4) the pre-existing `PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest` and walk-in fixture failures above remain open and are candidates for a separate QA decision; (5) no migration, `.env`, cache, dependency, `public/hot`, Git or Railway change was made, and no full-suite run was attempted (focused gates only, per instructions).

## DEPLA QA Correction Sprint: Profile Action Colors + Cancel Button Restoration (2026-10-18)

- Requirement: correct two QA findings from the sprint above while keeping every successful part of it (server-side completion-date restriction, `effectiveDateIso`, Asia/Manila date-only comparison, automatic filters). (1) Three Patient Profile header text actions - Ultrasound Records Edit, Medical History Edit, Referral Follow-through Manage - must use the same neutral-gray text styling as the prenatal Print link (`text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:text-gray-900`), replacing the blue text; prenatal Edit already matches its Print sibling and stays. (2) Restore Cancel for future-dated confirmed appointments: the previous fix had put `isCompletionDue(selected?.effectiveDateIso)` on the SHARED Cancel/Complete container's `x-show`, so a future date hid Cancel too. The date gate must hide ONLY Mark Completed; Cancel keeps the original `!action && selected?.status === 'confirmed'` container condition; backend cancel eligibility is untouched (staff cancel has never carried a schedule-date restriction - only the confirmed-status transition).
- Files MODIFIED: `resources/views/patients/show.blade.php` (three anchor class strings blue-600/blue-800 -> gray-600/gray-900: medical-histories.edit link, ultrasound.edit per-record link, referrals.index Manage link; the prenatal Edit icon from the previous sprint is unchanged; other blue links such as ultrasound View Image/PDF and previous-referral View are deliberately untouched); `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php` (confirmed-status container restored to `x-show="!action && selected?.status === 'confirmed'"`; the Complete button alone gains `x-show="isCompletionDue(selected?.effectiveDateIso)"`; comment block corrected; every other gate, form, modal and the FIX 3 filter wiring byte-identical). Tests MODIFIED: `tests/Feature/AppointmentCompletionDateRestrictionTest.php` (the old view-gate test's shared-container assertion replaced with the per-button structure plus a negative regression against the combined gate; new `completionGateCancel()` helper; two new tests: future-confirmed Cancel still succeeds through the original backend rules with its audit row, and the per-status action affordances stay rendered). Tests ADDED: `tests/Feature/PatientProfileActionColorTest.php` (2 tests / 11 assertions: exact gray class on Ultrasound Edit, Medical History Edit and Referral Manage anchors - the Manage match is anchored on its label because `referrals.index` also appears in the nav and other panels - plus prenatal Edit/Print gray verification and no-blue/no-emerald negatives).
- Root cause (Cancel): one Alpine `x-show` governed a flex container holding BOTH the Cancel and Mark Completed buttons; the previous QA fix appended the completion-date predicate to that shared condition, so when `isCompletionDue()` returned false the entire container (Cancel included) was hidden. Correction: the predicate moved to the Mark Completed button's own `x-show`; the container reverted to its pre-QA condition, which is Cancel's original independent visibility rule. Backend `cancel()` was inspected and is untouched - it only asserts the confirmed status, so a future confirmed appointment remains cancellable exactly as before.
- Tests (SQLite `:memory:` only; isolation re-confirmed: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active, no optimize/cache commands run): focused gate of 11 files = 188 passed / 1 failed / 1283 assertions. The single failure is the documented pre-existing `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` walk-in fixture (`appointment_date = 2026-10-15` is in the past relative to the wall clock; the file's only edits remain the two completion-fixture dates from the previous sprint). The other documented pre-existing failure (`PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest:246`) was not in this run's file set. `php -l` clean on both touched test files.
- Defense notes: the negative markup assertion (`not->toContain("status === 'confirmed' && isCompletionDue(")`) makes it impossible to silently reintroduce the shared-gate bug; the cancel-success test pins the backend contract that cancellation never consults the schedule date, so a future contributor cannot "helpfully" extend the completion rule into cancel(); the Manage-link regex anchors on the visible label because the same route is legitimately rendered elsewhere on the page with different styling; only the three QA-named anchors were recolored - every other blue text link in the view is out of scope by explicit instruction.
- Known limitations: (1) x-show assertions are markup-level (no browser automation may be installed) - the per-button gates are proven present/absent in the rendered source, matching the repository's HTTP-testing convention; (2) the two pre-existing failures from the previous sprint remain open; (3) no migration, `.env`, cache, dependency, `public/hot`, Git or Railway change was made, and no full-suite run was attempted.

## DEPLA QA Fix: Patient Profile Header Container + Required-Records UI Gate Removal (Option A) (2026-10-18)

- Requirement (Option A only, approved scope): (1) remove the UI-only restriction that hid "Add Prenatal Visit" on the Patient Profile until Medical History + Ultrasound + Birth Plan all existed - the disabled "Complete Required Records First" button and its hover checklist are deleted entirely; the action renders unconditionally for eligible ONGOING non-admin users, matching the already-ungated Prenatal Visits index and the never-gated server (`PrenatalVisitController@create/@store` never checked completeness); (2) fix the header container defect where the hover checklist was clipped by `.panel { overflow: hidden }` because the `overflow-visible` utility class (added after the last Vite build) was never compiled into the served CSS; (3) no replacement checklist/warning; no change to routes, icons, styling, or admin/staff/DELIVERED authorization rules. The clinical layer is strictly out of scope: `CompletenessValidator`, `RiskAssessmentService`, `DecisionIntegrationService`, `ClinicalRuleEngine`, `MachineLearningService`, `PatientAssessmentRecalculationService`, referral HIGH-eligibility, notification logic, HIGH/LOW thresholds and assessment history are byte-identical.
- Files MODIFIED: `resources/views/patients/show.blade.php` - the `@if($canAddPrenatalVisit) / @else / @endif` wrapper around the Primary Action removed (net -83 lines); the "Add Prenatal Visit" anchor (route, patient context, icon, `btn btn-primary w-full justify-center` classes, position above Secondary Actions) is byte-identical to its previous form; the disabled button + `group relative` hover checklist block (Medical History / Ultrasound Record / Birth Plan rows with Add links) deleted; header container restored from `<div class="panel mb-6 overflow-visible">` to the standard `<div class="panel mb-6">` (the `overflow-visible` class was dead code - verified absent from the compiled `public/build/assets/app-BjjwKPgm.css`); `app/Http/Controllers/PatientController.php` - the four gate variables (`$hasMedicalHistory`, `$hasUltrasound`, `$hasBirthPlan`, `$canAddPrenatalVisit`, lines ~307-313) deleted after verifying via grep they were referenced only by the removed block; the `patients.show` compact() call trimmed accordingly; no other method touched; `docs/MANUAL_TESTING_UAT.md` - PAT-004 expected-behavior line updated (was documenting the removed `canAddPrenatalVisit` gate). Tests ADDED: `tests/Feature/PatientProfileRequiredRecordsGateRemovalTest.php` (6 tests / 26 assertions, gateRemoval-prefixed helpers): Add Prenatal Visit visible for an eligible ONGOING patient with zero prerequisite records; "Complete Required Records First" and checklist copy absent; admin exclusion unchanged; DELIVERED patients get the historical read-only card and no Add action; core profile sections still render; a POST to `prenatal-visits.store` with no prerequisite records succeeds and classifies the visit `ASSESSMENT INCOMPLETE` with `decision_source = COMPLETENESS`.
- Root cause (container): two compounding defects - (a) the checklist dropdown (`absolute right-0 z-50 group-hover:block`) needed the panel to stop clipping, and the source carried `overflow-visible`, but the compiled CSS build (2026-10-05) predates the commit that added the class, so `.panel { overflow: hidden }` won in every environment serving the stale build; (b) `.btn { white-space: nowrap }` forced the long disabled label onto one line where it could overflow on narrow viewports, and the hover-only dropdown was unreachable on touch devices. Both vanish with the checklist itself - no Vite rebuild is needed and none was run.
- Clinical-behavior verification (unchanged by construction, re-proven by test): a visit saved before the three records exist still classifies `ASSESSMENT INCOMPLETE` (never LOW - `DecisionIntegrationService` has no missing-data LOW path); BP-URG still bypasses completeness; `PatientAssessmentRecalculationService` still upgrades ASSESSMENT INCOMPLETE visits when the three records arrive while finalized HIGH/LOW visits stay immutable; referral eligibility still requires the latest assessment to be HIGH.
- Tests (SQLite `:memory:` only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, `phpunit.xml` forces `APP_ENV`/`DB_CONNECTION=sqlite`/`DB_DATABASE=:memory:` with `force="true"`; no optimize/config-cache command run): focused gate of 12 files = 118 passed / 14 failed / 601 assertions. All 6 new tests pass. A baseline re-run was performed with this sprint's three production edits temporarily reverted: the 2 new-behavior tests fail against the old gate (negative control proving the tests detect the removal) while the other 4 guard tests pass in both states, and the same 5 non-documented failures (GestationalAgeAutofillTest x4, MedicalHistoryScopeTest profile-scope test) fail identically with the changes reverted - proving they are pre-existing, not regressions. After restore, file mtimes were refreshed because `Copy-Item` preserved the older timestamp and Blade served a stale compiled view once (an operational artifact of the baseline-verification procedure, not a code issue).
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this sprint, verified and deliberately left unfixed: (1) `PatientProfileRiskPanelTest` (8 failures) - documented pre-existing before this work; (2) `PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest` "shows only the Print action..." (`:246`) - documented pre-existing in the previous two sprints; (3) `GestationalAgeAutofillTest` (4 failures: locked create page LMP-backed GA, ultrasound create/edit GA, stored edit GA with missing LMP, blank ultrasound edit errors) - deterministic (`Carbon::setTestNow`) failures on `prenatal-visits.create/edit` and `ultrasound.create/edit` routes that this sprint never touches; reproduced identically with all three production edits reverted; (4) `MedicalHistoryScopeTest` "groups the medical history on the patient profile..." - asserts the profile contains "Conditions Also Assessed During Prenatal Visits", a string that exists only in `medical_histories/create.blade.php` and `medical_histories/edit.blade.php` and is absent from `show.blade.php` at HEAD too (verified via `git show HEAD:...`); reproduced identically with this sprint's changes reverted. No production logic was weakened to make any of these pass.
- Defense notes: the new tests pin both the removal (checklist copy absent, Add link always present for ONGOING non-admin) and the preserved invariants (admin exclusion, DELIVERED read-only, `ASSESSMENT INCOMPLETE`/`COMPLETENESS` on store with missing records), so a future contributor cannot silently re-add the UI gate nor accidentally weaken the completeness classification; the gate variables were deleted from the controller only after a repo-wide grep proved the removed view block was their sole consumer; the header reverted to the standard `panel mb-6` rather than keeping a dead utility class, so the container styling again matches what the compiled CSS actually provides.
- Known limitations: (1) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - all assertions are HTTP/markup level per repository convention; (2) the four pre-existing failure groups above remain open and are candidates for separate QA decisions; (3) Option B (pre-completeness profile-factor HIGH evaluation, e.g. AGE-Y) remains explicitly out of scope pending separate clinical approval; (4) no migration, `.env`, cache-clear, dependency, `public/hot`, Vite build, Git or Railway change was made, and no full-suite run was attempted (focused gates only, per instructions).

## DEPLA Appointment Scheduling Upgrade - Phase 1: Database Foundation and Core Services (2026-10-18)

- Requirement (approved Phase 1A architecture): create the additive database foundation and reusable domain services for the 30-minute slot system ONLY - no wiring into booking paths, no Blade/UI changes, no Admin routes, no changes to existing appointment records or lifecycle methods. Approved rules encoded: clinic hours 08:00-17:00 Asia/Manila, 30-minute slots for checkup/counseling, 18 slots/day (starts 08:00..16:30), one confirmed/completed appointment per slot, pending requests never reserve, cancelled/declined release, completed retains, delivery requests date-only (no holds, no occupancy); admin closures (single date, inclusive range <= 366 days, partial slot windows, overlapping rows with union semantics, hard delete with audit, explicit admin confirmation required when existing confirmed/completed appointments are affected, reasons admin-internal, existing appointments never auto-cancelled/rescheduled).
- Files CREATED: `database/migrations/2026_10_18_000001_create_clinic_unavailabilities_table.php` (start_date/end_date inclusive, all_day, nullable start_time/end_time, reason(255), created_by FK SET NULL, composite index (start_date, end_date); cross-field rules application-level by design); `database/migrations/2026_10_18_000002_create_appointment_slot_holds_table.php` (hold_date, slot_start, appointment_id CASCADE, UNIQUE (hold_date, slot_start) + UNIQUE appointment_id - the database-level concurrency barrier); `app/Models/ClinicUnavailability.php` (date casts, coveringDate/overlappingRange scopes, no patient-facing reason accessor); `app/Models/AppointmentSlotHold.php` (FULLY GUARDED - empty fillable; NO date cast on hold_date, deliberately: equality lookups must hit the unique index, and a Carbon date cast serializes to 'Y-m-d 00:00:00' on SQLite, breaking equality while whereDate cannot use the index on MySQL); `app/Services/ClinicAvailabilityService.php` (single source of truth: slot catalog, labels, alignment, Manila past detection, closure read/union/validation, occupancy, affected preview, closure CRUD + audit, hold reserve/move/release); `tests/Feature/AppointmentSlotAvailabilityTest.php`, `tests/Feature/ClinicClosureManagementTest.php`, `tests/Feature/AppointmentSlotHoldTest.php` (37 tests / 169 assertions). Files MODIFIED: `tests/Feature/Phase3B3PatientAppointmentsTest.php` - the exact-set guard "does not add unexpected appointment tables" now expects the approved third table `appointment_slot_holds` (the appointments schema itself remains unmutated); this is an intentional expectation update for the approved new table, not a logic weakening.
- Occupancy doctrine (legacy-compatible): a slot [start, end) is occupied when a hold row exists for (date, slot_start) OR a confirmed/completed non-delivery appointment's confirmed_time falls inside the window - so a legacy 14:37 appointment occupies 14:30-15:00 without being rewritten or backfilled. Pending never occupies; multiple pending requests for the same slot are allowed. Documented limitation (in migration + service docblocks): legacy rows without holds have no database-level barrier - protection is the caller's transaction + this read-side query; the unique index is the PRIMARY guarantee for NEW grid reservations (lockForUpdate alone is never claimed as sufficient; SQLite makes this testable since lockForUpdate is a no-op there).
- Service integration posture: ClinicAvailabilityService deliberately does NOT touch AppointmentManagementService - it reuses the existing CLINIC_* constants as the shared source, its hold helpers open no implicit transaction (callers embed them in the existing appointment-then-request lock discipline with the hold write LAST), and booking-path enforcement is Phase 2. Unique-constraint conflicts are detected via Laravel's UniqueConstraintViolationException + SQLSTATE 23000/23505 with a precise conflicting-row re-check before translating to DomainException (never fragile error-message matching). Closure create/update/remove write CLINIC_UNAVAILABILITY_* audit rows (module CLINIC_UNAVAILABILITY, patient_id NULL) inside the same transaction; update merges `$data + $defaults` so omitted keys keep current values.
- Patient-safe output: slotsForDate() returns only {date, clinic_today, fully_unavailable, slots[{start,end,label,state}]} with state precedence past > unavailable > occupied > available - never patient names, appointment IDs or closure reasons (tested by payload-key and JSON-content assertions).
- Tests (SQLite `:memory:` only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active, tests/TestCase.php guard active; migrations executed ONLY by RefreshDatabase against SQLite :memory:, never against local MySQL or Railway): focused gate = 37 new tests green in one run, then 78 green with Phase3B3, then regression subset of 8 appointment suites = 198 passed / 2 failed (both failures analyzed: Phase3B3 guard - fixed as described; Phase3B4 walk-in fixture :213 - the documented pre-existing hardcoded 2026-10-15 past-date failure, not caused by this phase). Final combined re-run after the guard fix: 78 passed (432 assertions).
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this phase: `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` "does not notify the clinic for walk-in creation" (`:213`) - hardcoded `appointment_date = 2026-10-15` is in the past relative to the wall clock (2026-10-18); the file's prior modifications (completion-fixture dates from the earlier sprint) are untouched by this phase.
- Defense notes: the unique index is proven by direct constraint tests (second hold for same slot refused with QueryException) and by service-level DomainException translation; hold rollback is proven inside a failing surrounding transaction; the closure confirmation gate is proven to refuse-and-persist-nothing and to leave appointment rows byte-identical (full-row snapshot comparison) after a confirmed save; 366-day boundary tested at exactly 366 (allowed) and 367 (refused); union semantics tested for overlapping AND adjacent windows (adjacent half-days fully close the day).
- Known limitations: (1) Phase 1 wires nothing into production booking paths - the server-side double-booking gap closes only when Phase 2 integrates; (2) no Admin UI/routes/FormRequests yet (closure reasons and affected-appointment confirmation modal are Phase 3); (3) hold helpers must be called inside the caller's transaction - the service does not enforce that for future callers beyond documentation and tests; (4) the migration FILES were created but NOT executed against local MySQL or Railway (only SQLite :memory: via tests); (5) no browser click-through, no full-suite run, no migration execution on real databases, and no `.env`/cache/dependency/`public/hot`/Git/Railway change was made.

## DEPLA Appointment Scheduling Upgrade - Phase 2: Backend Enforcement and Slot Availability Endpoints (2026-10-18)

- Requirement (approved Phase 2): wire the Phase 1 ClinicAvailabilityService into every appointment WRITE path (confirm, walk-in, online request, reschedule submission, reschedule approval) plus cancel/decline/complete hold lifecycle, add FormRequest-level slot-grid alignment as early field feedback, expose the two read-only slot catalog JSON endpoints (staff + patient), and prove the behavior with focused tests. Approved rules enforced: 30-minute grid slot starts only (08:00..16:30; 17:00 and non-grid times like 09:17 refused); full-day closures block ALL types including delivery-request dates; partial time-window closures block only timed types; one confirmed/completed appointment per slot (hold row OR legacy-window appointment); pending requests never reserve and may share a requested slot; cancel/decline RELEASE the hold; completion RETAINS it; reschedule approval MOVES it; delivery requests never hold slots. Legacy records are never rewritten: they stay viewable/cancellable/completable, occupy their slot window on the read side, staff must pick a grid time when confirming a legacy pending request, and a legacy non-grid reschedule proposal is refused at approval.
- Files MODIFIED: `app/Services/ClinicAvailabilityService.php` - `slotOccupyingAppointments()`/`isSlotOccupied()` gained an optional `?int $ignoreAppointmentId = null` (a reschedule never blocks on its own current slot; the ignored hold row and the ignored appointment's legacy window are both excluded); new memoized `assertSchedulingSchemaReady()` deployment-boundary guard (two Schema::hasTable checks once per PHP process; a database missing the Phase 1 tables now fails early and cleanly with a rollout instruction - "run php artisan migrate" - instead of a raw mid-transaction table-not-found error; wired into slotsForDate, reserve/move/releaseHold and the write-path helpers); new static `slotStartIssue()` (FormRequest-level alignment message, format-invalid input returns null so format rules own that error). `app/Services/AppointmentManagementService.php` - constructor DI of ClinicAvailabilityService (container resolves it for controllers and app() callers alike; no direct `new` anywhere); class docblock's "no capacity limit" clause superseded by the Phase 2 rules; `validateSchedule()` now calls `availability->assertSlotStart()` for timed types (replacing the provisional 08:00/17:00 hours window - grid alignment AND window in one rule) and routes ALL types through new private `assertClinicAvailable()` (full-day closure message for every type incl. delivery; separate partial-window message for timed); new private `assertSlotNotOccupied()` (DomainException "That appointment time slot is no longer available...") and `usesTimedSlots()`; `confirm()` - occupancy re-check after validateSchedule, `reserveHold()` as the LAST data write before audit (timed only; the UNIQUE (hold_date, slot_start) index is the authoritative concurrency barrier - a race loser rolls back with the appointment unchanged); `createWalkIn()` - same occupancy + reserve pattern; `createOnlineRequest()` - rejects a slot already held by a confirmed/completed appointment (shared pendings still allowed); `decline()`, `cancel()`, `cancelByPatient()`, `approveCancellationRequest()` - `releaseHold()` (idempotent); `complete()` - deliberate NO release (completed retains occupancy, commented); `requestAppointmentReschedule()` - occupancy excluding self; `approveRescheduleRequest()` - authoritative occupancy re-check excluding self, then `hasHold() ? moveHold() : reserveHold()` after the appointment save (legacy confirmed rows without a hold get a fresh reserve; a lost race rolls the whole approval back). Four FormRequests (`PatientAppointmentStoreRequest`, `PatientAppointmentRescheduleRequest`, `AppointmentConfirmRequest`, `AppointmentWalkInStoreRequest`) - `withValidator` after() slot-alignment checks via `ClinicAvailabilityService::slotStartIssue()` for the timed field (early field-level errors; delivery proposals and unresolvable targets skipped; the existing clinicHoursRules/format/past rules untouched); `AppointmentManagementController` + `PatientAppointmentController` - ClinicAvailabilityService constructor DI + new `slots()` actions delegating to `slotsForDate()` (invalid/missing date -> 422 JSON {message}; patient action runs portalUserOrAbort first). `routes/web.php` - `GET /patient/appointments/slots` (name patient.appointments.slots, same portal middleware chain incl. no-store) and `GET /appointments/slots` (name appointments.slots, inside the existing ['auth','clinic','no-store'] clinic group).
- Tests CREATED: `tests/Feature/AppointmentSlotEnforcementTest.php` (22 tests - non-grid/17:00/16:30 boundaries on confirm + FormRequest 422s for walk-in and patient store; legacy non-grid pending confirmation refusal; second-confirmation and walk-in occupancy rejection with rollback proof (appointment stays pending, no orphan hold); legacy 14:37 window occupancy; multi-pending shared slot then first-confirm-wins; closures across all five write paths incl. full-day blocking delivery dates, partial windows leaving outside slots bookable, delivery-confirmation under partial window succeeding without a hold, and closure-added-after-submission still refusing confirmation; hold lifecycle: cancel releases, decline idempotent on pending, complete retains (frozen clock past due), cancellation-request approval releases, reschedule moves (old slot freed), legacy-no-hold reschedule creates a fresh hold, non-grid proposal refusal with unchanged schedule, own-slot and occupied-slot proposal behavior; delivery date-only compatibility; staff HTTP confirm DomainException surface (occupied slot + closure) with session error and unchanged status); `tests/Feature/AppointmentSlotEndpointsTest.php` (13 tests - guest redirect both endpoints; linked patient 200 + JSON schema; clinic role 403 on patient endpoint; staff+admin 200 on staff endpoint; patient 403 on staff endpoint; exactly 18 slots 08:00..16:30 with exclusive ends and state enum; occupied state from a confirmed hold; partial-window unavailable states; full-unavailable flag with reason-string absence; past date all-past; payload key-exactness (top-level and per-slot) plus name/id-string absence; 422 for malformed and missing date).
- Regression fallout FIXED (approved-rule expectation updates, not enforcement weakening): `Phase3B2AAppointmentsCalendarTest` route guard now expects 8 appointments.* routes with the GET-only set adding appointments.slots; `Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest` - accepted boundary times 16:59/16:56/09:15/14:35 moved to grid values (16:30/09:30), non-grid times added to the rejected sets, the "multiple appointments share the same date and time" walk-in test rewritten as "one confirmed appointment per slot" (second walk-in into the same slot refused, adjacent slot still bookable), the equal-minute walk-in boundary refrozen exactly ON a grid minute (14:30:00 booking 14:30), and the re-pending loop in the checkup clinic-hours test now deletes the fixture's hold row (no production transition re-pends a confirmed appointment; the fixture simulates one); `Phase3B3PatientAppointmentsTest` request-layer clinic-hours test: 16:59 moved from accepted to rejected (slot message), 16:30 accepted as the last grid slot; `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` archived-account confirm moved to the 11:00 slot (the same test's first confirm already occupies 10:00); `Phase3D3BPatientRescheduleTest` boundary proposal 16:59 -> 16:30, and "allows a proposal matching an appointment of a different type" rewritten as "refuses a proposal matching a confirmed appointment slot even when the type differs" (Phase 2 occupancy is type-agnostic).
- Tests (SQLite `:memory:` only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active, tests/TestCase.php guard active; migrations executed ONLY by RefreshDatabase against SQLite :memory:): new suites = 41 tests / 215 assertions green; final combined sweep of 16 suites (2 new + 3 Phase 1 + 11 regression) = 352 passed / 1 failed / 2129 assertions.
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this phase: `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` "does not notify the clinic for walk-in creation" (`:213`) - the untouched `phase3b4WalkIn()` fixture hardcodes `appointment_date = 2026-10-15`, in the past relative to the wall clock (2026-10-18); unchanged from the documented pre-existing state in Phases 1 and the earlier sprints. The other documented pre-existing groups (PatientProfileRiskPanelTest x8, PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest:246, GestationalAgeAutofillTest x4, MedicalHistoryScopeTest profile-scope) were not in this run's file set and remain out of scope.
- Defense notes: occupancy and closure are re-checked INSIDE every authoritative write's transaction (a slot taken or a closure added after a pending request/proposal was submitted still refuses confirmation/approval); the hold INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE is always the LAST data write inside the existing appointment-row -> change-request-row lock discipline (consistent global lock order, deadlock-safe; SQLite's no-op lockForUpdate is exactly why the unique index - not the row lock - is documented as the primary barrier); the reschedule self-exclusion covers BOTH the hold row (appointment_id !=) and the legacy-window query (ignored appointment filtered in PHP) so a move never blocks on its own current slot; the legacy-no-hold fallback uses reserveHold (never moveHold, which would refuse); reservation failure at approval throws BEFORE the change-request save and rolls back the appointment mutation in the same transaction (original schedule preserved, proven by test); FormRequest alignment is defence in depth only - the service repeats every rule for direct callers (proven by service-level tests that bypass HTTP); the patient slots payload is proven key-exact and name-free so a future refactor cannot leak patient data through the catalog; the schema-ready guard makes an un-migrated deployment fail with a rollout instruction instead of a 500 (memoized per process; never fires once the migrations have run).
- Known limitations: (1) the Phase 1 migrations have still NOT been executed against local MySQL or Railway - production rollout requires `php artisan migrate` BEFORE deploying this phase (the schema-ready guard turns a mis-ordered rollout into a clean DomainException, but scheduling is unusable until the tables exist); (2) no Blade/UI changes were made - the staff calendar and patient booking form still submit free-form time inputs; the FormRequest alignment message is the current UX for non-grid times, and consuming the slots JSON in the pickers is a later UI phase; (3) the closure management endpoints/UI remain Phase 3 (ClinicAvailabilityService::createClosure is exercised only by tests here); (4) documented pre-existing limitations from Phase 1 (legacy rows have no unique barrier; hold helpers rely on the caller's transaction) are unchanged; (5) no browser click-through, no full-suite run, no migration execution on real databases, and no `.env`/cache/dependency/`public/hot`/Git/Railway change was made (the pre-existing `public/hot` Vite marker was left untouched).


## DEPLA Appointment Scheduling Upgrade - Phase 3: Admin Clinic Availability Management (2026-10-18)

- Requirement (approved Phase 3): an Admin-only surface to create (single day / inclusive date range / selected 30-minute time slots), view, edit and remove clinic closure records, with an affected-appointments preview + explicit confirmation modal, informational overlap warnings, audit logging, and the Part H concurrency analysis. Approved rules preserved: closures never cancel or reschedule existing appointments; overlapping closure rows are allowed (union semantics); reasons are admin-internal; the client never supplies created_by and never supplies trusted affected counts.
- Architecture: three-layer authorization identical to Patient Account Management - the inherited 'auth' + 'clinic' + no-store route group, AdminMiddleware on a nested group in routes/web.php, per-FormRequest authorize() (User && !trashed && role admin), plus a controller assertAdmin(). Routes (inside the existing AdminMiddleware group): GET/POST /admin/clinic-unavailability, GET .../affected (read-only JSON preview), PATCH/DELETE .../{closure} (route-model bound), named admin.clinic-unavailability.{index,store,update,destroy,affected}.
