- Files CREATED: `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/ClinicAvailabilityController.php` (index/store/update/destroy/affected; normalizes the three form modes into canonical row(s), wraps the multi-window batch in one DB::transaction - nested per-row transactions become savepoints so an invalid window rolls back the WHOLE batch - catches ValidationException/DomainException into field-scoped redirect errors); `app/Http/Requests/AdminClinicAvailabilityStoreRequest.php` (mode single_day|date_range|slots; slot_starts[] with per-index grid-alignment via ClinicAvailabilityService::slotStartIssue; 366-day range check; date required for single_day AND slots), `AdminClinicAvailabilityUpdateRequest.php`, `AdminClinicAvailabilityAffectedRequest.php` (preview shape + optional ignore id), `AdminClinicAvailabilityDestroyRequest.php` (authorize-only); `resources/views/admin/clinic-unavailability/index.blade.php` (DEPLA admin theme conventions; three mode tabs; 18-slot chip picker with live contiguous-window summary; closures table with pagination + Edit/Remove modals; shared affected-preview modal fed by GET .../affected per window with counts summed across disjoint windows; confirm_affected hidden inputs; overlap warning shown informationally, never blocking); `tests/Feature/AdminClinicAvailabilityManagementTest.php` (31 tests / 126 assertions, cam-prefixed helpers).
- Files MODIFIED: `app/Services/ClinicAvailabilityService.php` - `affectedAppointments()` gained `bool $forUpdate = false` (lockForUpdate on the in-range appointment rows, threaded from assertAffectedConfirmedAccepted inside the create/update transactions - Part H serialization of admin closure vs staff confirmation; preview stays unlocked) and a new `completed_count` return key (confirmed_count stays the combined occupying count so every existing consumer and the confirm-affected gate keep their meaning); new `windowsFromSlotStarts()` (single source of truth for slot-mode grouping: contiguous runs become ONE window, gaps become separate rows, duplicates ignored, off-grid throws DomainException); new `timeLabel()`/`timeWindowLabel()` (12-hour display labels that accept ANY grid-aligned time including the 17:00 exclusive end - slotLabel() deliberately rejects it); `app/Models/ClinicUnavailability.php` - `scopeOverlappingRange` switched from raw string comparison to whereDate() (pre-existing SQLite portability bug: the date cast serializes as 'Y-m-d 00:00:00' so 'Y-m-d 00:00:00' <= 'Y-m-d' is lexicographically false and overlap warnings silently reported zero on SQLite; MySQL DATE columns store bare 'Y-m-d' so whereDate() is a no-op there - matches the existing scopeCoveringDate convention); `routes/web.php` (five routes + ClinicAvailabilityController import); `resources/views/layouts/app.blade.php` (Clinic Availability nav item in the role-gated Administration block after Patient Accounts, request()->routeIs active matching).
- Form-mode doctrine: single_day -> one all_day row; date_range -> one all_day row (max 366 inclusive days, end >= start); slots -> windowsFromSlotStarts() groups the selected starts (10:00,10:30,12:00 => rows 10:00-11:00 and 12:00-12:30), each validated by validateClosureInput() at the write boundary. The affected-preview modal runs BEFORE submit (per-window GETs, counts summed - safe because disjoint windows cannot both claim one appointment); when occupying_count > 0 the modal requires explicit Confirm & Save which sets confirm_affected=1; when only overlapping_count > 0 an informational Save Closure modal appears; the backend independently re-runs affectedAppointments(forUpdate: true) inside the write transaction, so a stale or forged client flag can never bypass the gate (proven by test).
- Part H concurrency analysis (no new migration required): the confirm path locks the specific appointment row first (lockAppointment), the closure path locks ALL in-range appointment rows (new forUpdate flag) inside its transaction - the two serialize, so a confirmation either commits before the closure re-check (counted as an affected existing appointment, never silently cancelled) or the closure commits first and confirmation's assertClinicAvailable refuses the now-closed slot. On SQLite lockForUpdate is a no-op but single-writer transactions serialize anyway; the walk-in INSERT-phantom race (a walk-in confirmed after the range-lock read) is documented: the walk-in is treated as an affected existing appointment and is NEVER auto-cancelled - approved behavior. Preview endpoint stays unlocked (informational only).
- Tests (SQLite :memory: only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: bootstrap/cache/config.php absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active, tests/TestCase.php guard active; migrations executed ONLY by RefreshDatabase against SQLite :memory:): new suite 31/31 green (authorization matrix incl. staff 403 on all five routes and guest redirect; single-day create with created_by spoof ignored; multi-day range; 366/367 boundary; reversed range; contiguous slot grouping; non-contiguous two-row split; off-grid refusal with zero rows; slots-mode missing slot/date; batch rollback on unconfirmed-gate mid-batch; update + full-day-with-times refusal; remove + overlapping rows still blocking; preview JSON confirmed/completed/pending split; overlap count with edit-mode ignore; time-window scoping; unconfirmed-gate refusal with zero rows and untouched appointments; confirm-flag acceptance with byte-identical appointment rows; stale-preview bypass recompute; edit newly-covering gate; multi-slot atomic commit; audit trio via HTTP; patient slot endpoint reason-leak absence; confirm into a different free slot while closures exist; confirm INTO closed slot refused; windowsFromSlotStarts unit). Final combined sweep of 17 suites (16 Phase 1/2 + 1 new) = 383 passed / 1 failed / 2257 assertions.
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this phase: `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` "does not notify the clinic for walk-in creation" (`:213`) - the untouched phase3b4WalkIn() fixture hardcodes appointment_date = 2026-10-15, in the past relative to the wall clock (2026-10-18); unchanged from the documented pre-existing state in Phases 1-2. Other documented pre-existing groups (PatientProfileRiskPanelTest x8, PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest:246, GestationalAgeAutofillTest x4, MedicalHistoryScopeTest profile-scope) were not in this run's file set.
- Defense notes: the client-supplied confirm_affected flag and preview counts are never trusted - the service re-computes affected appointments under FOR UPDATE inside the write transaction; the multi-window batch is one wrapping transaction so a partial multi-slot closure can never commit; created_by is stamped from the acting session user only (spoofed POST field proven ignored); the scopeOverlappingRange whereDate fix is covered by the overlap tests and matches the scopeCoveringDate convention; the reason field is proven absent from the patient slots payload; audit rows (CLINIC_UNAVAILABILITY_CREATED/UPDATED/REMOVED, module CLINIC_UNAVAILABILITY) are asserted through the HTTP surface; existing appointments are proven byte-identical (status and schedule) after every confirmed closure save.
- Known limitations: (1) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - modal/JS flows are asserted at HTTP/markup level per repository convention; (2) the Phase 1 migrations have still NOT been executed against local MySQL or Railway - production rollout requires php artisan migrate BEFORE deploying this phase; (3) the walk-in INSERT-phantom race is documented rather than eliminated (a walk-in landing between the closure's lock read and commit becomes an affected existing appointment - never auto-cancelled, approved behavior); (4) the closures UI does not yet consume the slots JSON in any staff/patient picker - the staff calendar and patient booking form still submit free-form time inputs (a later UI phase); (5) no migration, .env, cache-clear, dependency, public/hot, Vite build, Git or Railway change was made, and no full-suite run was attempted (focused gates only, per instructions).


## DEPLA Appointment Scheduling Upgrade - Phase 4: Shared 30-Minute Slot Picker UI (2026-10-18)

- Requirement (approved Phase 4): replace the arbitrary/free-form time inputs in ALL FOUR timed booking workflows (patient online booking, patient reschedule request, staff confirm, staff walk-in) with ONE shared, accessible, responsive 30-minute slot-picker component that renders the server catalog from the Phase 2 availability endpoints as its ONLY source of truth; delivery requests stay date-only; backend validation remains authoritative; no second client-side slot-generation implementation.
- Architecture: a single Blade component `resources/views/components/slot-picker.blade.php` (props: time-name, date-id, url, initial, picker-id, label, required, hide-expr, preselect-notice) containing self-contained CSS (the compiled Vite Tailwind build predates this component so no new utility class is relied upon), a @once classic inline `slotPicker(config)` Alpine factory (executes during HTML parse BEFORE the deferred Vite module runs Alpine.start() - the same convention appointments/calendar.blade.php already uses), and the fieldset/legend/radiogroup markup. One hidden HH:MM carrier input (`:name="timeName"`, `data-slot-carrier`) is the ONLY field the backend reads; it is disabled while availability is unresolved (`!ready`) OR when a parent hide-expr is truthy (delivery requests), so an unverified or date-only submission can never carry a timed value. Radio chips are rendered from the fetched slot array via x-for with x-model="selected"; non-available states are disabled by the server-computed state, labelled Booked/Past/Clinic Unavailable with full aria-labels, and never color-only (visible focus ring + text badge). A monotonic requestSeq token drops stale out-of-order fetch responses; a previous/preselected value is restored ONLY when the response reports that exact start as available (legacy non-grid times are never rounded); fetch failure shows the mandated copy + Retry; fully-unavailable and zero-available messages are mandated strings; `slot-picker-state` events bubble to each form so submit buttons stay disabled until the picker is ready AND a slot is chosen.
- Files CREATED: `resources/views/components/slot-picker.blade.php` (the shared component + factory described above); `tests/Feature/AppointmentSlotPickerUiTest.php` (22 tests / 104 assertions, spu-prefixed helpers - component markup on all four forms, endpoint wiring, field names via the Js::from-escaped config needles, delivery date-only rules, old free-form input removal, readiness gating bindings, mandated copy + stale-guard + exact-match-preselect source assertions, factory-once-per-page, route/role boundaries, 18-slot endpoint sanity).
- Files MODIFIED: `resources/views/patient/appointments/create.blade.php` - form x-data gains slotReady + slot-picker-state listener + submit guard; the requested_time free-form time input is replaced by `<x-slot-picker ... picker-id="book" hide-expr="!requiresTime">` (hidden entirely for delivery requests); submit button disabled while (requiresTime && !slotReady); type-hint copy updated. `resources/views/patient/appointments/partials/card.blade.php` - the reschedule form carries its own `x-data="{ slotReady, submitting }"` (the index page has no parent Alpine scope; multiple cards produce multiple independent picker instances keyed reschedule-{id}); proposed_time free-form input replaced by the picker for non-delivery appointments only (delivery reschedules stay date-only with NO picker rendered); submit button disabled while !slotReady. `resources/views/appointments/calendar.blade.php` - appointmentDetailsPanel() gains confirmSlotReady/walkInSlotReady state (walkIn.time state removed); the Confirm button click now dispatches `depla-slot-sync` (timed types only, $nextTick) which the picker listens to via x-on:depla-slot-sync.window and turns into sync(date, time) -> refetch -> exact-match-only preselection of the patient-requested time with the mandated preselect-notice when it is occupied/past/closed/non-grid; the #confirm-time free-form input (non-delivery branch) is replaced by `<x-slot-picker ... picker-id="confirm" preselect-notice hide-expr="selected?.type === 'delivery_request'">` while the optional #confirm-time-delivery date-only input is unchanged; the #walkin-time free-form input is replaced by `<x-slot-picker ... picker-id="walk-in" hide-expr="walkIn.type === 'delivery_request'">` plus a date-only delivery note; both forms listen for slot-picker-state and gate their submits; guardConfirmSchedule()/guardWalkInSchedule() now read the picker carrier via `document.querySelector('[data-picker-id=...] [data-slot-carrier]')` instead of the removed time inputs. Regression expectation updates (approved-rule/UI-contract changes, not weakening): `Phase3B3PatientAppointmentsTest` (old-input preservation now asserts the picker's initial config needle; the request-form test asserts the picker carrier + copy instead of name="requested_time"/type="time"); `Phase3B2BAppointmentManagementTest` (management-UI copy assertion; walk-in button disabled binding includes walkInSlotReady; the confirmed_time dual-input test rewritten as "one literal name=confirmed_time (delivery) + one Alpine-bound picker carrier, each disabled for the other's mode" preserving the duplicate-key invariant); `Phase3D3B4PatientRescheduleUiTest` (clinic-hours test asserts the picker instance + carrier instead of min/max time attributes; delivery-omission test asserts no proposed_time string and no picker instance; old-input test asserts the initial config needle); `AppointmentCompletionDateRestrictionTest` (Confirm click assertion includes the depla-slot-sync dispatch clause).
- Delivery date-only doctrine (unchanged from Phases 2-3, now enforced in the UI layer as well): the patient booking picker is wrapped in x-show="requiresTime" AND its carrier is disabled by hide-expr="!requiresTime"; the reschedule picker is simply not rendered for delivery appointments; the staff confirm picker's carrier is disabled by hide-expr="selected?.type === 'delivery_request'" (the optional plain #confirm-time-delivery input is the only time control for delivery confirms); the walk-in picker's carrier is disabled by hide-expr="walkIn.type === 'delivery_request'" and a date-only explanatory note renders instead. In every case the FORMREQUEST re-validates, so a bypassed UI still cannot submit a timed delivery value.
- Preselection doctrine: the staff confirm picker never preselects from a static value - the Confirm button dispatches the requested date+time, the picker refetches the live catalog, and restores the value ONLY if that exact start is returned as available; a legacy non-grid time, an occupied slot, a past slot or a closed window produces the mandated notice ("The patient-requested time is not available...") and staff must choose another slot. old() values on all four forms flow through the same validated-initial path (config.initial), never a raw value attribute.
- Tests (SQLite :memory: only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: bootstrap/cache/config.php absent, phpunit.xml force=true guards active, tests/TestCase.php guard active; migrations executed ONLY by RefreshDatabase against SQLite :memory:): new suite 22/22 green; updated regression suites green (Phase3B3 34/34, Phase3B2B subset, Phase3D3B4 14/14, AppointmentCompletionDateRestrictionTest 14/14); final combined sweep of 19 appointment-related suites = 432 passed / 1 failed / 2727 assertions (the one failure is the documented pre-existing Phase3B4 walk-in fixture hardcoding 2026-10-15 against wall clock 2026-10-18 - unchanged from Phases 1-3).
- Pre-existing failures - NOT caused by this phase: `Phase3B4AppointmentNotificationsTest` "does not notify the clinic for walk-in creation" (`:213`) - the untouched phase3b4WalkIn() fixture hardcodes appointment_date = 2026-10-15, in the past relative to the wall clock (2026-10-18); unchanged from the documented pre-existing state in Phases 1-3. Other documented pre-existing groups (PatientProfileRiskPanelTest x8, PrenatalVisitHistoryAndPrintTest:246, GestationalAgeAutofillTest x4, MedicalHistoryScopeTest profile-scope) were not in this run's file set.
- Defense notes: the component contains NO hardcoded slot catalog (source-asserted: no start: '08:00'-style literals; the factory only renders Array.isArray(data.slots) from the endpoint); the hidden carrier is disabled whenever readiness is unresolved, so a stale or failed fetch can never silently submit an old time; the stale-response seq guard is asserted at source level and mirrors the calendar's existing Alpine conventions; every backend FormRequest + ClinicAvailabilityService rule is unchanged and remains the write-boundary authority (this phase is presentation + client-side gating only); the compiled-Tailwind-staleness risk is eliminated by self-contained CSS using only existing DEPLA palette hexes; the @once directive guarantees the factory script renders exactly once per page even with multiple picker instances (asserted); backend @error messages render inside the component without suppression or rewriting; accessibility: fieldset/legend, role=radiogroup, per-chip aria-labels combining slot label + state description, visible focus outline, 44px minimum touch targets, and text badges (never color alone).
- Known limitations: (1) no browser click-through was performed (no browser automation may be installed) - Alpine fetch/race/retry behavior is covered by source-level assertions and the UAT checklist appended below, per repository convention; (2) the Phase 1 migrations have still NOT been executed against local MySQL or Railway - production rollout requires php artisan migrate BEFORE deploying this phase; (3) the compiled Vite CSS build was NOT rebuilt (public/hot left untouched, npm build not run per instructions) - the picker's self-contained style block means it renders correctly without a rebuild, but any future Tailwind additions elsewhere still require one; (4) the patient booking form's picker is rendered inside x-show="requiresTime" so a delivery-request submit never includes a timed carrier - this is a second layer on top of the hide-expr carrier-disable, chosen for clarity; (5) no migration, .env, cache-clear, dependency, public/hot, Vite build, Git or Railway change was made, and no full-suite run was attempted (focused gates only, per instructions).

### Phase 4 Browser UAT Checklist (manual - JS runtime behavior not automatable in feature tests)

The following scenarios require a real browser and were NOT executed in this sprint (no browser automation may be installed). They are listed so clinic QA can verify the Alpine runtime paths the feature tests cannot reach:

1. Patient booking: open the form, pick a date, wait for the 18 chips to render; verify Booked/Past/Clinic Unavailable chips are disabled with visible text badges; select an available slot and confirm the hidden carrier receives its HH:MM value in the submitted payload; switch the type to Delivery Request and verify the picker disappears and no requested_time key is submitted.
2. Patient booking retry: block the slots endpoint in devtools (offline), change the date, verify the mandated network-error copy + Retry button appear; click Retry with the network restored and verify the grid recovers.
3. Stale-response guard: on a slow connection change the date twice quickly and verify only the latest date's catalog renders (the older response is dropped).
4. Patient reschedule: same as (1) on a confirmed online card; verify multiple cards each have an independent picker instance.
5. Staff confirm: open a pending checkup appointment whose requested time is still available - the picker should fetch on Confirm click and preselect that exact slot; open one whose requested time is now occupied - verify the mandated preselect-notice appears and no chip is preselected; verify a legacy non-grid requested time (e.g. 10:37) is never rounded to 10:30.
6. Staff confirm delivery: open a pending delivery request - verify no picker grid renders and the optional plain time input remains.
7. Staff walk-in: verify the picker loads after choosing a date; switch the type to Delivery Request and verify the picker hides and the carrier is disabled; verify the walk-in submit button stays disabled until a slot is chosen for timed types.
8. Full-day closure: pick a closed date on any form and verify the mandated full-day message replaces the grid.
9. Zero-available day: pick a fully booked date and verify the "No available time slots remain" message.
10. Accessibility: keyboard-tab through the chips; verify visible focus ring, radio semantics, and screen-reader labels combining slot time + state.

## DEPLA Patient Archive/Restore Fix: Baby SoftDeletes, Transaction Safety and Patient List Ordering (2026-10-18)

- Root cause of the Restore Patient HTTP 500: `Patient`'s `restoring` model event (`app/Models/Patient.php:98-105`) calls `$patient->babies()->withTrashed()` at line 101, but the `Baby` model did not use `SoftDeletes`, so the `withTrashed` local macro was never registered (`SoftDeletingScope::extend` runs only for SoftDeletes models) and `Relation::__call` forwarded the call until `ForwardsCalls` threw `BadMethodCallException: Call to undefined method Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\Relations\HasMany::withTrashed()`. The exception fired inside `PatientController::restore()` (`:159-167`) with no transaction, after the prenatal-visits and ultrasounds cascade had already executed their own restores - so every failed attempt also left the related rows half-restored while the patient stayed archived. Proven empirically (isolated script, SQLite `:memory:`) and by the system's own logs (`storage/logs/laravel.log`, two `local.ERROR` entries on 2026-10-18 with stack frames `Patient.php(101)` -> `PatientController.php(163)` -> `SoftDeletes.php(170)`), which also disproved the "works locally" premise - restore failed identically on XAMPP.
- Baby SoftDeletes correction (the complete fix, not a line removal): `app/Models/Baby.php` now uses `Illuminate\Database\Eloquent\SoftDeletes`; ONE new additive, reversible migration `database/migrations/2026_10_18_000003_add_deleted_at_to_babies_table.php` adds a nullable `deleted_at` to the existing `babies` table (`up()`: `$table->softDeletes()`; `down()`: `$table->dropSoftDeletes()`). No equivalent migration existed (checked every migration - `2026_03_24_094248_add_deleted_at_to_all_tables` covers patients/prenatal_visits/ultrasounds/medical_histories/birth_plans only, and `babies` was created later in `2026_04_06_174007`). The table is never recreated or dropped; every existing row is preserved; the migration was NOT run against local MySQL or Railway (SQLite `:memory:` tests apply it through RefreshDatabase).
- Archive/restore lifecycle after the fix: `Patient`'s deleting hook line 85 (`$patient->babies()->delete()`) now soft-deletes baby rows through the SoftDeletingScope instead of issuing a hard `DELETE` (previously it permanently destroyed baby records on every patient archive - a data-loss defect), and restoring hook line 101 now resolves and restores babies with the patient. `forceDelete()` paths are unchanged (intentional permanent delete for permanent patient deletion, with the existing `babies.patient_id` ON DELETE CASCADE FK). No duplicate rows are possible (restore only updates `deleted_at`; `PregnancyOutcomeRecordingService::Baby::create` remains the sole creation path and is unaffected).
- Transaction safety: `PatientController::destroy()` and `PatientController::restore()` now wrap the model call (and, for archive, the audit entry) in `DB::transaction()`. Because the deleting/restoring model events fire INSIDE those calls, the transaction covers the complete lifecycle - parent row, every cascade statement, and the audit row. Any mid-cascade failure rolls the whole operation back: a failed archive leaves the patient active with all related records untouched and no audit row claiming a rolled-back archive; a failed restore returns the patient AND every already-un-deleted related row to the archived state (pinned by two rollback tests using listeners that throw after the built-in cascade).
- Patient list ordering: Active Patients (`PatientController::index`) already ordered `orderByDesc('created_at')` then `orderByDesc('id')` BEFORE `paginate(10)` - unchanged, now pinned by tests with timestamps deliberately disagreeing with insert order. Archived Patients (`PatientController::trashed`) gained `orderByDesc('deleted_at')` then `orderByDesc('id')` before the query executes. The archived page keeps its existing `->get()` collection (it renders an archived-count stat from `$patients->count()`; adding pagination would change that UI semantic and was out of "preserve existing UI design" scope), so there is no pagination to order around there. Search, filters, pagination page links (`withQueryString`), status filters, Staff-only access and the existing UI are all preserved.
- Related-record lifecycle findings (documented limitation, no redesign without approval): the schema has NO column distinguishing a record archived independently from one archived by the patient cascade (timestamps are second-precision and unreliable as provenance), so patient restore continues to restore EVERY related row in the trash - including independently archived prenatal visits/ultrasounds (existing behavior, now pinned by a test that documents it). For babies the point is moot: no code path archives a baby independently, so every trashed baby row is cascade-origin by construction. A timestamp-heuristic ("rows trashed before the patient were independent") was considered and deliberately NOT implemented - it would be an unapproved archive-lifecycle redesign with same-second ambiguity.
- Historical hard-delete recovery limitation: babies hard-deleted by the pre-fix archive path are permanently gone; nothing in this sprint recreates or fabricates them. Recovery requires separate backup analysis and explicit approval, per instructions.
- Tests (SQLite `:memory:` only; isolation re-confirmed before every run: `bootstrap/cache/config.php` absent, phpunit.xml `force="true"` guards active, `tests/TestCase.php` isolation guard active; migrations executed ONLY by RefreshDatabase against SQLite `:memory:`, never against local MySQL or Railway): NEW `tests/Feature/PatientArchiveRestoreLifecycleTest.php` = 16 tests / 76 assertions, covering all sixteen required scenarios (archive succeeds; restore succeeds with no HTTP 500; babies soft-deleted not hard-deleted with row-count preservation; babies restored with the patient; prenatal-visit lifecycle; ultrasound lifecycle; birth plan + medical history consistency; archive rollback on simulated failure; restore rollback on simulated failure incl. rows the cascade had already touched; no duplicate records across two archive/restore cycles; independently archived related records per documented lifecycle; unauthorized restore rejected - patient role 403, admin 403, guest redirect, unknown id 404, all with unchanged records; active list newest-first; archived list most-recently-archived-first; search + pagination page-1/page-2 ordering; unrelated patients and their records untouched). Focused regression run of `ClinicRouteAuthorizationTest` + `PrenatalVisitArchiveWorkflowTest` + `DeliveredPatientWorkflowTest` + `PatientExportConsistencyTest` = 30 passed / 728 assertions plus the one pre-existing failure below. Zero new failures.
- Pre-existing failure - NOT caused by this sprint: `PatientExportConsistencyTest:122` `assertSeeText('HIGH RISK')` - case-sensitive literal absent from every view in the working tree (only `referrals/select-patient.blade.php` contains it) AND from committed HEAD's `patients/show.blade.php`, while the page renders "High Risk"/"HIGH"; it belongs to the same risk-panel wording family as the documented `PatientProfileRiskPanelTest` x8 baseline, none of this sprint's changed files (Baby model, babies migration, destroy/restore/trashed ordering) participate in that rendering path, and it fails identically in isolation.
- Defense notes: the fix restores the intended cascade rather than deleting the failing line, so clinical archive semantics for babies become consistent with every other related table; transactions live at the two (and only two) patient archive/restore call sites in the codebase, covering model events fired within them, so no hook-level behavior was rewritten; authorization (StaffMiddleware exact role `staff`, POST-only routes, CSRF, `onlyTrashed()->findOrFail` 404s) was preserved untouched and is test-pinned; no clinical thresholds, appointment code, `.env`, config, cache, dependencies, `public/hot`, Git or Railway state were touched, and no migration was executed against any real database.
